
### tools/capacity-saturation-probe.js
1: #!/usr/bin/env node
2: /* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
3:    capacity-saturation-probe.js — کاوشگرِ نقطهٔ اشباع (بسته ۲ نقشه راه)
4:    ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
5:    مالک: چت ۴ (Capacity Engineering) · طرحِ هم‌زمانِ docs/CAPACITY_WORKLOAD_MODEL.md
6:    خروجی: پاکتِ ظرفیت در قالب docs/CAPACITY_ENVELOPE_TEMPLATE.md
7: 
8:    چه می‌کند (روی staging چندنودی — بسته ۱):
9:      ۱. نردبانِ بارِ پله‌ای (closed-loop): هر پله = تعداد کارگرِ هم‌زمان.
10:      ۲. هر پله: p50/p95/p99 و throughput و نرخ خطا به‌تفکیک اندپوینت.
11:      ۳. اسکراپِ `/metrics` قبل/بعدِ هر پله (counters → دلتا؛ gauges → لحظه‌ای):
12:         pool/queue/cache/slow-queries/auth — نام‌ها از server/metrics.js.
13:      ۴. کشفِ زانو (knee): سه نامزدِ مستند —
14:         (a) slo      : اولین پلهٔ p95 > SLO (پیش‌فرض 300ms از CAPACITY_MODEL §۵)
15:         (b) plateau  : اولین پله‌ای که بهرهٔ حاشیه‌ای throughput نسبت به
16:                        بهرهٔ حاشیه‌ای concurrency < 50٪ شود
17:         (c) errors   : اولین پلهٔ نرخ 5xx > 1٪ (۱۰× بودجهٔ خطا)
18:         زانو = قدیمی‌ترینِ نامزدهای آشکار؛ خروجی شامل همهٔ نامزدهاست (شفافیت).
19:      ۵. پاکتِ JSON (پرچمِ NOT-COLLECTED برای جمع‌آورِ غایب — هرگز جعل نمی‌شود).
20: 
21:    چه «نمی‌کند» (صادقانه):
22:      • اجرا در این سندباکس — طراحی/آماده‌سازی است؛ اجرا NOT-RUN تا staging.
23:      • بازپخشِ الگوی زمانیِ کامل — هر اجرا یک «حالتِ پایدار» را می‌سنجد
24:        (mixed-national | read-browse | write-burst — مدل بار §۳–۴).
25: 
26:    اجرا:
27:      node tools/capacity-saturation-probe.js --validate          ← آفلاین؛ بدون شبکه
28:      node tools/capacity-saturation-probe.js \
29:         --target https://staging.example.internal --confirm-staging \
30:         --profile mixed-national --staircase 50,100,200,400,800,1600 \
31:         --step-seconds 120 --out capacity-envelope.json
32:    زوایای ایمنی: بدون --confirm-staging اجرا نمی‌شود (محافظ production) ·
33:    توقفِ اضطراری اگر نرخ 5xx از --abort-error-rate (پیش‌فرض 5٪) گذرد.
34:    احراز: --token یا PAYESH_PROBE_TOKEN (bearer) · اسکراپ: PAYESH_METRICS_TOKEN.
35:    بارِ مفیدِ POST: پیش‌فرض = حداقلی؛ واقعی‌اش را با --payloads <file> بدهید
36:    (یک JSON از مسیر→بدنه؛ بدون آن، پاسخِ اعتبارسنجیِ 4xx هم سیگنالِ مسیر است
37:    ولی «نوشتنِ واقعی» را نمی‌سنجد — در پاکت ثبت می‌شود).
38:    ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
39: 'use strict';
40: const { performance } = require('perf_hooks');
41:

71: const SCRAPE = {
72:   counters: ['payesh_http_requests_total', 'payesh_sync_requests_total',
73:     'payesh_cache_hits_total', 'payesh_cache_misses_total', 'payesh_cache_lookups_total',

78:     'payesh_db_pool_waiting', 'payesh_db_up'],

81: /* ── CLI ── */
82: const argv = process.argv.slice(2);
83: const has = (f) => argv.includes(f);
84: const arg = (f, d) => { const i = argv.indexOf(f); return i >= 0 && argv[i + 1] ? argv[i + 1] : d; };
85: const opt = {
86:   target: arg('--target', process.env.PAYESH_BASE_URL || ''),
87:   profile: arg('--profile', 'mixed-national'),
88:   staircase: String(arg('--staircase', '50,100,200,400,800,1600')),
89:   stepSeconds: Number(arg('--step-seconds', 120)) || 120,
90:   warmupSeconds: Number(arg('--warmup-seconds', 15)) || 15,
91:   sloP95ms: Number(arg('--slo-p95-ms', 300)) || 300,
92:   abortErrorRate: Number(arg('--abort-error-rate', 5)) || 5,
93:   reqTimeoutMs: Number(arg('--req-timeout-ms', 10000)) || 10000,
94:   metricsPath: arg('--metrics-path', '/metrics'),
95:   metricsToken: arg('--metrics-token', process.env.PAYESH_METRICS_TOKEN || ''),
96:   token: arg('--token', process.env.PAYESH_PROBE_TOKEN || ''),
97:   out: arg('--out', 'capacity-envelope.json'),
98:   payloads: arg('--payloads', ''),
99:   datasetScale: arg('--dataset-scale', ''),
100:   nodes: arg('--nodes', ''),
101: };

103: function validate() {
104:   const errs = [];
105:   if (!PROFILES[opt.profile]) errs.push('پروفایل ناشناخته: ' + opt.profile + ' (مجاز: ' + Object.keys(PROFILES).join(' | ') + ')');
106:   const steps = opt.staircase.split(',').map((s) => Number(s.trim()));
107:   if (!steps.length || steps.some((n) => !Number.isFinite(n) || n < 1 || !Number.isInteger(n))) errs.push('نردبان نامعتبر: ' + opt.staircase);
108:   else if (steps.some((n, i) => i && n <= steps[i - 1])) errs.push('نردبان باید اکیداً صعودی باشد');
109:   const wsum = PROFILES[opt.profile] ? PROFILES[opt.profile].reduce((a, e) => a + e.w, 0) : 0;
110:   if (PROFILES[opt.profile] && Math.abs(wsum - 100) > 0.01) errs.push('وزن پروفایل ≠ ۱۰۰: ' + wsum);
111:   if (opt.sloP95ms < 1) errs.push('SLO نامعتبر');
112:   console.log('── طرحِ اجرا (آفلاین؛ هیچ شبکه‌ای در کار نیست) ──');
113:   console.log('پروفایل: ' + opt.profile + ' (Σوزن=' + wsum + ')');
114:   console.log('نردبان: ' + (steps.length ? steps.join(' → ') : '—') + ' · هر پله ' + opt.stepSeconds + 's (+گرم‌کردن ' + opt.warmupSeconds + 's)');
115:   console.log('SLO پذیرش: p95 < ' + opt.sloP95ms + 'ms · توقف اضطراری: 5xx > ' + opt.abortErrorRate + '٪');
116:   console.log('نامزدهای زانو: slo | plateau (بهرهٔ حاشیه‌ای < ۵۰٪) | errors (5xx > 1٪)');
117:   console.log('جمع‌آورها: HTTP+‪/metrics‬ (هسته) · redis-info (اختیاری، RESP) · pg-direct (اختیاری، ماژول pg)');
118:   if (errs.length) { errs.forEach((e) => console.error('  ✗ ' + e)); process.exit(1); }
119:   console.log('  ✓ طرح معتبر — برای اجرا: --target <url> --confirm-staging');
120:   process.exit(0);
121: }
122: 
123: /* ── انتخابِ وزنیِ اندپوینت ── */
124: function pick(profile) {
125:   let r = Math.random() * 100;
126:   for (const e of profile) { if ((r -= e.w) <= 0) return e; }
127:   return profile[profile.length - 1];
128: }
129: 
130: /* ── اسکراپِ Prometheus text 0.0.4 ── */
131: function parseProm(text) {
132:   const out = {};
133:   for (const line of text.split('\n')) {
134:     if (!line || line.startsWith('#')) continue;
135:     const m = line.match(/^([a-z_][a-z0-9_]*)(\{[^}]*\})?\s+([^\s]+)/i);
136:     if (!m) continue;
137:     const labels = {};
138:     if (m[2]) m[2].slice(1, -1).replace(/([a-z_]+)="([^"]*)"/g, (_, k, v) => { labels[k] = v; return ''; });
139:     out[m[1] + (m[2] ? JSON.stringify(labels) : '')] = { name: m[1], labels, value: Number(m[3]) };
140:   }
141:   return out;
142: }
143: 
144: async function scrapeMetrics() {
145:   const ctl = new AbortController();
146:   const t = setTimeout(() => ctl.abort(), opt.reqTimeoutMs);
147:   try {
148:     const res = await fetch(opt.target.replace(/\/$/, '') + opt.metricsPath, {
149:       headers: Object.assign({}, opt.metricsToken ? { authorization: 'Bearer ' + opt.metricsToken } : {}),
150:       signal: ctl.signal,
151:     });
152:     if (!res.ok) return { error: 'HTTP ' + res.status };
153:     return { series: parseProm(await res.text()) };
154:   } catch (e) { return { error: String(e.message || e) }; }
155:   finally { clearTimeout(t); }
156: }
157: 
158: /* جمع‌آورِ اختیاریِ Redis — RESP روی سوکتِ خام (صفر وابستگی) */
159: async function redisInfo() {
160:   const url = arg('--redis-url', process.env.PAYESH_REDIS_URL || '');
161:   if (!url) return { status: 'NOT-COLLECTED (بدون --redis-url)' };
162:   const m = url.match(/^redis:\/\/([^:]+):(\d+)/); if (!m) return { status: 'NOT-COLLECTED (URL نامعتبر)' };
163:   const net = require('net');
164:   return new Promise((resolve) => {
165:     const s = net.connect(Number(m[2]), m[1]);
166:     let buf = '';
167:     const done = (r) => { try { s.destroy(); } catch (_) {} resolve(r); };
168:     const t = setTimeout(() => done({ status: 'NOT-COLLECTED (timeout)' }), 3000);
169:     s.on('connect', () => s.write('*1\r\n$4\r\nINFO\r\n'));
170:     s.on('data', (d) => {
171:       buf += d.toString();
172:       if (!buf.includes('\r\n') || !buf.endsWith('\r\n')) return;
173:       clearTimeout(t);
174:       const kv = {};
175:       for (const line of buf.split('\r\n')) { const i = line.indexOf(':'); if (i > 0) kv[line.slice(0, i)] = line.slice(i + 1); }
176:       done({
177:         status: 'OK',
178:         used_memory_bytes: Number(kv.used_memory) || null,
179:         keyspace_hits: Number(kv.keyspace_hits) || 0,
180:         keyspace_misses: Number(kv.keyspace_misses) || 0,
181:         connected_clients: Number(kv.connected_clients) || null,
182:       });
183:     });
184:     s.on('error', (e) => { clearTimeout(t); done({ status: 'NOT-COLLECTED (' + e.message + ')' }); });
185:   });
186: }
187: 
188: /* جمع‌آورِ اختیاریِ مستقیمِ PG — فقط اگر --pg-dsn داده شود و ماژول pg موجود باشد.
189:    CPU پایگاه‌داده از SQL درنمی‌آید (لایهٔ OS) — صادقانه NOT-COLLECTED ثبت می‌شود. */
190: async function pgStats() {
191:   const dsn = arg('--pg-dsn', process.env.PAYESH_PG_DSN || '');
192:   if (!dsn) return { status: 'NOT-COLLECTED (بدون --pg-dsn)' };
193:   let Pool; try { ({ Pool } = require('pg')); } catch (_) { return { status: 'NOT-COLLECTED (ماژول pg نصب نیست)' }; }
194:   const pool = new Pool({ connectionString: dsn, connectionTimeoutMillis: 3000 });
195:   try {
196:     const db = (await pool.query("SELECT xact_commit, xact_rollback, blks_read, blks_hit, deadlocks, conflicts FROM pg_stat_database WHERE datname = current_database()")).rows[0];
197:     const act = (await pool.query("SELECT count(*) FILTER (WHERE wait_event_type = 'Lock') AS lock_waiting, count(*) AS connections FROM pg_stat_activity")).rows[0];
198:     const lk = (await pool.query("SELECT count(*) AS not_granted FROM pg_locks WHERE NOT granted")).rows[0];
199:     return {
200:       status: 'OK', db_cpu: 'NOT-COLLECTED (لایهٔ OS — از exporter نود)',
201:       xact_commit: +db.xact_commit, xact_rollback: +db.xact_rollback,
202:       blks_read: +db.blks_read, blks_hit: +db.blks_hit,
203:       block_hit_ratio_pct: +(100 * db.blks_hit / Math.max(1, db.blks_hit + db.blks_read)).toFixed(1),
204:       deadlocks: +db.deadlocks, conflicts: +db.conflicts,
205:       lock_waiting: +act.lock_waiting, connections: +act.connections, locks_not_granted: +lk.not_granted,
206:     };
207:   } catch (e) { return { status: 'NOT-COLLECTED (' + (e.message || e) + ')' }; }
208:   finally { await pool.end().catch(() => {}); }
209: }
210: 
211: /* استخراجِ دلتا/مقادیر از دو اسکراپِ قبل/بعدِ پله */
212: function extractMetrics(before, after) {
213:   if (before.error || after.error) return { status: 'SCRAPE-FAILED', before: before.error || 'ok', after: after.error || 'ok' };
214:   const sum = (series, name) => Object.values(series).filter((s) => s.name === name)
215:     .reduce((a, s) => a + (Number.isFinite(s.value) ? s.value : 0), 0);
216:   const exposed = (series, name) => Object.values(series).some((s) => s.name === name);
217:   const gauge = (series, name) => { const s = Object.values(series).find((x) => x.name === name); return s ? s.value : null; };
218:   const o = { status: 'OK' };
219:   for (const c of SCRAPE.counters) {
220:     o[c + '_delta'] = (exposed(before.series, c) && exposed(after.series, c))
221:       ? +(sum(after.series, c) - sum(before.series, c)).toFixed(0) : 'NOT-EXPOSED';
222:   }
223:   for (const g of SCRAPE.gauges) o[g] = exposed(after.series, g) ? gauge(after.series, g) : 'NOT-EXPOSED';
224:   const h = o.payesh_cache_hits_total_delta, m = o.payesh_cache_misses_total_delta;
225:   o.cache_hit_ratio_pct = (Number.isFinite(h) && Number.isFinite(m) && h + m > 0)
226:     ? +(100 * h / (h + m)).toFixed(1) : 'NOT-COMPUTABLE';
227:   return o;
228: }
229: 
230: /* ── یک درخواست با زمان‌سنجی ── */
231: async function fire(ep, payloads, headers) {
232:   const body = ep.method === 'POST' ? JSON.stringify(payloads[ep.path] || {}) : undefined;
233:   const ctl = new AbortController();
234:   const t = setTimeout(() => ctl.abort(), opt.reqTimeoutMs);
235:   const t0 = performance.now();
236:   try {
237:     const res = await fetch(opt.target.replace(/\/$/, '') + ep.path, {
238:       method: ep.method, body, signal: ctl.signal,
239:       headers: Object.assign({ 'content-type': 'application/json' }, headers),
240:     });
241:     await res.arrayBuffer().catch(() => {});
242:     return { ok: true, ms: performance.now() - t0, status: res.status, ep };
243:   } catch (e) { return { ok: false, ms: performance.now() - t0, status: 0, ep, err: String(e.message || e) }; }
244:   finally { clearTimeout(t); }
245: }
246: 
247: function pct(sorted, p) { if (!sorted.length) return null; const i = Math.min(sorted.length - 1, Math.ceil((p / 100) * sorted.length) - 1); return +sorted[Math.max(0, i)].toFixed(1); }
248: 
249: /* ── کشفِ زانو ── */
250: function detectKnee(steps, sloMs) {
251:   const cands = [];
252:   steps.forEach((s, i) => {
253:     if (s.p95 != null && s.p95 > sloMs) cands.push({ method: 'slo', stepIndex: i, concurrency: s.concurrency, detail: 'p95=' + s.p95 + 'ms > SLO=' + sloMs + 'ms' });
254:     if (i > 0 && steps[i - 1].throughput != null && s.throughput != null) {
255:       const dC = s.concurrency - steps[i - 1].concurrency;
256:       const dT = s.throughput - steps[i - 1].throughput;
257:       if (dC > 0 && dT / dC < 0.5) cands.push({ method: 'plateau', stepIndex: i, concurrency: s.concurrency, detail: 'Δthroughput/Δconcurrency=' + (dT / dC).toFixed(2) + ' < 0.5' });
258:     }
259:     if (s.errorRatePct != null && s.errorRatePct > 1) cands.push({ method: 'errors', stepIndex: i, concurrency: s.concurrency, detail: '5xx=' + s.errorRatePct + '٪ > 1٪' });
260:   });
261:   const first = cands.length ? cands.reduce((a, b) => (a.stepIndex <= b.stepIndex ? a : b)) : null;
262:   const sustained = first ? (steps[first.stepIndex - 1] || steps[first.stepIndex]) : steps[steps.length - 1];
263:   return {
264:     knee: first || { method: 'none', stepIndex: -1, concurrency: null, detail: 'در بازهٔ نردبان اشباع دیده نشد — نردبان را گسترش بده' },
265:     maxSustainableRps: sustained ? sustained.throughput : null,
266:     candidates: cands,
267:   };
268: }
269: 
270: /* ── اجرای یک پله ── */
271: async function runStep(concurrency, seconds, payloads, headers) {
272:   const lat = []; const perEp = {}; let done = 0, err5 = 0, err0 = 0;
273:   const deadline = performance.now() + seconds * 1000;
274:   async function worker() {
275:     while (performance.now() < deadline) {
276:       const ep = pick(PROFILES[opt.profile]);
277:       const r = await fire(ep, payloads, headers);
278:       done++;
279:       lat.push(r.ms);
280:       const k = ep.method + ' ' + ep.path;
281:       perEp[k] = perEp[k] || { n: 0, p95sorted: [], errors: 0 };
282:       perEp[k].n++;
283:       perEp[k].p95sorted.push(r.ms);
284:       if (r.status >= 500) { err5++; perEp[k].errors++; }
285:       if (r.status === 0) { err0++; perEp[k].errors++; }
286:     }
287:   }
288:   await Promise.all(Array.from({ length: concurrency }, worker));
289:   const sorted = [...lat].sort((a, b) => a - b);
290:   const secs = seconds;
291:   const byEp = {};
292:   for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(perEp)) {
293:     const s = v.p95sorted.sort((a, b) => a - b);
294:     byEp[k] = { count: v.n, p50: pct(s, 50), p95: pct(s, 95), p99: pct(s, 99), errors: v.errors };
295:   }
296:   return {
297:     concurrency, seconds,
298:     requests: done,
299:     throughput: +(done / secs).toFixed(1),
300:     p50: pct(sorted, 50), p95: pct(sorted, 95), p99: pct(sorted, 99),
301:     errorRatePct: +(100 * (err5 / Math.max(1, done))).toFixed(3),
302:     transportErrors: err0,
303:     perEndpoint: byEp,
304:   };
305: }
306: 
307: /* ── اصلی ── */
308: (async () => {
309:   if (has('--validate') || has('--help')) validate();
310: 
311:   const problems = [];
312:   if (!opt.target) problems.push('--target لازم است (یا PAYESH_BASE_URL)');
313:   if (!has('--confirm-staging')) problems.push('این ابزار فقط برای staging است — --confirm-staging بدهید');
314:   if (problems.length) { problems.forEach((p) => console.error('✗ ' + p)); process.exit(2); }
315: 
316:   let payloads = {};
317:   if (opt.payloads) payloads = JSON.parse(require('fs').readFileSync(opt.payloads, 'utf8'));
318:   const headers = opt.token ? { authorization: 'Bearer ' + opt.token } : {};
319:   const steps = opt.staircase.split(',').map((s) => Number(s.trim()));
320: 
321:   console.log('کاوشگر اشباع — ' + opt.target + ' · پروفایل ' + opt.profile);
322:   const stepResults = [];
323:   for (const c of steps) {
324:     console.log('── پلهٔ ' + c + ' هم‌زمان (گرم‌کردان ' + opt.warmupSeconds + 's…)');
325:     await runStep(Math.min(c, 10), opt.warmupSeconds, payloads, headers); /* گرم‌کردن سبک */
326:     const mBefore = await scrapeMetrics();
327:     const r = await runStep(c, opt.stepSeconds, payloads, headers);
328:     const mAfter = await scrapeMetrics();
329:     r.metrics = extractMetrics(mBefore, mAfter);
330:     r.pg = await pgStats();
331:     r.redis = await redisInfo();
332:     console.log('   throughput=' + r.throughput + '/s · p50/p95/p99=' + r.p50 + '/' + r.p95 + '/' + r.p99 + 'ms · 5xx=' + r.errorRatePct + '٪');
333:     stepResults.push(r);
334:     if (r.errorRatePct > opt.abortErrorRate) { console.log('   ⚠ نرخ خطا > ' + opt.abortErrorRate + '٪ — توقفِ اضطراری (ایمنی)'); break; }
335:   }
336: 
337:   const knee = detectKnee(stepResults, opt.sloP95ms);
338:   const envelope = {
339:     schema: 'CAPACITY_ENVELOPE/1',
340:     meta: { date: new Date().toISOString(), target: opt.target, profile: opt.profile, datasetScale: opt.datasetScale || 'NOT-RECORDED', nodes: opt.nodes || 'NOT-RECORDED', args: argv.join(' ') },
341:     workload: { profile: opt.profile, weights: PROFILES[opt.profile], payloadsFile: opt.payloads || 'پیش‌فرضِ حداقلی (نوشتنِ واقعی سنجه نشده)' },
342:     staircase: steps,
343:     steps: stepResults,
344:     knee,
345:     slo: { p95ms: opt.sloP95ms, verdictP95: stepResults.every((s) => s.p95 == null || s.p95 <= opt.sloP95ms) ? 'PASS' : 'BREACHED' },
346:     verdict: 'این پاکت تا بازبینیِ انسانی «پیش‌نویس» است — قواعد حکم در docs/CAPACITY_ENVELOPE_TEMPLATE.md',
347:   };
348:   require('fs').writeFileSync(opt.out, JSON.stringify(envelope, null, 2));
349:   console.log('── زانو: ' + knee.knee.method + (knee.knee.concurrency != null ? ' @ concurrency=' + knee.knee.concurrency : '') + ' · پایدارِ بیشینه ≈ ' + knee.maxSustainableRps + ' rps');
350:   console.log('پاکت: ' + opt.out);
351:   process.exit(0);
352: })().catch((e) => { console.error('خطای کاوشگر: ' + (e.stack || e)); process.exit(1); });

### tests/performance/run-benchmarks.sh
1: #!/usr/bin/env bash
2: # ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
3: # run-benchmarks.sh — اجرای جامع اسکریپت‌های تست بار و بنچ‌مارک k6
4: # سامانه پایش (فاز ۵ — ارزیابی عملکرد و پایداری در مقیاس کشوری)
5: # ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
6: 
7: set -e
8: 
9: DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
10: ROOT_DIR="$(cd "$DIR/../.." && pwd)"
11: 
12: # رنگ‌بندی ترمینال
13: GREEN='\033[0;32m'
14: BLUE='\033[0;34m'
15: YELLOW='\033[1;33m'
16: RED='\033[0;31m'
17: NC='\033[0m' # No Color
18: 
19: ENV="local"
20: TARGET_SCENARIO=""
21: TARGET_SUITE=""
22: CUSTOM_VUS=""
23: CUSTOM_DURATION=""
24: SPAWN_LOCAL_SERVER=false
25: LOCAL_SERVER_PID=""
26: BASE_URL=""
27: 
28: print_banner() {
29:   echo -e "${BLUE}═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════${NC}"
30:   echo -e "${GREEN}🚀 سامانه پایش — اسکریپت اجرای آزمون‌های کارایی و پایداری k6 (فاز ۵)${NC}"
31:   echo -e "${BLUE}═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════${NC}"
32: }
33: 
34: usage() {
35:   echo "راهنمای استفاده از اسکریپت:"
36:   echo "  $0 [گزینه‌ها]"
37:   echo ""
38:   echo "گزینه‌ها:"
39:   echo "  --scenario <name>    اجرای یک سناریوی خاص (01-login, 02-bootstrap, 03-attendance, 04-grades, 05-notifications, 06-sync-batch, all)"
40:   echo "  --suite <name>       اجرای یک سوئیت تست (saturation, chaos-redis, soak-24h, spike-mehr, all)"
41:   echo "  --env <environment>  انتخاب محیط هدف: local (پیش‌فرض), staging, production, cluster"
42:   echo "  --base-url <url>     تنظیم مستقیم آدرس URL سرور هدف"
43:   echo "  --vus <number>       تنظیم تعداد کاربران همزمان (VU) به عنوان Override"
44:   echo "  --duration <time>    تنظیم مدت زمان اجرای آزمون (مثال: 30s, 2m, 24h)"
45:   echo "  -h, --help           نمایش این راهنما"
46:   echo ""
47:   echo "مثال‌ها:"
48:   echo "  $0 --scenario 01-login"
49:   echo "  $0 --scenario all --env local"
50:   echo "  $0 --suite saturation"
51:   echo "  $0 --suite spike-mehr --base-url http://127.0.0.1:3000"
52:   exit 0
53: }
54: 
55: # پردازش پارامترهای خط فرمان
56: while [[ $# -gt 0 ]]; do
57:   case $1 in
58:     --scenario)
59:       TARGET_SCENARIO="$2"
60:       shift 2
61:       ;;
62:     --suite)
63:       TARGET_SUITE="$2"
64:       shift 2
65:       ;;
66:     --env)
67:       ENV="$2"
68:       shift 2
69:       ;;
70:     --base-url)
71:       BASE_URL="$2"
72:       shift 2
73:       ;;
74:     --vus)
75:       CUSTOM_VUS="$2"
76:       shift 2
77:       ;;
78:     --duration)
79:       CUSTOM_DURATION="$2"
80:       shift 2
81:       ;;
82:     -h|--help)
83:       usage
84:       ;;
85:     *)
86:       echo -e "${RED}خطا: گزینه ناشناخته $1${NC}"
87:       usage
88:       ;;
89:   esac
90: done
91: 
92: # بررسی نصب بودن k6
93: if ! command -v k6 &> /dev/null; then
94:   echo -e "${RED}❌ ابزار k6 یافت نشد! لطفاً ابتدا آن را نصب کنید:${NC}"
95:   echo "   npm install -g k6 یا دانلود از https://k6.io"
96:   exit 1
97: fi
98: 
99: # تعیین Base URL
100: if [ -z "$BASE_URL" ]; then
101:   case $ENV in
102:     local)
103:       BASE_URL="http://127.0.0.1:3000"
104:       ;;
105:     staging)
106:       BASE_URL="https://staging.payesh.ir"
107:       ;;
108:     production)
109:       BASE_URL="https://api.payesh.ir"
110:       ;;
111:     cluster)
112:       BASE_URL="http://load-test-cluster.payesh.internal:8080"
113:       ;;
114:     *)
115:       BASE_URL="http://127.0.0.1:3000"
116:       ;;
117:   esac
118: fi
119: 
120: cleanup() {
121:   if [ "$SPAWN_LOCAL_SERVER" = true ] && [ -n "$LOCAL_SERVER_PID" ]; then
122:     echo -e "\n${YELLOW}⏹️ در حال متوقف‌سازی سرور محلی موقت (PID: $LOCAL_SERVER_PID)...${NC}"
123:     kill -TERM "$LOCAL_SERVER_PID" 2>/dev/null || true
124:     wait "$LOCAL_SERVER_PID" 2>/dev/null || true
125:   fi
126: }
127: trap cleanup EXIT INT TERM
128: 
129: # اگر محیط local است و سرور در حال اجرا نیست، سرور موقت راه‌اندازی شود
130: if [ "$ENV" = "local" ] && [[ "$BASE_URL" =~ 127\.0\.0\.1:3000|localhost:3000 ]]; then
131:   if ! curl -s "http://127.0.0.1:3000/api/health" &> /dev/null; then
132:     echo -e "${YELLOW}⚡ سرور محلی فعال روی پورت ۳۰۰۰ یافت نشد. راه‌اندازی سرور آزمون محلی...${NC}"
133:     cd "$ROOT_DIR"
134:     PORT=3000 \
135:     PAYESH_DEMO_CODE=1 \
136:     PAYESH_SMS_PROVIDER=mock \
137:     PAYESH_SMS_DRY_RUN=1 \
138:     PAYESH_SMS_MAX_PER_DAY=1000000 \
139:     PAYESH_SMS_COOLDOWN_S=0 \
140:     PAYESH_SMS_DAILY_CAP=1000000 \
141:     PAYESH_SMS_IP_LIMIT=1000000 \
142:     PAYESH_SMS_PHONE_LIMIT=1000000 \
143:     PAYESH_LOGIN_IP_LIMIT=1000000 \
144:     node server/index.js &
145:     LOCAL_SERVER_PID=$!
146:     SPAWN_LOCAL_SERVER=true
147:     sleep 2
148:     if ! curl -s "http://127.0.0.1:3000/api/health" &> /dev/null; then
149:       echo -e "${RED}❌ خطا در راه‌اندازی سرور آزمون محلی!${NC}"
150:       exit 1
151:     fi
152:     echo -e "${GREEN}✅ سرور محلی با موفقیت راه‌اندازی شد.${NC}"
153:   fi
154: fi
155: 
156: print_banner
157: echo -e "محیط هدف: ${GREEN}$ENV${NC} ($BASE_URL)"
158: echo ""
159: 
160: run_k6_file() {
161:   local script_path="$1"
162:   local test_name="$2"
163:   
164:   echo -e "${BLUE}▶ در حال اجرای:${NC} ${YELLOW}$test_name${NC} ($script_path)"
165:   
166:   local k6_args=()
167:   k6_args+=("-e" "BASE_URL=$BASE_URL")
168:   
169:   if [ -n "$CUSTOM_VUS" ]; then
170:     k6_args+=("--vus" "$CUSTOM_VUS")
171:   fi
172:   if [ -n "$CUSTOM_DURATION" ]; then
173:     k6_args+=("--duration" "$CUSTOM_DURATION")
174:   fi
175: 
176:   k6 run "${k6_args[@]}" "$script_path"
177:   local status=$?
178:   
179:   if [ $status -eq 0 ]; then
180:     echo -e "${GREEN}✔ آزمون $test_name با موفقیت به پایان رسید.${NC}\n"
181:   else
182:     echo -e "${RED}✖ آزمون $test_name با خطا مواجه شد (کد خروج: $status).${NC}\n"
183:     return $status
184:   fi
185: }
186: 
187: ALL_PASSED=true
188: 
189: # اجرای سناریوها
190: if [ -n "$TARGET_SCENARIO" ]; then
191:   if [ "$TARGET_SCENARIO" = "all" ]; then
192:     for s in "$DIR/scenarios/"*.js; do
193:       [ -e "$s" ] || continue
194:       name="$(basename "$s" .js)"
195:       run_k6_file "$s" "سناریوی $name" || ALL_PASSED=false
196:     done
197:   else
198:     file="$DIR/scenarios/$TARGET_SCENARIO.js"
199:     if [ ! -f "$file" ]; then
200:       file="$DIR/scenarios/0$TARGET_SCENARIO.js"
201:     fi
202:     if [ -f "$file" ]; then
203:       run_k6_file "$file" "سناریوی $TARGET_SCENARIO" || ALL_PASSED=false
204:     else
205:       echo -e "${RED}❌ فایل سناریو یافت نشد: $TARGET_SCENARIO${NC}"
206:       exit 1
207:     fi
208:   fi
209: fi
210: 
211: # اجرای سوئیت‌ها
212: if [ -n "$TARGET_SUITE" ]; then
213:   if [ "$TARGET_SUITE" = "all" ]; then
214:     for s in "$DIR/suites/"*.js; do
215:       [ -e "$s" ] || continue
216:       name="$(basename "$s" .js)"
217:       # برای soak-24h در اجرای سراسری، مدت زمان کوتاه تنظیم می‌شود مگر آنکه کاربر override داده باشد
218:       if [ "$name" = "soak-24h-test" ] && [ -z "$CUSTOM_DURATION" ]; then
219:         SOAK_DURATION="10s" run_k6_file "$s" "سوئیت $name (تست کوتاه)" || ALL_PASSED=false
220:       else
221:         run_k6_file "$s" "سوئیت $name" || ALL_PASSED=false
222:       fi
223:     done
224:   else
225:     file="$DIR/suites/$TARGET_SUITE.js"
226:     if [ ! -f "$file" ]; then
227:       file="$DIR/suites/$TARGET_SUITE-test.js"
228:     fi
229:     if [ -f "$file" ]; then
230:       run_k6_file "$file" "سوئیت $TARGET_SUITE" || ALL_PASSED=false
231:     else
232:       echo -e "${RED}❌ فایل سوئیت یافت نشد: $TARGET_SUITE${NC}"
233:       exit 1
234:     fi
235:   fi
236: fi
237: 
238: # اگر هیچ گزینه‌ای داده نشده بود، سناریوی پیش‌فرض اجرا شود
239: if [ -z "$TARGET_SCENARIO" ] && [ -z "$TARGET_SUITE" ]; then
240:   echo -e "${YELLOW}هیچ سناریو یا سوئیتی مشخص نشده است. اجرای پیش‌فرض سناریوی ورود (01-login)...${NC}"
241:   run_k6_file "$DIR/scenarios/01-login.js" "سناریوی ورود (01-login)" || ALL_PASSED=false
242: fi
243: 
244: if [ "$ALL_PASSED" = true ]; then
245:   echo -e "${GREEN}═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════${NC}"
246:   echo -e "${GREEN}🎉 تمام آزمون‌های عملکردی انتخاب‌شده با موفقیت ۱۰۰٪ پاس شدند ✅[0m"
247:   echo -e "${GREEN}═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════${NC}"
248:   exit 0
249: else
250:   echo -e "${RED}═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════${NC}"
251:   echo -e "${RED}❌ برخی از آزمون‌های عملکردی با خطا یا نقض آستانه‌های SLO مواجه شدند.${NC}"
252:   echo -e "${RED}═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════${NC}"
253:   exit 1
254: fi

### tests/performance/suites/saturation-test.js
1: /* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
2:    tests/performance/suites/saturation-test.js
3:    سوئیت آزمون اشباع و نقطه شکست پایگاه داده (Database Saturation Test)
4:    سند مرجع: docs/PERFORMANCE_TESTING_PLAN.md §۳
5:    ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
6: import http from 'k6/http';
7: import { check, sleep, group } from 'k6';
8: import { dbQueryDuration, successfulRequests } from '../helpers/metrics.js';
9: import { getBaseUrl, setupAllSessions, getAuthHeaders } from '../helpers/auth-helper.js';
10: import { generateAttendancePayload, generateGradePayload, generateBatchSyncOps } from '../helpers/payload-generator.js';
11: 
12: export const options = {
13:   scenarios: {
14:     database_saturation_ramp: {
15:       executor: 'ramping-vus',
16:       startVUs: 5,
17:       stages: [
18:         { duration: '10s', target: 25 },   // فاز ۱: ترافیک معمول
19:         { duration: '20s', target: 75 },   // فاز ۲: افزایش بار و پر شدن کانکشن‌پول
20:         { duration: '30s', target: 150 },  // فاز ۳: ورود به محدوده آستانه و اشباع
21:         { duration: '10s', target: 200 },  // فاز ۴: تست حداکثر فشار (Knee Point)
22:         { duration: '10s', target: 0 },    // فاز ۵: بازیابی و تخلیه بار
23:       ],
24:       gracefulRampDown: '10s',
25:     },
26:   },
27:   thresholds: {
28:     http_req_failed: ['rate<0.05'], // در اوج اشباع کمتر از ۵٪ خطا مجاز است
29:     http_req_duration: ['p(95)<1200'], // آستانه مجاز تاخیر در اوج بار
30:   },
31: };
32: 
33: const BASE_URL = getBaseUrl();
34: 
35: export function setup() {
36:   return setupAllSessions(BASE_URL);
37: }
38: 
39: export default function (data) {
40:   const managerSession = data.manager;
41:   const teacherSession = data.teacher;
42:   if (!managerSession || !managerSession.cookie || !teacherSession || !teacherSession.cookie) return;
43: 
44:   const managerHeaders = getAuthHeaders(managerSession.cookie);
45:   const teacherHeaders = getAuthHeaders(teacherSession.cookie);
46:   const operationType = __ITER % 4;
47: 
48:   group('ترکیب عملیات سنگین پایگاه داده', () => {
49:     if (operationType === 0) {
50:       // عملیات ۱: خوانش سنگین فهرست‌ها با فیلتر (Indexed Database Read)
51:       const res = http.get(`${BASE_URL}/api/v1/students?limit=50&sort=name`, {
52:         headers: managerHeaders,
53:         tags: { name: 'GET /api/v1/students [heavy read]' }
54:       });
55:       check(res, { 'students list read ok': (r) => r.status === 200 });
56:     } else if (operationType === 1) {
57:       // عملیات ۲: نوشتن ردیف حضور و غیاب توسط مدیر (Atomic Insert)
58:       const payload = JSON.stringify(generateAttendancePayload({
59:         school_id: 1,
60:         class_id: 1 + (__VU % 5),
61:         student_id: 16 + (__VU % 30)
62:       }));
63:       const res = http.post(`${BASE_URL}/api/v1/attendance`, payload, {
64:         headers: managerHeaders,
65:         tags: { name: 'POST /api/v1/attendance [write]' }
66:       });
67:       check(res, { 'attendance write ok': (r) => r.status === 200 || r.status === 201 });
68:     } else if (operationType === 2) {
69:       // عملیات ۳: واکشی نمرات همراه با محاسبات سمت سرور
70:       const res = http.get(`${BASE_URL}/api/v1/grades?limit=30`, {
71:         headers: managerHeaders,
72:         tags: { name: 'GET /api/v1/grades [query]' }
73:       });
74:       check(res, { 'grades query ok': (r) => r.status === 200 });
75:     } else {
76:       // عملیات ۴: همگام‌سازی دسته آفلاین توسط مدیر (Batch Write)
77:       const ops = generateBatchSyncOps(5, managerSession.user.id, managerSession.user.school_id || 1, 'manager');
78:       const res = http.post(`${BASE_URL}/api/sync`, JSON.stringify({ ops }), {
79:         headers: managerHeaders,
80:         tags: { name: 'POST /api/sync [batch]' }
81:       });
82:       check(res, { 'sync batch ok': (r) => r.status === 200 });
83:     }
84:   });
85: 
86:   sleep(0.1 + Math.random() * 0.2);
87: }

### tests/performance/suites/chaos-redis-test.js
1: /* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
2:    tests/performance/suites/chaos-redis-test.js
3:    سوئیت آزمون آشوب و پایداری در زمان قطع Redis (Redis Chaos & Fallback Test)
4:    سند مرجع: docs/PERFORMANCE_TESTING_PLAN.md §۴
5:    ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
6: import http from 'k6/http';
7: import { check, sleep, group } from 'k6';
8: import { circuitBreakerTrips, redisLatency, successfulRequests } from '../helpers/metrics.js';
9: import { getBaseUrl, setupAllSessions, getAuthHeaders } from '../helpers/auth-helper.js';
10: 
11: export const options = {
12:   scenarios: {
13:     redis_chaos_traffic: {
14:       executor: 'constant-vus',
15:       vus: 30,
16:       duration: '45s',
17:     },
18:   },
19:   thresholds: {
20:     // الزامات تاب‌آوری در زمان قطع کش:
21:     // ۱. هیچ خطای ۵۰۰ یا کرش نباید بازگردد (Circuit Breaker & Fallback)
22:     // ۲. نشست‌ها باید بدون وقفه تایید شوند
23:     http_req_failed: ['rate<0.01'],
24:     http_req_duration: ['p(95)<400'],
25:   },
26: };
27: 
28: const BASE_URL = getBaseUrl();
29: 
30: export function setup() {
31:   return setupAllSessions(BASE_URL);
32: }
33: 
34: export default function (data) {
35:   const roles = ['manager', 'teacher', 'student', 'parent'];
36:   const role = roles[__VU % roles.length];
37:   const session = data[role];
38: 
39:   if (!session || !session.cookie) return;
40:   const headers = getAuthHeaders(session.cookie);
41: 
42:   group('تست تاب‌آوری و اعتبارسنجی خودکفا در غیاب Redis', () => {
43:     // ۱. فراخوانی اندپوینت Bootstrap (بررسی عملکرد لایه L1 In-Memory Cache و عدم رخداد 500)
44:     const t0 = Date.now();
45:     const bootstrapRes = http.get(`${BASE_URL}/api/v1/bootstrap`, {
46:       headers: headers,
47:       tags: { name: 'GET /api/v1/bootstrap [chaos-resilience]' }
48:     });
49:     const lat = Date.now() - t0;
50:     redisLatency.add(lat);
51: 
52:     check(bootstrapRes, {
53:       'no 500 internal server error during redis disruption': (r) => r.status !== 500,
54:       'status is 200 with fallback data': (r) => r.status === 200,
55:       'response has valid user payload': (r) => {
56:         try {
57:           const b = JSON.parse(r.body);
58:           return b.ok === true && !!b.user;
59:         } catch (e) {
60:           return false;
61:         }
62:       }
63:     });
64: 
65:     // ۲. بررسی اعتبارسنجی توکن نشست رمزنگاری‌شده (JWT Verification Resilience)
66:     const meRes = http.get(`${BASE_URL}/api/auth/me`, {
67:       headers: headers,
68:       tags: { name: 'GET /api/auth/me [token-resilience]' }
69:     });
70: 
71:     check(meRes, {
72:       'jwt verified without external redis state': (r) => r.status === 200,
73:       'identity matches sub': (r) => {
74:         try {
75:           const b = JSON.parse(r.body);
76:           return b.ok === true && b.user && b.user.id === session.user.id;
77:         } catch (e) {
78:           return false;
79:         }
80:       }
81:     });
82:   });
83: 
84:   sleep(0.3);
85: }

### tests/performance/suites/soak-24h-test.js
1: /* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
2:    tests/performance/suites/soak-24h-test.js
3:    سوئیت آزمون پایداری و استقامت طولانی‌مدت (24-Hour Soak / Endurance Test)
4:    سند مرجع: docs/PERFORMANCE_TESTING_PLAN.md §۸
5:    ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
6: import http from 'k6/http';
7: import { check, sleep, group } from 'k6';
8: import { successfulRequests } from '../helpers/metrics.js';
9: import { getBaseUrl, setupAllSessions, getAuthHeaders } from '../helpers/auth-helper.js';
10: import { generateAttendancePayload, generateGradePayload } from '../helpers/payload-generator.js';

12: // مدت زمان تست قابل تنظیم از طریق محیط (پیش‌فرض ۲۴ ساعت برای آزمون واقعی، ۱ دقیقه برای تست اعتبارسنجی)
13: const SOAK_DURATION = __ENV.SOAK_DURATION || '24h';
14: const SOAK_VUS = parseInt(__ENV.SOAK_VUS || '30', 10);
15: 
16: export const options = {
17:   scenarios: {
18:     continuous_soak_load: {
19:       executor: 'constant-vus',
20:       vus: SOAK_VUS,
21:       duration: SOAK_DURATION,
22:     },
23:   },
24:   thresholds: {
25:     // در طول آزمون ۲۴ ساعته:
26:     // ۱. نرخ خطا باید زیر ۰.۱٪ باقی بماند (عدم رخداد نشت حافظه یا قطع ارتباط)
27:     // ۲. زمان پاسخ ۹۵٪ درخواست‌ها باید زیر ۲۰۰ میلی‌ثانیه حفظ شود
28:     http_req_failed: ['rate<0.001'],
29:     http_req_duration: ['p(95)<200', 'p(99)<400'],
30:   },
31: };
32: 
33: const BASE_URL = getBaseUrl();
34: 
35: export function setup() {
36:   return setupAllSessions(BASE_URL);
37: }
38: 
39: export default function (data) {
40:   const sessionIndex = __VU % 4;
41:   const session = sessionIndex === 0 ? data.manager : (sessionIndex === 1 ? data.teacher : (sessionIndex === 2 ? data.student : data.parent));
42:   if (!session || !session.cookie) return;
43: 
44:   const headers = getAuthHeaders(session.cookie);
45:   const phase = __ITER % 3;
46: 
47:   group('چرخه پایدار درخواست‌های پیوسته', () => {
48:     if (phase === 0) {
49:       // بررسی سلامت و وضعیت سیستم (Heartbeat / Healthcheck)
50:       const res = http.get(`${BASE_URL}/api/health`);
51:       check(res, { 'healthcheck is 200': (r) => r.status === 200 });
52:     } else if (phase === 1) {
53:       // بارگذاری داده اولیه (Bootstrap Read)
54:       const res = http.get(`${BASE_URL}/api/v1/bootstrap`, { headers: headers });
55:       check(res, { 'bootstrap ok': (r) => r.status === 200 });
56:     } else {
57:       // ثبت تراکنش سبک دوره‌ای
58:       if (session.user.role === 'teacher' || session.user.role === 'manager') {
59:         const payload = JSON.stringify(generateAttendancePayload({
60:           school_id: 1,
61:           class_id: 1,
62:           student_id: 16 + (__VU % 15)
63:         }));
64:         const res = http.post(`${BASE_URL}/api/v1/attendance`, payload, { headers: headers });
65:         check(res, { 'transaction ok': (r) => r.status === 200 || r.status === 201 });
66:       } else {
67:         const res = http.get(`${BASE_URL}/api/bell/now`, { headers: headers });
68:         check(res, { 'bell query ok': (r) => r.status === 200 });
69:       }
70:     }
71:   });
72: 
73:   // خواب تنظیم‌شده برای شبیه‌سازی رفتار طبیعی کاربران و جلوگیری از اسپایک غیرواقعی
74:   sleep(1.0 + Math.random() * 0.5);
75: }

71: export function getBaseUrl() {
72:   if (__ENV.BASE_URL) {
73:     return __ENV.BASE_URL.replace(/\/+$/, '');
74:   }
75:   return 'http://127.0.0.1:3000';
76: }
77: 
78: export function extractCookie(response, cookieName = 'payesh_session') {
79:   if (!response || !response.headers) return null;
80:   
81:   if (response.cookies && response.cookies[cookieName] && response.cookies[cookieName].length > 0) {
82:     return `${cookieName}=${response.cookies[cookieName][0].value}`;
83:   }
84:   
85:   const setCookie = response.headers['Set-Cookie'] || response.headers['set-cookie'];
86:   if (!setCookie) return null;
87: 
88:   const match = setCookie.match(new RegExp(`${cookieName}=([^;]+)`));
89:   return match ? `${cookieName}=${match[1]}` : null;
90: }
91: 
92: export function loginUser(baseUrl, user) {
93:   const url = baseUrl || getBaseUrl();
94:   const phone = user.phone;
95:   const nationalId = user.national_id;
96: 
97:   // ۱. درخواست ارسال کد ورود
98:   const sendRes = http.post(
99:     `${url}/api/auth/send-code`,
100:     JSON.stringify({ phone: phone }),
101:     { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }
102:   );
103: 
104:   let demoCode = '1234';
105:   try {
106:     const json = JSON.parse(sendRes.body);
107:     if (json.demo_code) demoCode = json.demo_code;
108:   } catch (e) {}
109: 
110:   // ۲. تکمیل فرآیند ورود با کد اعتبارسنجی
111:   const loginRes = http.post(
112:     `${url}/api/auth/login`,
113:     JSON.stringify({
114:       phone: phone,
115:       code: demoCode,
116:       national_id: nationalId
117:     }),
118:     { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }
119:   );
120: 
121:   const cookie = extractCookie(loginRes);
122:   const ok = loginRes.status === 200 && !!cookie;
123: 
124:   return {
125:     ok: ok,
126:     status: loginRes.status,
127:     cookie: cookie,
128:     user: user,
129:     headers: {
130:       'Content-Type': 'application/json',
131:       'Cookie': cookie || ''
132:     }
133:   };
134: }
135: 
136: export function getAuthHeaders(cookie) {
137:   return {
138:     'Content-Type': 'application/json',
139:     'Cookie': cookie || ''
140:   };
141: }
142: 
143: export function setupAllSessions(baseUrl) {
144:   const url = baseUrl || getBaseUrl();
145:   return {
146:     manager: loginUser(url, SEED_USERS.manager),
147:     manager2: loginUser(url, SEED_USERS.manager2),
148:     teacher: loginUser(url, SEED_USERS.teacher),
149:     teacher2: loginUser(url, SEED_USERS.teacher2),
150:     student: loginUser(url, SEED_USERS.student),
151:     parent: loginUser(url, SEED_USERS.parent),
152:     superadmin: loginUser(url, SEED_USERS.superadmin)
153:   };
154: }

### server/db.js
1: /* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
2:    server/db.js — Unified Database Layer & PostgreSQL Connection Pool
3:    -------------------------------------------------------------------
4:    Phase 2: Relational PostgreSQL Engine with Graceful JSON Fallback
5:    - Full ACID transaction management with connection pooling.
6:    - Dual-mode operation: Native PostgreSQL when DATABASE_URL is set,
7:      or zero-dependency in-memory JSON fallback when unset.
8:    - Methods: query(sql, params), queryRead(sql, params), transaction(callback),
9:      ping(), persistOp(op), persistOpsBatch(ops), healthCheck(), close().
10:    - Supports: PG_POOL_MIN, PG_POOL_MAX, PG_TIMEOUT_MS, DATABASE_URL,
11:      READ_DATABASE_URL (+ READ_POOL_MIN, READ_POOL_MAX).
12: 
13:    Wave 10 (chat2) — Database Scale: READ REPLICA (additive, safe-by-default).
14:    An optional read-only connection pool is created when READ_DATABASE_URL is
15:    set. Heavy GET-list reads (the DB-native paged lists) are routed to it via
16:    queryRead(); if the replica is not configured or hiccups, queryRead() falls
17:    back to the primary pool (behaviour identical to before). Writes and sync /
18:    pull correctness reads (readCollection/readOne/delta) intentionally stay on
19:    the PRIMARY pool so a replica can never lag a client's own recent writes.
20:    Partitioning of large tables is DESIGN-ONLY here (pending a live PG run);
21:    see docs/WAVE10_DB_SCALE.md.
22:    ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
23: 'use strict';
24: 
25: const fs = require('fs');
26: const path = require('path');
27: /* ویو ۱۴ (Observability) — سیگنالِ metrics برای لایهٔ دیتابیس. بدون
28:    وابستگیِ بیرونی؛ metrics.js فقط crypto را require می‌کند (بدون چرخه). */
29: const metrics = require('./metrics');
30: 
31: /* آستانهٔ «کوئریِ کند» — فقط برای شمارش، نه برای تغییرِ رفتار. */
32: const DB_SLOW_MS = (Number(process.env.PAYESH_DB_SLOW_MS) > 0)
33:   ? Number(process.env.PAYESH_DB_SLOW_MS) : 250;
34: function dbSeconds(startNs) {
35:   try { return Number(process.hrtime.bigint() - startNs) / 1e9; }
36:   catch (e) { return 0; }
37: }
38: function dbSlow(startNs) {
39:   try { return Number(process.hrtime.bigint() - startNs) / 1e6 >= DB_SLOW_MS; }
40:   catch (e) { return false; }
41: }
42: 
43: let pg = null;
44: try {
45:   pg = require('pg');
46: } catch (e) {
47:   // pg driver is optional for offline/standalone execution
48: }
49: 
50: let pool = null;
51: let readPool = null;          /* Wave 10: optional read-replica pool (READ_DATABASE_URL) */
52: let readPoolActive = false;   /* Wave 10: true only after the replica answers a ping */
53: let replicaReprobeTimer = null; /* S3-1: کاوشِ خودکارِ بازگشتِ رپلیکا */
54: let REPLICA_REPROBE_MS = 10000;
55: let isPgActive = false;
56: let memoryStore = null;
57: let reconnectTimer = null;
58: 
59: const config = {
60:   connectionString: process.env.DATABASE_URL || null,
61:   min: parseInt(process.env.PG_POOL_MIN || '2', 10),
62:   max: parseInt(process.env.PG_POOL_MAX || '20', 10),
63:   connectionTimeoutMillis: parseInt(process.env.PG_TIMEOUT_MS || process.env.PG_TIMEOUT || '3000', 10),
64:   idleTimeoutMillis: 30000,
65:   /* Wave 10 — read replica (optional). When READ_DATABASE_URL is present a
66:      second read-only pool is opened and heavy GET-list reads route to it. */
67:   readConnectionString: process.env.READ_DATABASE_URL || null,
68:   readMin: parseInt(process.env.READ_POOL_MIN || '2', 10),
69:   readMax: parseInt(process.env.READ_POOL_MAX || '10', 10)
70: };
71: 
72: /* ── P0-1 (Package 1) — PostgreSQL production enforcement ─────────────
73:    In production the JSON/in-memory store must never be the backing store:
74:    it is per-process, so two instances silently diverge on every write
75:    (auth codes, idempotency UIDs, attendance, grades, finance) and a
76:    container restart loses everything since the last 2s persist tick.
77: 
78:    This is the exact contract Redis already implements for itself
79:    (server/redis.js:150-158 "REDIS_URL is required when NODE_ENV=production
80:    (in-memory fallback is dev-only)" and redis.js:224-236 "Redis unreachable
81:    in production"), so the two backing stores now fail the same way.
82: 
83:    ALLOW_MEMORY_FALLBACK=1 is an explicit dev/test opt-in. It is IGNORED in
84:    production — a flag must not be able to re-open a production data-loss
85:    path. */
86: function isProductionEnv() {
87:   return process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production' || process.env.PAYESH_ENV === 'production';
88: }
89: 
90: function memoryFallbackAllowed() {
91:   /* Phase 8.1 (R5): NODE_ENV=production is the hard-production contract —
92:      the explicit opt-in flag is IGNORED here (the P0-1 comment above: "a
93:      flag must not be able to re-open a production data-loss path"). This
94:      restores the behaviour pinned by tests/pg-prod-no-json-writes.js (a3/a4),
95:      tests/pg-prod-boot-no-db.js (3a/3b) and tests/pg-prod-suite-policy.js
96:      (3a). PAYESH_ENV=production alone is the posture flag (TLS/env gates);
97:      it keeps the explicit dev/test opt-in so harnesses such as server17 T2
98:      and wave15 can exercise production code paths without infra — the boot
99:      env-mismatch warning still fires loudly in that shape. */
100:   if (process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production') return false;
101:   if (isProductionEnv()) return memoryFallbackRequested();
102:   return true; /* dev/test: fallback stays available (see init() warning) */
103: }
104: 
105: function memoryFallbackRequested() {
106:   return process.env.ALLOW_MEMORY_FALLBACK === '1';
107: }
108: 
109: /**
110:  * Why the JSON/in-memory backing store is (not) usable right now.
111:  * Pure/env-only — safe to call from boot gates and tests.
112:  */
113: function backingStorePolicy() {
114:   const production = isProductionEnv();
115:   const allow = memoryFallbackAllowed();
116:   return {
117:     production: production,
118:     allow_memory_fallback: allow,
119:     flag_requested: memoryFallbackRequested(),
120:     reason: !production
121:       ? 'non-production: JSON/in-memory backing store permitted'
122:       : (allow
123:         ? 'production: JSON/in-memory backing store permitted'
124:         : 'production: DATABASE_URL required — JSON/in-memory fallback is dev-only')
125:   };
126: }
127: 
128: /* P0-1 (round 3, item 2) — see the pool.on('connect') handler in init().
129:    Counted so a test can prove the event was observed rather than crashed on. */
130: let clientErrorCount = 0;
131: function onClientError(err) {
132:   clientErrorCount++;
133:   const msg = (err && err.message) || String(err);
134:   if (isProductionEnv()) {
135:     console.error('[DB] PostgreSQL client error in production — the operation fails explicitly and readiness goes red (no silent retry, no process crash):', msg);
136:   } else {
137:     console.warn('[DB] PostgreSQL client error:', msg);
138:   }
139: }
140: function clientErrors() { return clientErrorCount; }
141: 
142: /**
143:  * Initialize Database Layer & Pool Lifecycle
144:  * @param {Object} fallbackStore - In-memory store object loaded from payesh.json
145:  */
146: async function init(fallbackStore) {
147:   if (fallbackStore) {
148:     memoryStore = fallbackStore;
149:   }
150:   config.connectionString = process.env.DATABASE_URL || null;
151: 
152:   if (!config.connectionString || !pg) {
153:     isPgActive = false;
154:     readPoolActive = false;
155:     if (readPool) { try { readPool.end().catch(() => {}); } catch (e) {} readPool = null; }
156:     /* P0-1: production refuses the JSON/in-memory backing store (mirrors
157:        server/redis.js:150-158). ok:false is what the boot gate keys on. */
158:     if (!memoryFallbackAllowed()) {
159:       const error = !config.connectionString
160:         ? 'DATABASE_URL is required when NODE_ENV=production (JSON/in-memory fallback is dev-only)'
161:         : 'pg driver is not installed (required when NODE_ENV=production)';
162:       return { ok: false, driver: 'none', poolSize: 0, read_replica: false, error: error };
163:     }
164:     if (!memoryFallbackRequested()) {
165:       console.warn('[DB] no DATABASE_URL — using the JSON/in-memory store. Dev/test only; '
166:         + 'set ALLOW_MEMORY_FALLBACK=1 to silence this warning. In production this is a startup failure.');
167:     }
168:     return { ok: true, driver: 'memory', poolSize: 0, read_replica: false };
169:   }
170: 
171:   try {
172:     if (pool) {
173:       try { await pool.end(); } catch (e) {}
174:     }
175: 
176:     pool = new pg.Pool({
177:       connectionString: config.connectionString,
178:       min: config.min,
179:       max: config.max,
180:       connectionTimeoutMillis: config.connectionTimeoutMillis,
181:       idleTimeoutMillis: config.idleTimeoutMillis
182:     });
183: 
184:     pool.on('error', (err) => {
185:       console.error('[DB] PostgreSQL pool background error:', err.message);
186:       // Attempt reconnect if pool died
187:       scheduleReconnect();
188:     });
189: 
190:     /* P0-1 (round 3, item 2) — a checked-out pg Client whose connection dies
191:        emits an 'error' event that nothing listened to, which aborted the
192:        whole Node process ("Unhandled 'error' event" on Client). The in-flight
193:        query already rejects, so the caller still sees an explicit failure;
194:        this listener only stops the crash and records the event.
195: 
196:        Production semantics deliberately follow the existing Redis precedent
197:        (server/redis.js:224-236 and tests/redis-prodfail.js): operations throw
198:        and readiness goes red — the process is NOT killed, because a single
199:        reset connection must not become a full outage. Exit-on-client-error
200:        would be stricter than the contract this codebase already ships for its
201:        other shared backend. */
202:     pool.on('connect', (c) => {
203:       try { c.on('error', onClientError); } catch (e) {}
204:     });
205: 
206:     // Test connection & verify ping
207:     const client = await pool.connect();
208:     let serverTime = null;
209:     try {
210:       const res = await client.query('SELECT NOW() as server_time');
211:       serverTime = res.rows && res.rows[0] ? res.rows[0].server_time : null;
212:       isPgActive = true;
213:       if (reconnectTimer) {
214:         clearTimeout(reconnectTimer);
215:         reconnectTimer = null;
216:       }
217:     } finally {
218:       client.release();
219:     }
220: 
221:     /* Wave 10 — best-effort read-replica pool. Never fatal: if the replica is
222:        not configured or cannot answer a ping, readPoolActive stays false and
223:        queryRead() transparently uses the primary pool (identical behaviour). */
224:     try {
225:       if (config.readConnectionString && pg) {
226:         if (readPool) { try { await readPool.end(); } catch (e) {} }
227:         readPool = new pg.Pool({
228:           connectionString: config.readConnectionString,
229:           min: config.readMin,
230:           max: config.readMax,
231:           connectionTimeoutMillis: config.connectionTimeoutMillis,
232:           idleTimeoutMillis: config.idleTimeoutMillis
233:         });
234:         readPool.on('error', (err) => {
235:           console.error('[DB] Read-replica pool background error:', err.message);
236:           readPoolActive = false;   /* stop routing to a dead replica */
237:           scheduleReplicaReprobe(); /* S3-1: ولی برایِ بازگشتش کاوش کن */
238:         });
239:         const rc = await readPool.connect();
240:         try { await rc.query('SELECT 1 AS ping'); readPoolActive = true; }
241:         finally { rc.release(); }
242:       } else {
243:         /* READ_DATABASE_URL no longer set → drop any lingering replica pool */
244:         readPoolActive = false;
245:         if (readPool) { try { await readPool.end(); } catch (e) {} readPool = null; }
246:       }
247:     } catch (e) {
248:       readPoolActive = false;
249:       console.warn('[DB] Read replica unavailable; reads will use primary pool:', e.message);
250:       scheduleReplicaReprobe(); /* S3-1: اگر pool هست، بعداً دوباره بچش */
251:     }
252: 
253:     return { ok: true, driver: 'postgres', serverTime, read_replica: readPoolActive };
254:   } catch (err) {
255:     isPgActive = false;
256:     readPoolActive = false;
257:     if (readPool) { try { readPool.end().catch(() => {}); } catch (e) {} readPool = null; }
258:     scheduleReconnect();
259:     /* P0-1: an unreachable PostgreSQL in production is a startup failure, not a
260:        silent downgrade to the JSON store (mirrors server/redis.js:224-236). */
261:     if (!memoryFallbackAllowed()) {
262:       console.error('[DB] PostgreSQL unreachable in production — refusing the JSON/in-memory fallback:', err.message);
263:       return { ok: false, driver: 'none', error: 'PostgreSQL unreachable in production: ' + err.message };
264:     }
265:     console.warn('[DB] PostgreSQL connection failed. Falling back to JSON in-memory store:', err.message);
266:     return { ok: true, driver: 'memory', fallback: true, warning: err.message };
267:   }
268: }
269: 
270: /**
271:  * Schedule background reconnection when PG goes down
272:  */
273: function scheduleReconnect() {
274:   if (!config.connectionString || reconnectTimer) return;
275:   reconnectTimer = setTimeout(async () => {
276:     reconnectTimer = null;
277:     try {
278:       await init(memoryStore);
279:       if (isPgActive) {
280:         console.log('[DB] Successfully reconnected to PostgreSQL database');
281:       }
282:     } catch (e) {}
283:   }, 10000).unref();
284: }
285: 
286: /* S3-1 (موج ۱۰): خطایِ خودِ کوئری (گناهِ statement) در برابرِ خطایِ اتصال.
287:    کلاس‌هایِ SQLSTATE ‏22 (داده) / 23 (جامعیت) / 42 (نحو/دسترسی/ناشناخته)
288:    قطعاً گناهِ statement است و ربطی به سلامتِ رپلیکا ندارد — مسیریابی نباید
289:    بخوابد. هر چیزِ دیگر (از جمله خطایِ بی‌کد — پینِ D3c) ابهامِ اتصال است. */
290: function isReplicaQueryError(err) {
291:   const code = String((err && err.code) || '');
292:   return code.length >= 2 && (code.indexOf('22') === 0 || code.indexOf('23') === 0 || code.indexOf('42') === 0);
293: }
294: 
295: /**
296:  * S3-1: کاوشِ خودکارِ بازگشتِ رپلیکا (آینهٔ scheduleReconnect برایِ پرماری).
297:  * پیش‌تر رپلیکایِ خوابیده هیچ مسیرِ بازگشتی نداشت — تا ری‌استارت، همهٔ
298:  * خوانش‌ها رویِ پرماری می‌ماند. شکستِ کاوش ساکت است (شکستِ اول همان‌جا که
299:  * رخ داد لاگ شد)؛ فقط بهبودی لاگ می‌شود تا در قطعیِ طولانی لاگ هرز نرود.
300:  */
301: function scheduleReplicaReprobe() {
302:   if (!readPool || readPoolActive || replicaReprobeTimer) return;
303:   replicaReprobeTimer = setTimeout(async () => {
304:     replicaReprobeTimer = null;
305:     if (!readPool || readPoolActive) return;
306:     try {
307:       const rc = await readPool.connect();
308:       try { await rc.query('SELECT 1 AS ping'); }
309:       finally { try { rc.release(); } catch (e) {} }
310:       readPoolActive = true;
311:       console.log('[DB] Read replica recovered; routing reads back to replica');
312:     } catch (e) {
313:       scheduleReplicaReprobe();   /* هنوز خواب است — بعداً دوباره */
314:     }
315:   }, REPLICA_REPROBE_MS).unref();
316: }
317: 
318: /**
319:  * Check if PostgreSQL is active
320:  */
321: function isPostgres() {
322:   return isPgActive && pool !== null;
323: }
324: 
325: /**
326:  * Get active connection pool (primary/write)
327:  */
328: function getPool() {
329:   return pool;
330: }
331: 
332: /* Wave 10 — read-replica accessors (optional; active only when configured+live) */
333: function isReplicaActive() {
334:   return readPoolActive && readPool !== null;
335: }
336: function getReadPool() {
337:   return readPool;
338: }
339: 
340: /**
341:  * Execute parameterized query with automatic client lease
342:  */
343: async function query(text, params) {
344:   if (!isPostgres()) {
345:     return { rows: [], rowCount: 0 };
346:   }
347:   const start = process.hrtime.bigint();
348:   try {
349:     const res = await pool.query(text, params);
350:     metrics.observeDb('query', 'primary', dbSeconds(start), false, dbSlow(start));
351:     return res;
352:   } catch (err) {
353:     metrics.observeDb('query', 'primary', dbSeconds(start), true, false);
354:     console.error('[DB] Query execution error:', err.message);
355:     throw err;
356:   }
357: }
358: 
359: /**
360:  * Wave 10 — READ-ONLY query routed to the read replica when one is live.
361:  * Used by the heavy GET-list read seam (dbquery.executePagedList). Falls back
362:  * to the primary pool when the replica is absent or errors — so behaviour is
363:  * identical to query() in every non-replica configuration. Callers MUST only
364:  * pass read-only SQL here (no writes); replicas reject/ignore writes anyway.
365:  */
366: async function queryRead(text, params) {
367:   if (isReplicaActive()) {
368:     const start = process.hrtime.bigint();
369:     try {
370:       const res = await readPool.query(text, params);
371:       metrics.observeDb('query_read', 'replica', dbSeconds(start), false, dbSlow(start));
372:       return res;
373:     } catch (err) {
374:       metrics.observeDb('query_read', 'replica', dbSeconds(start), true, false);
375:       /* S3-1: فقط خطایِ اتصال مسیریابی را می‌خواباند (+ کاوشِ بازگشت)؛
376:          خطایِ خودِ کوئری (SQL بد) سلامتِ رپلیکا را زیرِ سؤال نمی‌برد.
377:          fallback به پرماری در هر دو حالت سرِ جاست. */
378:       if (!isReplicaQueryError(err)) {
379:         readPoolActive = false;   /* dead replica → stop routing, fall back to primary */
380:         scheduleReplicaReprobe();
381:       }
382:       console.warn('[DB] Read replica query failed; falling back to primary:', err.message);
383:     }
384:   }
385:   return query(text, params);
386: }
387: 
388: /* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
389:    Wave 1 (chat2) — Read-path seam.
390:    -------------------------------------------------------------------
391:    A single entry point for server READS so that, once PostgreSQL is the
392:    configured source of truth, every read route serves from the DB instead
393:    of reaching into the in-memory JSON `store`.
394: 
395:    Dual mode (identical to the rest of this file):
396:    - PG active (DATABASE_URL + pg driver):  SELECT * FROM "<table>"
397:    - memory fallback / offline:              (memoryStore[name] || [])
398:      In this mode memoryStore === the JSON store that `server/index.js`
399:      loaded, so the returned rows are byte-for-byte what the caller would
400:      have read from `store[name]` before. Behavior is therefore preserved.
401: 
402:    ⚠️ Verification note (recorded honestly): the PostgreSQL branch of
403:    these helpers is defined and wired, but was NOT executed against a live
404:    PostgreSQL in the CI sandbox for this part (no DATABASE_URL / driver /
405:    seeded DB). It is exercised only when a real PG is present. See
406:    docs/WAVE1_READS_INVENTORY.md. Only real data tables may be read here;
407:    internal store keys (__deleted_records, __server_version, …) are NOT
408:    relational tables and are intentionally NOT routable through PG.
409:    ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
410: 
411: const PG_READABLE_TABLE = /^[a-z][a-z0-9_]*$/;
412: 
413: /* Wave 1 — relational tables mirrored from server/schema.sql (domain
414:    collections only; server_* infra tables are PG-internal and have no store
415:    counterpart). Used by hydrateStoreFromPg: ONLY these collections are ever
416:    replaced from PG, so a collection without a PG table keeps its store copy. */
417: const SCHEMA_TABLES = new Set(('announcements app_settings assets assoc_minutes attendance attendance_modes ' +
418:   'bell_schedules bus_events bus_followups bus_locations bus_needs bus_routes bus_students calendar certificates ' +
419:   'class_subject_members classes corrections counselor_msgs counselor_refs counties discipline districts donations ' +
420:   'dojo_types dorm_assignments dorm_meals dorm_rooms enrollments exam_duties exam_terms exams grades hw_assignments ' +
421:   'hw_submissions installments internships leaves lib_books lib_loans makeup_classes meeting_slots messages ' +
422:   'nid_conflicts notifications notify_queue nudges offices parent_links parent_subscriptions parent_verifications ' +
423:   'pre_enrollments preapps provinces reexams safety_drills schedule scholarships school_years schools sedascores ' +
424:   'sms_log sms_wallet staff_attendance student_archive student_transfers subjects subscription_payments substitutions ' +
425:   'summer_classes support_tickets teacher_evaluations teacher_notes teacher_schools teacher_sms training_courses ' +
426:   'transactions transfer_requests tuition_plans tuitions users vclass_attendance vclass_links vclass_questions ' +
427:   'vclass_sessions visitors sync_conflicts').split(' '));
428: 
429: /* Wave 1 — shape parity: persistOp JSON-stringifies object values and the pg
430:    driver returns TIMESTAMPTZ as Date, while the store holds ISO strings and
431:    live objects. Revive PG rows so PG-reads are byte-shape-identical to the
432:    store shape callers already handle. Conservative: only {/[-led strings are
433:    parse-attempted (with fallback), Dates become ISO strings. Memory mode is
434:    untouched (it returns store references directly, never through here). */
435: function reviveValue(v) {
436:   if (v instanceof Date) return isNaN(v.getTime()) ? null : v.toISOString();
437:   if (typeof v === 'string' && (v.charAt(0) === '{' || v.charAt(0) === '[')) {
438:     try { return JSON.parse(v); } catch (e) { return v; }
439:   }
440:   return v;
441: }
442: function reviveRows(rows) {
443:   if (!Array.isArray(rows)) return [];
444:   return rows.map((r) => {
445:     if (!r || typeof r !== 'object') return r;
446:     const o = {};
447:     for (const k of Object.keys(r)) o[k] = reviveValue(r[k]);
448:     return o;
449:   });
450: }
451: 
452: function isPgReadableTable(name) {
453:   return typeof name === 'string'
454:     && PG_READABLE_TABLE.test(name)
455:     && name.indexOf('__') !== 0;
456: }
457: 
458: /* Wave 10 (chg_id): ستون‌هایِ داخلیِ لایهٔ DB — هرگز از سرور بیرون نمی‌روند.
459:    SELECT * آن‌ها را برمی‌گرداند (ستونِ پارتیشن‌نشده به ازایِ هر جدول)؛ این‌جا
460:    کنار گذاشته می‌شوند تا شکلِ سطرِ PG با حالتِ حافظه بایت‌به‌بایت یکی بماند و
461:    هیچ‌وقت به op کلاینت راه پیدا نکنند (validate.js آن را unknown_field می‌گیرد). */
462: const INTERNAL_ROW_COLUMNS = new Set(['chg_id']);
463: 
464: /**
465:  * Remove server-internal columns from rows leaving the DB layer.
466:  * Pure function; returns the same array shape with shallow-copied rows.
467:  * @param {Array<Object>} rows
468:  * @returns {Array<Object>}
469:  */
470: function stripInternalColumns(rows) {
471:   if (!Array.isArray(rows)) return rows;
472:   return rows.map((r) => {
473:     if (!r || typeof r !== 'object') return r;
474:     let has = false;
475:     for (const k of INTERNAL_ROW_COLUMNS) { if (k in r) { has = true; break; } }
476:     if (!has) return r;
477:     const c = Object.assign({}, r);
478:     for (const k of INTERNAL_ROW_COLUMNS) delete c[k];
479:     return c;
480:   });
481: }
482: 
483: /**
484:  * Read one full collection via the unified layer.
485:  * @param {string} name - collection / table name (real data table only)
486:  * @returns {Promise<Array>} array of row objects
487:  */
488: async function readCollection(name) {
489:   if (typeof name !== 'string' || !name) return [];
490:   if (isPostgres() && isPgReadableTable(name)) {
491:     const res = await pool.query(`SELECT * FROM "${name}"`);
492:     return stripInternalColumns(reviveRows(res.rows));
493:   }
494:   return (memoryStore && Array.isArray(memoryStore[name])) ? memoryStore[name] : [];
495: }
496: 
497: /**
498:  * Read a single row by numeric id (via readCollection).
499:  * @param {string} name - collection / table name
500:  * @param {number|string} id
501:  * @returns {Promise<Object|null>}
502:  */
503: async function readOne(name, id) {
504:   /* Wave 1 — indexed single-row read when PG is live (PK lookup instead of
505:      full-table scan); memory mode keeps the exact legacy find semantics. */
506:   if (isPostgres() && isPgReadableTable(name)) {
507:     const n = Number(id);
508:     if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return null;
509:     const res = await pool.query(`SELECT * FROM "${name}" WHERE id = $1 LIMIT 1`, [n]);
510:     const rows = stripInternalColumns(reviveRows(res.rows));
511:     return rows.length ? rows[0] : null;
512:   }
513:   const rows = await readCollection(name);
514:   const n = Number(id);
515:   return rows.find((r) => r && Number(r.id) === n) || null;
516: }
517: 
518: /**
519:  * Wave 1 — boot hydration: replace store domain collections with PG truth.
520:  * Iterates SCHEMA_TABLES (not store keys) so a skeleton boot — whose store has
521:  * no domain keys yet — is POPULATED from PG, not left empty. Only SCHEMA_TABLES
522:  * members are ever touched, so cache-only collections (outbox, tombstones,
523:  * __deleted_records, __* internals) keep their store copies. Per-table
524:  * try/catch: one bad table warns and keeps going (single reads still route to
525:  * PG when live, so boot stays safe).
526:  * @param {Object} store - live in-memory store object (mutated in place)
527:  * @returns {Promise<{ok:boolean, hydrated:number, skipped:Array}>}
528:  */
529: async function hydrateStoreFromPg(store, opts) {
530:   const out = { ok: true, hydrated: 0, skipped: [], capped: [], env_skipped: [], kept: [], mirror_incomplete: false };
531:   /* B7 (Phase-2 remediation directive): an empty PG table must never wipe a
532:      non-empty in-memory collection — unless the caller explicitly forces it
533:      (opts.force === true or PAYESH_FORCE_HYDRATION=1). Protection against
534:      hydration data loss on a fresh/empty database. */
535:   const FORCE = !!((opts && opts.force === true) || process.env.PAYESH_FORCE_HYDRATION === '1');
536:   if (!store || typeof store !== 'object') return out;
537:   /* Wave 18 — هیدراتاسیونِ مقیّد (مانورِ بارِ ملی): در مقیاسِ ملی، بارگذاریِ
538:      کلِ جدول‌ها در RAM ممکن نیست (کاربران ۱۰M ⇒ چند GB شیءِ JS؛ OOM در بوت).
539:      دو env اختیاری، هر دو پیش‌فرض خاموش (رفتارِ فعلی حفظ می‌شود):
540:        PAYESH_PG_HYDRATE_SKIP=t1,t2      — این جدول‌ها اصلاً هیدراته نشوند
541:        PAYESH_PG_HYDRATE_LIMIT=u:5000    — سقفِ سطرِ per-جدول (ORDER BY id)
542:      مسیرهایِ خواندنِ زنده (pgLive ⇒ db.readCollection/executePagedList)
543:      همچنان مستقیم از PG می‌خوانند؛ سقف فقط «آینهٔ درون‌حافظه‌ایِ بوت» را
544:      مقیّد می‌کند. یافتهٔ مانور: auth فعلاً از همین آینه می‌خواند (اسکنِ
545:      خطیِ store.users) — در مقیاسِ واقعی باید به جست‌وجویِ ایندکس‌دارِ PG
546:      برود (users.phone ایندکس ندارد). */
547:   const skipEnv = String(process.env.PAYESH_PG_HYDRATE_SKIP || '')
548:     .split(',').map((s) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
549:   const limEnv = {};
550:   String(process.env.PAYESH_PG_HYDRATE_LIMIT || '')
551:     .split(',').map((s) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean).forEach((p) => {
552:       const i = p.indexOf(':');
553:       if (i > 0) {
554:         const t = p.slice(0, i).trim(), n = Number(p.slice(i + 1));
555:         if (t && Number.isFinite(n) && n >= 0) limEnv[t] = n;
556:       }
557:     });
558:   for (const key of SCHEMA_TABLES) {
559:     if (!isPgReadableTable(key)) continue;
560:     if (skipEnv.indexOf(key) > -1) { out.env_skipped.push(key); continue; }
561:     try {
562:       const cap = limEnv[key];
563:       let rows;
564:       if (cap !== undefined) {
565:         const res = await pool.query(`SELECT * FROM "${key}" ORDER BY id LIMIT $1`, [cap]);
566:         rows = reviveRows(res.rows);
567:       } else {
568:         rows = await readCollection(key);
569:       }
570:       /* B7 rule: empty PG + non-empty memory ⇒ keep memory (no destructive
571:          overwrite). Audited via out.kept so operators can see it. */
572:       if (!FORCE && Array.isArray(rows) && rows.length === 0
573:           && Array.isArray(store[key]) && store[key].length > 0) {
574:         out.kept.push(key);
575:         continue;
576:       }
577:       store[key] = rows;
578:       if (cap !== undefined) out.capped.push(key + ':' + cap);
579:       out.hydrated++;
580:     } catch (e) {
581:       out.skipped.push(key);
582:       console.warn('[DB] Hydration skipped for ' + key + ':', e.message);
583:     }
584:   }
585:   /* بازخوردِ بازبینِ PR #94: آینهٔ سقف‌دار/ناقص هرگز نباید روی فایلِ
586:      store.json نوشته شود — فایلِ کاملِ قبلی را می‌کُشد. این پرچم به
587:      index.js می‌گوید مسیرهای persist فایل را در PG-live ببندد. */
588:   out.mirror_incomplete = out.capped.length > 0 || out.env_skipped.length > 0;
589:   return out;
590: }
591: 
592: /**
593:  * آیا آینهٔ درون‌حافظه‌ای را باید روی store.json نوشت؟
594:  * خالث/خالص — قابلِ تستِ مستقیم (tests/wave18-hydration-guards.js).
595:  * فقط وقتی «نه» می‌گوید که PG مرجع است و هیدراتاسیون عمداً بریده
596:  * بوده (capped/env-skipped). هر حالتِ دیگر — از جمله آینهٔ کامل و
597:  * حالتِ بدونِ PG — رفتارِ قبلی (نوشتن) را حفظ می‌کند.
598:  */
599: function shouldPersistMirrorFile(pgLive, hydrateResult){
600:   return !(pgLive && hydrateResult && hydrateResult.mirror_incomplete);
601: }
602: 
603: /**
604:  * آیا سقفِ هیدراتاسیون جدولِ users را بریده؟ (هشدارِ بوت: کاربرانِ
605:  * بیرونِ سقف با auth مبتنی بر آینه نمی‌توانند وارد شوند — فقط برای
606:  * محیط‌های آزمونِ بار معنا دارد.)
607:  */
608: function hydrationUsersCapped(h){
609:   return !!(h && Array.isArray(h.capped) && h.capped.some(function(s){
610:     return String(s).split(':')[0] === 'users';
611:   }));
612: }
613: 
614: /* F1 (chaos-drill #185 — بحرانی): «PG انتظار می‌رود؟»
615:    در production با DATABASE_URL ست، اگر isPgActive وسطِ اجرا false شود
616:    (قطعِ PG + reconnectِ ناموفق)، هیچ مسیری حق ندارد memory را «سالم»
617:    جا بزند — وگرنه readiness سبزِ دروغ می‌شود و ackهای 200 پس از بازگشتِ
618:    PG در آن نیستند (گم‌شدنِ دائمیِ دادهٔ ackشده؛ شاهد: chaos-drill-pg-outage). */
619: function pgExpected() {
620:   return !memoryFallbackAllowed() && !!(process.env.DATABASE_URL);
621: }
622: 
623: /**
624:  * Quick ping for health probes & readiness checks
625:  */
626: async function ping() {
627:   if (!isPostgres()) {
628:     if (pgExpected()) {
629:       return { ok: false, driver: 'none', alive: false,
630:         error: 'PostgreSQL expected in production (DATABASE_URL set) but not connected — refusing memory driver' };
631:     }
632:     return { ok: true, driver: 'memory', alive: true };
633:   }
634:   try {
635:     const res = await pool.query('SELECT 1 AS ping');
636:     return { ok: true, driver: 'postgres', alive: res.rows && res.rows[0] && res.rows[0].ping === 1 };
637:   } catch (err) {
638:     return { ok: false, driver: 'postgres', alive: false, error: err.message };
639:   }
640: }
641: 
642: /**
643:  * Execute a function within an atomic PostgreSQL transaction
644:  * @param {Function} callback - async (client) => { ... }
645:  */
646: async function transaction(callback) {
647:   if (!isPostgres()) {
648:     return await callback(null);
649:   }
650: 
651:   const start = process.hrtime.bigint();
652:   const client = await pool.connect();
653:   try {
654:     await client.query('BEGIN');
655:     const result = await callback(client);
656:     await client.query('COMMIT');
657:     metrics.observeDb('transaction', 'primary', dbSeconds(start), false, dbSlow(start));
658:     return result;
659:   } catch (err) {
660:     metrics.observeDb('transaction', 'primary', dbSeconds(start), true, false);
661:     try { await client.query('ROLLBACK'); }
662:     catch (rbErr) { console.error('[DB] ROLLBACK failed:', rbErr.message); }   /* P1-14: خطایِ rollback نباید خطایِ اصلی را بپوشاند */
663:     throw err;
664:   } finally {
665:     client.release();
666:   }
667: }
668: 
669: /* Wave 10 (پارتیشن‌بندی): فهرستِ جدول‌هایِ پارتیشن‌شده — PAYESH_PARTITIONED_TABLES
670:    (مثلاً "grades,attendance"). خالی ⇒ همه‌چیز مثلِ قبل (ON CONFLICT (id)).
671:    سوییچِ صریح تا rollout تدریجی و rollback لحظه‌ای ممکن باشد. */
672: function isPartitionedTable(name) {
673:   const v = String(process.env.PAYESH_PARTITIONED_TABLES || '');
674:   if (!v) return false;
675:   return v.split(',').map((x) => x.trim()).filter(Boolean).includes(String(name));
676: }
677: 
678: /**
679:  * P1-14: persist ONE op on a given client — THROWS on error (for use inside transactions).
680:  * Semantics mirror the old persistOp: empty-data ins/upd is a no-op; uid tracking is
681:  * best-effort (silent); del with no id skips the DELETE but still tracks the uid.
682:  */
683: async function persistOpWithClient(client, op) {
684:   const col = op.c;
685:   const t = op.t;
686:   const data = op.data || {};
687:   const ident = (name) => {
688:     if(!/^[a-zA-Z_][a-zA-Z0-9_]*$/.test(String(name || ''))) throw new Error('unsafe SQL identifier: ' + name);
689:     return '"' + name + '"';
690:   };
691:   const table = ident(col);
692:   const valOf = (v) => (v !== null && typeof v === 'object') ? JSON.stringify(v) : v;
693: 
694:   if (t === 'ins') {
695:     const fields = Object.keys(data);
696:     if (fields.length === 0) return;
697: 
698:     const cols = fields.map(ident).join(', ');
699:     const placeholders = fields.map((_, i) => `$${i + 1}`).join(', ');
700:     const values = fields.map(f => valOf(data[f]));
701:     const updateSet = fields
702:       .filter(f => f !== 'id')
703:       .map(f => `${ident(f)} = EXCLUDED.${ident(f)}`)
704:       .join(', ');
705: 
706:     /* Wave 10 (پارتیشن‌بندی): جدولِ پارتیشن‌شده PK (id, created_at) دارد —
707:        ON CONFLICT (id) دیگر به هیچ uniqueای نمی‌خورد و فرمِ (id, created_at)
708:        هم idempotency را می‌شکند (id تکراری با created_at متفاوت ⇒ سطرِ
709:        دوم). معادلِ دقیقِ upsert: اول UPDATE به id (ایندکسِ غیر یکتایِ id)؛
710:        اگر هیچ سطری نداشت INSERT؛ رقابتِ هم‌زمان با 23505 گرفته می‌شود و
711:        UPDATE دوباره می‌زند — «آخرین برنده»، همان ترتیبِ DO UPDATE. */
712:     if (isPartitionedTable(col)) {
713:       const upFields = fields.filter(f => f !== 'id');
714:       const hasId = data.id != null && Number.isFinite(Number(data.id));
715:       if (hasId && upFields.length > 0) {
716:         const setSql = upFields.map((f, i) => `${ident(f)} = $${i + 1}`).join(', ');
717:         const upVals = upFields.map(f => valOf(data[f]));
718:         const updSql = `UPDATE ${table} SET ${setSql} WHERE id = $${upFields.length + 1};`;
719:         const updRes = await client.query(updSql, upVals.concat([Number(data.id)]));
720:         if (updRes && Number(updRes.rowCount) > 0) return; /* سطرِ موجود ⇒ به‌روزرسانی شد */
721:       }
722:       const insSql = `INSERT INTO ${table} (${cols}) VALUES (${placeholders});`;
723:       try {
724:         await client.query(insSql, values);
725:       } catch (err) {
726:         /* رقابت: کسِ دیگری همین id را همین لحظه درج کرد — مثلِ ON CONFLICT
727:            DO UPDATE، ما برندهٔ نهایی هستیم: UPDATE دوباره. */
728:         if (err && err.code === '23505' && hasId && upFields.length > 0) {
729:           const setSql = upFields.map((f, i) => `${ident(f)} = $${i + 1}`).join(', ');
730:           const upVals = upFields.map(f => valOf(data[f]));
731:           const updSql = `UPDATE ${table} SET ${setSql} WHERE id = $${upFields.length + 1};`;
732:           const updRes = await client.query(updSql, upVals.concat([Number(data.id)]));
733:           if (!updRes || !(Number(updRes.rowCount) > 0)) throw err;
734:         } else {
735:           throw err;
736:         }
737:       }
738:       return;
739:     }
740: 
741:     const sql = `INSERT INTO ${table} (${cols}) VALUES (${placeholders}) ON CONFLICT (id) DO UPDATE SET ${updateSet};`;
742:     await client.query(sql, values);
743:   } else if (t === 'upd') {
744:     const fields = Object.keys(data).filter(f => f !== 'id');
745:     if (fields.length === 0) return;
746:     const id = Number(op.id != null ? op.id : data.id);
747:     if (!Number.isFinite(id) || id <= 0) throw new Error('missing id for update op');
748: 
749:     const values = fields.map(f => valOf(data[f]));
750:     // ADR-013: Universal Runtime OCC & Zero Naked Update Invariant
751:     const hasExplicitVersion = fields.includes('version');
752:     const versionClause = hasExplicitVersion ? '' : ', version = COALESCE(version, 1) + 1';
753:     const setSql = fields.map((f, i) => `${ident(f)} = $${i + 1}`).join(', ') + versionClause;
754: 
755:     if (op.base_version != null) {
756:       const base = Number(op.base_version);
757:       if (!Number.isInteger(base) || base < 1) {
758:         const e = new Error('bad base_version');
759:         e.code = 'bad_base_version';
760:         throw e;
761:       }
762:       const res = await client.query(
763:         `UPDATE ${table} SET ${setSql} WHERE id = $${fields.length + 1} AND version = $${fields.length + 2};`,
764:         values.concat([id, base])
765:       );
766:       if (res && res.rowCount === 0) {
767:         const e = new Error('optimistic concurrency conflict');
768:         e.code = 'occ_conflict';
769:         e.status = 409;
770:         throw e;
771:       }
772:     } else {
773:       if (process.env.PAYESH_STRICT_OCC === '1') {
774:         const e = new Error('optimistic concurrency conflict: missing required base_version');
775:         e.code = 'missing_base_version';
776:         e.status = 409;
777:         throw e;
778:       }
779:       await client.query(`UPDATE ${table} SET ${setSql} WHERE id = $${fields.length + 1};`, values.concat([id]));
780:     }
781:   } else if (t === 'del') {
782:     const delId = Number(op.id != null ? op.id : (data && data.id));
783:     if (delId) {
784:       await client.query(`DELETE FROM ${table} WHERE id = $1;`, [delId]);
785:     }
786:   }
787: 
788:   // Also persist processed UID into server_processed_uids (best-effort, as before)
789:   if (op.uid) {
790:     await client.query(
791:       `INSERT INTO server_processed_uids (uid, processed_at) VALUES ($1, NOW()) ON CONFLICT (uid) DO NOTHING;`,
792:       [op.uid]
793:     ).catch(() => {});
794:   }
795: }
796: 
797: /**
798:  * Persist an applied sync operation to PostgreSQL (single-op; failures are logged, never thrown)
799:  */
800: async function persistOp(op) {
801:   if (!isPostgres() || !op || !op.c) return;
802: 
803:   const col = op.c;
804:   const t = op.t;
805: 
806:   try {
807:     await persistOpWithClient({ query: (text, params) => pool.query(text, params) }, op);
808:   } catch (err) {
809:     console.error(`[DB] Error persisting sync op to PostgreSQL (${col}.${t}):`, err.message);
810:   }
811: }
812: 
813: /**
814:  * P1-14: persist applied ops on a given client, in order — THROWS on the first
815:  * failure so the caller's transaction rolls everything back.
816:  */
817: async function persistOpsBatchWithClient(client, ops) {
818:   let n = 0;
819:   for (const op of (ops || [])) {
820:     if (!op || !op.c) continue;
821:     await persistOpWithClient(client, op);
822:     n++;
823:   }
824:   return { ok: true, count: n };
825: }
826: 
827: /**
828:  * P1-14: atomic multi-record mirror — all ops in ONE transaction (all-or-nothing).
