829:  * Memory fallback: PG mirror is skipped (the JSON store is the source of truth there).
830:  * THROWS on failure (after ROLLBACK) so the caller can audit / mark for retry.
831:  */
832: async function persistOpsBatch(ops) {
833:   const list = ops || [];
834:   if (!isPostgres()) {
835:     /* F1 (chaos-drill #185): در production با DATABASE_URL، skipِ بی‌صدایِ
836:        آینه = ackِ 200ی که هرگز به PG نمی‌رسد ⇒ THROW تا sync.js همان مسیرِ
837:        رسمیِ sync_mirror_failed/503 + rollback را برود (کلاینت retry می‌کند). */
838:     if (pgExpected()) {
839:       throw new Error('PostgreSQL expected in production but not connected — refusing silent memory ack (F1)');
840:     }
841:     return { ok: true, driver: 'memory', count: list.length };
842:   }
843:   return await transaction(async (client) => {
844:     const r = await persistOpsBatchWithClient(client, list);
845:     r.driver = 'postgres';
846:     return r;
847:   });
848: }
849: 
850: /**
851:  * Check if a UID has been processed (PostgreSQL or memory store)
852:  */
853: async function isUidProcessed(uid) {
854:   if (isPostgres()) {
855:     try {
856:       const res = await pool.query('SELECT 1 FROM server_processed_uids WHERE uid = $1', [uid]);
857:       if (res.rowCount > 0) return true;
858:     } catch (e) {
859:       /* P0-1 (round 3, item 3) — this used to be an empty catch, which silently
860:          downgraded a PostgreSQL failure to the per-process JSON store. That is
861:          an idempotency hole: a sync uid whose "already applied?" check could
862:          not be answered was treated as *not* applied, so the same op could be
863:          applied twice across instances. In production we now fail closed; the
864:          sync route turns the throw into a clean 500 (server/index.js request
865:          wrapper) / 503, and the client retries. Dev/test keeps the old
866:          warn-and-continue behaviour so local offline runs still work. */
867:       if (!memoryFallbackAllowed()) {
868:         throw new Error('isUidProcessed: PostgreSQL idempotency check failed in production (refusing to fall back to the per-process store): '
869:           + ((e && e.message) || e));
870:       }
871:       console.warn('[DB] isUidProcessed: PostgreSQL query failed, using the in-memory store (dev/test only):', (e && e.message) || e);
872:     }
873:   }
874:   if (memoryStore && memoryStore.__processed_uids) {
875:     return !!memoryStore.__processed_uids[uid];
876:   }
877:   return false;
878: }
879: 
880: /**
881:  * Detailed Health check status
882:  */
883: async function healthCheck() {
884:   if (!isPostgres()) {
885:     return { ok: true, driver: 'memory', message: 'In-memory JSON store operational' };
886:   }
887: 
888:   const start = Date.now();
889:   try {
890:     await pool.query('SELECT 1');
891:     const latency = Date.now() - start;
892:     const out = {
893:       ok: true,
894:       driver: 'postgres',
895:       latency_ms: latency,
896:       total_count: pool.totalCount,
897:       idle_count: pool.idleCount,
898:       waiting_count: pool.waitingCount
899:     };
900:     /* Wave 10 — read-replica observability (present only when configured+live) */
901:     out.read_replica = isReplicaActive() ? {
902:       active: true,
903:       total_count: readPool.totalCount,
904:       idle_count: readPool.idleCount,
905:       waiting_count: readPool.waitingCount
906:     } : { active: false };
907:     return out;
908:   } catch (err) {
909:     return { ok: false, driver: 'postgres', error: err.message };
910:   }
911: }
912: 
913: /**
914:  * Wave 10 — concise pool-summary for /health-style introspection: primary +
915:  * (optional) read replica, their current lease counts, and replica routing flag.
916:  */
917: function poolStats() {
918:   const stats = {
919:     driver: isPostgres() ? 'postgres' : 'memory',
920:     primary: {
921:       active: isPostgres(),
922:       min: config.min,
923:       max: config.max,
924:       total_count: pool ? pool.totalCount : 0,
925:       idle_count: pool ? pool.idleCount : 0,
926:       waiting_count: pool ? pool.waitingCount : 0
927:     },
928:     read_replica: isReplicaActive() ? {
929:       active: true,
930:       min: config.readMin,
931:       max: config.readMax,
932:       total_count: readPool.totalCount,
933:       idle_count: readPool.idleCount,
934:       waiting_count: readPool.waitingCount
935:     } : { active: false }
936:   };
937:   stats.routing_reads_to_replica = isReplicaActive();   /* queryRead() target */
938:   return stats;
939: }
940: 
941: /**
942:  * Graceful shutdown
943:  */
944: async function close() {
945:   if (reconnectTimer) {
946:     clearTimeout(reconnectTimer);
947:     reconnectTimer = null;
948:   }
949:   if (replicaReprobeTimer) {
950:     clearTimeout(replicaReprobeTimer);
951:     replicaReprobeTimer = null;
952:   }
953:   if (pool) {
954:     try {
955:       await pool.end();
956:     } catch (e) {}
957:     pool = null;
958:     isPgActive = false;
959:   }
960:   /* Wave 10 — tear down the read replica too */
961:   if (readPool) {
962:     try {
963:       await readPool.end();
964:     } catch (e) {}
965:     readPool = null;
966:     readPoolActive = false;
967:   }
968: }
969: 
970: /* P1-14: seam تزریقِ pool برای تستِ اتمی‌بودن بدون PG واقعی (pg-mem).
971:    فقط تست از آن استفاده می‌کند؛ کدِ اجرایی همیشه از init می‌آید. */
972: function __setPoolForTests(p) {
973:   if (p) { pool = p; isPgActive = true; }
974:   else { pool = null; isPgActive = false; }
975: }
976: 
977: /* Wave 10 — same injection seam for the read-replica pool (fake-db tests only) */
978: function __setReadPoolForTests(p) {
979:   if (p) { readPool = p; readPoolActive = true; }
980:   else { readPool = null; readPoolActive = false; }
981: }
982: 
983: /* S3-1: درزِ تأخیرِ کاوش برایِ تست (پیش‌فرضِ اجرایی ۱۰۰۰۰ms دست‌نخورده) */
984: function __setReprobeDelayForTests(ms) {
985:   const v = Number(ms);
986:   if (Number.isFinite(v) && v >= 0) REPLICA_REPROBE_MS = v;
987:   return REPLICA_REPROBE_MS;
988: }
989: 
990: module.exports = {
991:   init,
992:   isPostgres,
993:   /* P0-1 (Package 1) — production backing-store policy */
994:   isProductionEnv,
995:   pgExpected,
996:   memoryFallbackAllowed,
997:   memoryFallbackRequested,
998:   backingStorePolicy,
999:   /* round 3, item 2 — observed pg Client errors (crash-prevention counter) */
1000:   clientErrors,
1001:   shouldPersistMirrorFile,
1002:   hydrationUsersCapped,
1003:   getPool,
1004:   isReplicaActive,
1005:   getReadPool,
1006:   query,
1007:   queryRead,
1008:   readCollection,
1009:   readOne,
1010:   hydrateStoreFromPg,
1011:   ping,
1012:   transaction,
1013:   persistOp,
1014:   persistOpWithClient,
1015:   persistOpsBatchWithClient,
1016:   persistOpsBatch,
1017:   __setPoolForTests,
1018:   /* Wave 10 (chg_id): کنارگذاریِ ستون‌های داخلی برای خواننده‌های بیرونی (pull/delta) */
1019:   stripInternalColumns,
1020:   /* Wave 10 (پارتیشن‌بندی): آیا این جدول در PAYESH_PARTITIONED_TABLES است */
1021:   isPartitionedTable,
1022:   __setReadPoolForTests,
1023:   __setReprobeDelayForTests,
1024:   isUidProcessed,
1025:   healthCheck,
1026:   poolStats,
1027:   close
1028: };

### server/worker.js
1: /* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
2:    server/worker.js — ویو ۸: کارگرِ پردازشِ صندوق رویدادها (Outbox)
3:    ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
4:    - رویدادهای `pending` را از `store.outbox` می‌خواند و به هندلرِ
5:      ثبت‌شده برای `type` آن‌ها می‌سپارد.
6:    - موفقیت ⇒ `status='processed'` + `processed_at`.
7:    - شکست ⇒ `retry_count++` و `last_error`؛ پس از `maxRetries` تلاش
8:      ⇒ `status='failed'`. رویداد در شکست **هرگز حذف نمی‌شود** — برای
9:      بازرسی و تلاشِ دستی باقی می‌ماند (داده نمی‌میرد).
10:    - رویدادهای بدون هندلر دست نمی‌خورند (کارِ مصرف‌کننده‌های دیگرند).
11:    - در برابر ورودِ دوباره محافظت شده (یک رویداد هم‌زمان دو بار پردازش
12:      نمی‌شود) و برای تستِ قطعی `tick()` به‌صورت دستی هم قابل صداست.
13:    - زمان‌بندی: `intervalMs` (پیش‌فرض ۱۰۰۰) — تلاشِ بعدی در تیکِ بعد
14:      (بک‌آف ساده؛ برای بارهای سنگین صفِ اختصاصی جدا شود).
15:    ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
16: 'use strict';
17: 
18: /* ویو ۱۴ (Observability) — نتیجهٔ پردازشِ هر رویداد به‌صورت metric.
19:    metrics.js هرگز خطا نمی‌دهد (R1)، پس رفتارِ کارگر تغییر نمی‌کند. */
20: const metrics = require('./metrics');
21: 
22: /**
23:  * @param {object} opts
24:  * @param {object} opts.store      — فروشگاه (آرایهٔ `store.outbox` منبع صف است)
25:  * @param {object} opts.outbox     — نمونهٔ ساخته‌شده از server/outbox (برای mark)
26:  * @param {object} opts.handlers   — نگاشتِ `type → async (evt) => {}`؛ کلیدِ
27:  *                                   ویژهٔ '*.deleted' برای همهٔ رویدادهای حذف می‌خورد.
28:  * @param {number} [opts.intervalMs=1000]
29:  * @param {number} [opts.maxRetries=5]
30:  */
31: function createWorker({ store, outbox, handlers, intervalMs, maxRetries }) {
32:   handlers = handlers || {};
33:   intervalMs = Number(intervalMs) > 0 ? Number(intervalMs) : 1000;
34:   maxRetries = Number.isFinite(Number(maxRetries)) ? Number(maxRetries) : 5;
35: 
36:   let timer = null;
37:   let running = false;
38:   let lastTickAt = 0;
39:   const inFlight = new Set();
40: 
41:   function handlerFor(evt) {
42:     if (!evt || !evt.type) return null;
43:     if (handlers[evt.type]) return handlers[evt.type];
44:     if (String(evt.type).slice(-8) === '.deleted' && handlers['*.deleted']) return handlers['*.deleted'];
45:     return null;
46:   }
47: 
48:   /* یک دور پردازش — برای تست به‌صورت دستی هم صدا زده می‌شود */
49:   async function tick() {
50:     if (running) return { processed: 0, failedDelta: 0, skippedBusy: true };
51:     running = true;
52:     lastTickAt = Date.now();
53:     let processed = 0, failedDelta = 0;
54:     try {
55:       let events = [];
56:       if (outbox && typeof outbox.fetchPendingBatch === 'function') {
57:         try {
58:           events = await outbox.fetchPendingBatch(50);
59:         } catch (_) {
60:           events = Array.isArray(store.outbox) ? store.outbox : [];
61:         }
62:       } else {
63:         events = Array.isArray(store.outbox) ? store.outbox : [];
64:       }
65: 
66:       for (const evt of events) {
67:         const status = evt.status || 'pending'; /* سازگاری با گذشته */
68:         if ((status !== 'pending' && status !== 'processing') || inFlight.has(evt.id)) continue;
69:         const h = handlerFor(evt);
70:         if (!h) continue; /* این رویداد کارِ این کارگر نیست */
71:         inFlight.add(evt.id);
72:         try {
73:           await h(evt);
74:           await outbox.mark(evt.id, {
75:             status: 'processed',
76:             processed_at: new Date().toISOString(),
77:             last_error: null
78:           });
79:           processed++;
80:           metrics.inc('payesh_worker_events_total', { outcome: 'processed' });
81:         } catch (err) {
82:           const rc = (Number(evt.retry_count) || 0) + 1;
83:           const errMsg = (err && (err.message || err.code)) || 'error';
84:           const patch = {
85:             status: rc >= maxRetries ? 'failed' : 'pending',
86:             retry_count: rc,
87:             last_error: errMsg
88:           };
89:           if (rc >= maxRetries) {
90:             failedDelta++;
91:             // B4: Transfer poison pill event to Dead-Letter Queue (DLQ)
92:             if (outbox && typeof outbox.moveToDlq === 'function') {
93:               try {
94:                 await outbox.moveToDlq(evt, errMsg);
95:               } catch (_) {}
96:             }
97:           }
98:           await outbox.mark(evt.id, patch);
99:           /* برچسب از مجموعهٔ بسته (retry/failed)؛ متن خطا هرگز label نیست. */
100:           metrics.inc('payesh_worker_events_total', { outcome: patch.status === 'failed' ? 'failed' : 'retry' });
101:         } finally {
102:           inFlight.delete(evt.id);
103:         }
104:       }
105:       return { processed, failedDelta };
106:     } finally {
107:       running = false;
108:     }
109:   }
110: 
111:   function start() {
112:     if (timer) return;
113:     lastTickAt = Date.now();
114:     timer = setInterval(() => { tick().catch(() => {}); }, intervalMs);
115:     if (timer.unref) timer.unref();
116:   }
117: 
118:   function stop() {
119:     if (timer) { clearInterval(timer); timer = null; }
120:   }
121: 
122:   function isHealthy(staleThresholdMs = 30000) {
123:     if (!timer) return true; // idle or manual mode
124:     if (!lastTickAt) return true;
125:     const age = Date.now() - lastTickAt;
126:     return age <= staleThresholdMs;
127:   }
128: 
129:   function health() {
130:     return {
131:       running,
132:       active: timer !== null,
133:       lastTickAt: lastTickAt ? new Date(lastTickAt).toISOString() : null,
134:       lastTickAgeMs: lastTickAt ? Date.now() - lastTickAt : 0,
135:       healthy: isHealthy()
136:     };
137:   }
138: 
139:   return { start, stop, tick, isHealthy, health };
140: }
141: 
142: module.exports = { createWorker };

### server/metrics.js
1: /* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
2:    server/metrics.js — Wave 14 (Observability): metrics signal
3:    ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
4:    A dependency-free metrics registry (Node stdlib only) that exposes
5:    the Prometheus text exposition format 0.0.4 on `GET /metrics`.
6: 
7:    Why not prom-client / OTel Metrics SDK?
8:      - the server's contract is "Runtime deps: Node stdlib only"
9:        (server/index.js header); tracing already pulls OTel for the
10:        *trace* signal, and adding a second metrics pipeline would give
11:        us two sources of truth for the same numbers.
12:      - the exposition format is ~40 lines of code; the value of this
13:        module is the *discipline* around it (bounded cardinality,
14:        label redaction, fail-closed scraping), not the format writer.
15: 
16:    Design rules (all enforced by tests/wave14-observability.js):
17:      R1  Observability NEVER breaks the request path. Every recorder
18:          swallows its own errors and counts them instead of throwing.
19:      R2  Bounded cardinality. A metric has a hard cap on label-series
20:          (PAYESH_METRICS_MAX_SERIES, default 1024). Overflow is DROPPED
21:          and counted in payesh_metrics_dropped_series_total — a hostile
22:          or buggy caller can never grow the registry without bound.
23:      R3  No PII / no raw URLs in labels. Paths are reduced to a closed
24:          allowlist of route templates by routeTemplate(); anything
25:          unmatched collapses to "api_unmatched" / "static_other".
26:          Free-form strings (SQL text, phone, nid, ip) are never labels.
27:      R4  Fail-closed scraping. In production /metrics requires a bearer
28:          token; without one the endpoint does not exist (404).
29:      R5  Labels are declared per metric. An unknown label key or a bad
30:          metric/label name drops the observation (counted), never throws.
31:    ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
32: 'use strict';
33: 
34: const crypto = require('crypto');
35: 
36: const METRIC_NAME_RE = /^[a-zA-Z_:][a-zA-Z0-9_:]*$/;
37: const LABEL_NAME_RE = /^[a-zA-Z_][a-zA-Z0-9_]*$/;
38: 
39: /* Prometheus default buckets, in seconds — good for HTTP/DB round trips. */
40: const DEFAULT_BUCKETS = [0.005, 0.01, 0.025, 0.05, 0.1, 0.25, 0.5, 1, 2.5, 5, 10];
41: 
42: function clampInt(v, dflt, min, max) {
43:   const n = Number(v);
44:   if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return dflt;
45:   return Math.min(max, Math.max(min, Math.trunc(n)));
46: }
47: 
48: /* ── label-value / help escaping (exposition format) ───────────────── */
49: function escLabelValue(v) {
50:   return String(v == null ? '' : v)
51:     .replace(/\\/g, '\\\\')
52:     .replace(/\n/g, '\\n')
53:     .replace(/"/g, '\\"');
54: }
55: function escHelp(h) {
56:   return String(h || '').replace(/\\/g, '\\\\').replace(/\n/g, '\\n');
57: }
58: 
59: /* ── registry ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
60: function createRegistry(opts) {
61:   const options = opts || {};
62:   const maxSeries = clampInt(options.maxSeries, 1024, 8, 100000);
63:   const metrics = new Map();          /* name -> metric record */
64:   const dropCounts = new Map();       /* `${name}|${reason}` -> count */
65: 
66:   function bumpDrop(name, reason) {
67:     const k = name + '|' + reason;
68:     dropCounts.set(k, (dropCounts.get(k) || 0) + 1);
69:   }
70: 
71:   function droppedSeriesTotal() {
72:     let t = 0;
73:     for (const [k, v] of dropCounts) if (k.indexOf('|series_overflow') > 0) t += v;
74:     return t;
75:   }
76: 
77:   /* Validate + normalize a label set against the metric's declared keys.
78:      Returns null when the observation must be dropped (R5). */
79:   function normalize(m, labels) {
80:     const names = m.labelNames;
81:     const out = new Array(names.length);
82:     const src = labels || {};
83:     if (typeof src !== 'object') return null;
84:     for (let i = 0; i < names.length; i++) {
85:       const v = src[names[i]];
86:       out[i] = (v == null ? '' : String(v));
87:     }
88:     /* unknown label key → drop (fail-closed, R5) */
89:     for (const k of Object.keys(src)) {
90:       if (names.indexOf(k) < 0) { bumpDrop(m.name, 'unknown_label'); return null; }
91:     }
92:     return out;
93:   }
94: 
95:   function seriesKey(vals) { return vals.join('\u0000'); }
96: 
97:   function declare(type, name, help, labelNames, buckets) {
98:     if (typeof name !== 'string' || !METRIC_NAME_RE.test(name)) {
99:       bumpDrop(String(name), 'bad_metric_name');
100:       return null;
101:     }
102:     const existing = metrics.get(name);
103:     if (existing) return existing;
104:     const labels = (labelNames || []).filter((n) => typeof n === 'string' && LABEL_NAME_RE.test(n) && n.indexOf('__') !== 0);
105:     if ((labelNames || []).length !== labels.length) bumpDrop(name, 'bad_label_name');
106:     const m = {
107:       type,
108:       name,
109:       help: help || '',
110:       labelNames: labels,
111:       series: new Map(),
112:       buckets: type === 'histogram' ? (buckets || DEFAULT_BUCKETS).slice().sort((a, b) => a - b) : null
113:     };
114:     metrics.set(name, m);
115:     return m;
116:   }
117: 
118:   function getSeries(m, vals, create) {
119:     const key = seriesKey(vals);
120:     let s = m.series.get(key);
121:     if (s) return s;
122:     if (!create) return null;
123:     if (m.series.size >= maxSeries) { bumpDrop(m.name, 'series_overflow'); return null; }
124:     s = m.type === 'histogram'
125:       ? { values: vals, counts: new Array(m.buckets.length).fill(0), count: 0, sum: 0 }
126:       : { values: vals, value: 0 };
127:     m.series.set(key, s);
128:     return s;
129:   }
130: 
131:   /* ── public recorders ──────────────────────────────────────────── */
132:   function counter(name, help, labelNames) { declare('counter', name, help, labelNames); return api; }
133:   function gauge(name, help, labelNames) { declare('gauge', name, help, labelNames); return api; }
134:   function histogram(name, help, labelNames, buckets) { declare('histogram', name, help, labelNames, buckets); return api; }
135: 
136:   function inc(name, labels, by) {
137:     try {
138:       const m = metrics.get(name);
139:       if (!m || m.type !== 'counter') { bumpDrop(name, 'not_a_counter'); return; }
140:       const vals = normalize(m, labels);
141:       if (!vals) return;
142:       const s = getSeries(m, vals, true);
143:       if (!s) return;
144:       const d = by == null ? 1 : Number(by);
145:       if (!Number.isFinite(d) || d < 0) { bumpDrop(name, 'bad_value'); return; }
146:       s.value += d;
147:     } catch (e) { bumpDrop(name, 'exception'); }
148:   }
149: 
150:   function set(name, labels, value) {
151:     try {
152:       const m = metrics.get(name);
153:       if (!m || m.type !== 'gauge') { bumpDrop(name, 'not_a_gauge'); return; }
154:       const v = Number(value);
155:       if (!Number.isFinite(v)) { bumpDrop(name, 'bad_value'); return; }
156:       const vals = normalize(m, labels);
157:       if (!vals) return;
158:       const s = getSeries(m, vals, true);
159:       if (!s) return;
160:       s.value = v;
161:     } catch (e) { bumpDrop(name, 'exception'); }
162:   }
163: 
164:   function add(name, labels, by) {
165:     try {
166:       const m = metrics.get(name);
167:       if (!m || m.type !== 'gauge') { bumpDrop(name, 'not_a_gauge'); return; }
168:       const d = Number(by);
169:       if (!Number.isFinite(d)) { bumpDrop(name, 'bad_value'); return; }
170:       const vals = normalize(m, labels);
171:       if (!vals) return;
172:       const s = getSeries(m, vals, true);
173:       if (!s) return;
174:       s.value += d;
175:     } catch (e) { bumpDrop(name, 'exception'); }
176:   }
177: 
178:   function observe(name, labels, valueSeconds) {
179:     try {
180:       const m = metrics.get(name);
181:       if (!m || m.type !== 'histogram') { bumpDrop(name, 'not_a_histogram'); return; }
182:       const v = Number(valueSeconds);
183:       if (!Number.isFinite(v) || v < 0) { bumpDrop(name, 'bad_value'); return; }
184:       const vals = normalize(m, labels);
185:       if (!vals) return;
186:       const s = getSeries(m, vals, true);
187:       if (!s) return;
188:       for (let i = 0; i < m.buckets.length; i++) if (v <= m.buckets[i]) s.counts[i]++;
189:       s.count++;
190:       s.sum += v;
191:     } catch (e) { bumpDrop(name, 'exception'); }
192:   }
193: 
194:   /* Timer helper: const done = timer('x'); ...; done({route:'r'}, ms) */
195:   function startTimer() { return process.hrtime.bigint(); }
196:   function elapsedSeconds(startNs) {
197:     try { return Number(process.hrtime.bigint() - startNs) / 1e9; }
198:     catch (e) { return 0; }
199:   }
200: 
201:   /* Prometheus histogram_quantile() equivalent: linear interpolation
202:      inside the bucket that contains the q-th observation. */
203:   function quantile(name, labels, q) {
204:     try {
205:       const m = metrics.get(name);
206:       if (!m || m.type !== 'histogram') return null;
207:       const vals = normalize(m, labels);
208:       if (!vals) return null;
209:       const s = m.series.get(seriesKey(vals));
210:       if (!s || s.count === 0) return null;
211:       const rank = q * s.count;
212:       let prevCount = 0, prevBound = 0;
213:       for (let i = 0; i < m.buckets.length; i++) {
214:         if (s.counts[i] >= rank) {
215:           const bound = m.buckets[i];
216:           const span = bound - prevBound;
217:           const inner = s.counts[i] - prevCount;
218:           if (inner <= 0) return bound;
219:           return prevBound + span * ((rank - prevCount) / inner);
220:         }
221:         prevCount = s.counts[i];
222:         prevBound = m.buckets[i];
223:       }
224:       return m.buckets[m.buckets.length - 1];   /* beyond the last bucket */
225:     } catch (e) { return null; }
226:   }
227: 
228:   function value(name, labels) {
229:     try {
230:       const m = metrics.get(name);
231:       if (!m) return null;
232:       const vals = normalize(m, labels);
233:       if (!vals) return null;
234:       const s = m.series.get(seriesKey(vals));
235:       return s ? s.value : null;
236:     } catch (e) { return null; }
237:   }
238: 
239:   function histSummary(name, labels) {
240:     try {
241:       const m = metrics.get(name);
242:       if (!m || m.type !== 'histogram') return null;
243:       const vals = normalize(m, labels);
244:       if (!vals) return null;
245:       const s = m.series.get(seriesKey(vals));
246:       if (!s) return { count: 0, sum: 0, p50: null, p95: null, p99: null };
247:       return {
248:         count: s.count,
249:         sum: s.sum,
250:         p50: quantile(name, labels, 0.5),
251:         p95: quantile(name, labels, 0.95),
252:         p99: quantile(name, labels, 0.99)
253:       };
254:     } catch (e) { return null; }
255:   }
256: 
257:   /* ── exposition ────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
258:   function render() {
259:     /* Keep the self-monitoring counter in the registry (not synthesized in
260:        text) so it is part of the declared catalogue and shows up in
261:        snapshot()/drift guards like any other metric. */
262:     const ds = metrics.get('payesh_metrics_dropped_series_total');
263:     if (ds) {
264:       const s = getSeries(ds, [], true);
265:       if (s) s.value = droppedSeriesTotal();
266:     }
267:     const out = [];
268:     for (const m of metrics.values()) {
269:       if (m.series.size === 0) continue;
270:       out.push('# HELP ' + m.name + ' ' + escHelp(m.help));
271:       out.push('# TYPE ' + m.name + ' ' + m.type);
272:       for (const s of m.series.values()) {
273:         const lbl = m.labelNames.map((n, i) => n + '="' + escLabelValue(s.values[i]) + '"');
274:         if (m.type === 'histogram') {
275:           for (let i = 0; i < m.buckets.length; i++) {
276:             const all = lbl.concat(['le="' + m.buckets[i] + '"']).join(',');
277:             out.push(m.name + '_bucket{' + all + '} ' + s.counts[i]);
278:           }
279:           const inf = lbl.concat(['le="+Inf"']).join(',');
280:           out.push(m.name + '_bucket{' + inf + '} ' + s.count);
281:           out.push(m.name + '_sum{' + lbl.join(',') + '} ' + s.sum);
282:           out.push(m.name + '_count{' + lbl.join(',') + '} ' + s.count);
283:         } else {
284:           out.push(m.name + (lbl.length ? '{' + lbl.join(',') + '}' : '') + ' ' + s.value);
285:         }
286:       }
287:     }
288:     return out.join('\n') + '\n';
289:   }
290: 
291:   /* Plain-JSON view (tests + /api/health debug), never for scraping. */
292:   function snapshot() {
293:     const o = {};
294:     for (const m of metrics.values()) {
295:       const arr = [];
296:       for (const s of m.series.values()) {
297:         const labels = {};
298:         m.labelNames.forEach((n, i) => { labels[n] = s.values[i]; });
299:         if (m.type === 'histogram') {
300:           arr.push({ labels, count: s.count, sum: s.sum, p50: quantile(m.name, labels, 0.5), p95: quantile(m.name, labels, 0.95), p99: quantile(m.name, labels, 0.99) });
301:         } else {
302:           arr.push({ labels, value: s.value });
303:         }
304:       }
305:       o[m.name] = { type: m.type, help: m.help, series: arr };
306:     }
307:     return o;
308:   }
309: 
310:   function reset() { metrics.clear(); dropCounts.clear(); declareAll(api); declareSelfMonitor(); }
311: 
312:   function stats() {
313:     const o = { max_series_per_metric: maxSeries, dropped: {}, dropped_series_total: droppedSeriesTotal() };
314:     for (const [k, v] of dropCounts) o.dropped[k] = v;
315:     return o;
316:   }
317: 
318:   /* Self-monitoring: the cardinality guard must be observable even in a
319:      brand-new registry, before any recorder has run — otherwise a scrape
320:      cannot tell "guard never fired" from "guard metric missing". */
321:   function declareSelfMonitor() {
322:     declare('counter', 'payesh_metrics_dropped_series_total',
323:       'Observations dropped by the cardinality guard (R2). A non-zero rate is a code bug.', []);
324:   }
325:   declareSelfMonitor();
326: 
327:   const api = {
328:     counter, gauge, histogram,
329:     inc, set, add, observe,
330:     startTimer, elapsedSeconds, quantile, value, histSummary,
331:     render, snapshot, reset, stats,
332:     maxSeries
333:   };
334:   return api;
335: }
336: 
337: /* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
338:    Route templating (R3). Closed allowlist — mirrors server/index.js.
339:    tests/wave14-observability.js T7 asserts the two stay in sync, so a
340:    new /api/ route cannot silently become an "api_unmatched" label.
341:    ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
342: const ROUTE_EXACT = new Set([
343:   '/api/health',
344:   '/api/auth/send-code', '/api/auth/login', '/api/auth/me',
345:   '/api/auth/logout', '/api/auth/delete-account',
346:   '/api/sync', '/api/sync/conflicts', '/api/sync/resolve-conflict',
347:   '/api/bell/now', '/api/public-report',
348:   '/api/admin/backup', '/api/admin/restore',
349:   '/api/sms/send',
350:   '/api/health-index', /* G.1 (main — PR #31) */
351:   /* Wave 15 (main) — Health/Deployment endpoints */
352:   '/api/liveness', '/api/readiness', '/api/__slow',
353:   '/api/v1/bootstrap', '/api/v1/pull',
354:   '/api/v1/students', '/api/v1/classes', '/api/v1/attendance',
355:   '/api/v1/grades', '/api/v1/users',
356:   /* Wave 23 (f303687) — چهار endpointِ گزارشِ وزارتی (routes/reports.js).
357:      ممیزی دور ۲: این‌ها از allowlist جا مانده بودند ⇒ T7a قرمز و هر
358:      فراخوانی برچسبِ fail-closedِ api_unmatched می‌گرفت (کوریِ سنجه). */
359:   '/api/v1/reports/attendance', '/api/v1/reports/academic',
360:   '/api/v1/reports/finance', '/api/v1/reports/teachers',
361:   /* Canary & System endpoints */
362:   '/api/system/canary/status', '/api/system/canary/promote',
363:   '/api/system/canary/weight', '/api/system/canary/rollback', '/api/system/canary/circuit-breaker',
364:   /* Analytics endpoints */
365:   '/api/v1/analytics/school-intelligence', '/api/v1/analytics/regional-intelligence',
366:   '/api/v1/analytics/quality-governance', '/api/v1/analytics/longitudinal-intelligence',
367:   '/api/v1/analytics/action-recommendations', '/api/v1/analytics/feedback-learning-memory',
368:   '/api/v1/analytics/intelligence-governance', '/api/v1/analytics/policy-simulation',
369:   '/api/v1/analytics/decision-command', '/api/v1/analytics/operational-execution',
370:   '/api/v1/analytics/outcome-evaluation', '/api/v1/analytics/intelligence-platform',
371:   '/api/v1/analytics/intelligence-certification',
372:   /* System Health & Certification endpoints */
373:   '/api/v1/system/scalability-health', '/api/v1/system/event-processing-health',
374:   '/api/v1/system/observability-health', '/api/v1/system/disaster-recovery-health',
375:   '/api/v1/system/pilot-deployment-health', '/api/v1/system/security-health',
376:   '/api/v1/system/phase4-certification', '/api/v1/system/scalability-certification',
377:   /* Phase 5 Federation & Provincial Pilot endpoints */
378:   '/api/v1/system/phase5/regions', '/api/v1/system/phase5/federation-health',
379:   '/api/v1/system/phase5/resource-governance', '/api/v1/system/phase5/pilot-approval',
380:   '/api/v1/system/phase5/provincial-pilots', '/api/v1/system/phase5/provincial-pilots/capacity',
381:   '/api/v1/system/phase5/provincial-pilots/activate', '/api/v1/system/phase5/provincial-pilots/traffic-rollout',
382:   /* National Control Plane endpoints */
383:   '/api/v1/system/national/regions', '/api/v1/system/national/capacity',
384:   '/api/v1/system/national/capacity/reservations', '/api/v1/system/national/capacity/reservation',
385:   '/api/v1/system/national/health', '/api/v1/system/national/traffic',
386:   '/api/v1/system/national/operations', '/api/v1/system/national/readiness',
387:   '/api/v1/system/national/load-test', '/api/v1/system/national/incidents',
388:   '/api/v1/system/national/change-request', '/api/v1/system/national/write-smoothing',
389:   /* Phase 6 Canary endpoints */
390:   '/api/v1/system/phase6/canary/status', '/api/v1/system/phase6/canary/promote',
391:   '/api/v1/system/phase6/canary/weight', '/api/v1/system/phase6/canary/rollback',
392:   '/api/v1/system/phase6/canary/circuit-breaker',
393:   '/metrics'
394: ]);
395: const ROUTE_PATTERNS = [
396:   { re: /^\/api\/students\/\d+$/, route: '/api/students/:id' },
397:   { re: /^\/api\/v1\/students\/\d+$/, route: '/api/v1/students/:id' },
398:   { re: /^\/api\/v1\/classes\/\d+$/, route: '/api/v1/classes/:id' },
399:   { re: /^\/api\/v1\/attendance\/\d+$/, route: '/api/v1/attendance/:id' },
400:   { re: /^\/api\/v1\/grades\/\d+$/, route: '/api/v1/grades/:id' },
401:   { re: /^\/api\/v1\/users\/\d+$/, route: '/api/v1/users/:id' }
402: ];
403: const STATIC_EXACT = new Set([
404:   '/', '/index.html', '/USER_GUIDE.html', '/guide.html',
405:   '/account-deletion.html', '/account-deletion', '/privacy.html', '/privacy'
406: ]);
407: 
408: /* Delta Phase 4 (gap 4): جمعِ سنجه‌هایِ sync برایِ /api/health —
409:    سری‌های برچسب‌دار (pulls{mode}، compressions{encoding}) جمع می‌شوند و
410:    برایِ هیستوگرام‌های حجم، میانگینِ sum/count گزارش می‌شود. خروجی فقط
411:    عدد صحیح/اعشاریِ محدود است (قاعدهٔ Q3 — هیچ دادهٔ session/tenant بیرون
412:    نمی‌رود). */
413: function syncHealthStats(snap) {
414:   snap = snap || {};
415:   const total = (name, labelKey, labelVal) => {
416:     const m = snap[name];
417:     if (!m || !Array.isArray(m.series)) return 0;
418:     return m.series
419:       .filter((x) => !labelKey || (x.labels && x.labels[labelKey] === labelVal))
420:       .reduce((a, x) => a + (Number(x.value) || 0), 0);
421:   };
422:   const avg = (name) => {
423:     const m = snap[name];
424:     if (!m || !Array.isArray(m.series) || !m.series.length) return null;
425:     let sum = 0, cnt = 0;
426:     for (const x of m.series) { sum += Number(x.sum) || 0; cnt += Number(x.count) || 0; }
427:     return cnt > 0 ? Math.round((sum / cnt) * 10) / 10 : null;
428:   };
429:   return {
430:     pulls_total: total('payesh_sync_pulls_total'),
431:     pulls_delta: total('payesh_sync_pulls_total', 'mode', 'delta'),
432:     pulls_full: total('payesh_sync_pulls_total', 'mode', 'full'),
433:     pushes_total: total('payesh_sync_pushes_total'),
434:     conflicts_total: total('payesh_sync_conflicts_total'),
435:     backpressure_rejections_total: total('payesh_sync_backpressure_rejections_total'),
436:     cursor_expired_total: total('payesh_cursor_expired_total'),
437:     cursor_region_mismatch_total: total('payesh_cursor_region_mismatch_total'),
438:     delta_size_bytes_avg: avg('payesh_sync_delta_size_bytes'),
439:     delta_wire_bytes_avg: avg('payesh_sync_delta_wire_bytes'),
440:     compressions_total: total('payesh_sync_delta_compressions_total')
441:   };
442: }
443: 
444: function routeTemplate(pathname) {
445:   if (typeof pathname !== 'string' || pathname === '') return 'static_other';
446:   const p = pathname.length > 512 ? pathname.slice(0, 512) : pathname;
447:   if (ROUTE_EXACT.has(p)) return p;
448:   for (let i = 0; i < ROUTE_PATTERNS.length; i++) {
449:     if (ROUTE_PATTERNS[i].re.test(p)) return ROUTE_PATTERNS[i].route;
450:   }
451:   if (STATIC_EXACT.has(p)) return 'static';
452:   /* Fail-closed: never promote an arbitrary path (query strings, ids,
453:      traversal attempts) into a label value. */
454:   return p.indexOf('/api/') === 0 ? 'api_unmatched' : 'static_other';
455: }
456: 
457: /* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
458:    The process-wide registry + the declared metric catalogue.
459:    ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
460: const registry = createRegistry({
461:   maxSeries: clampInt(process.env.PAYESH_METRICS_MAX_SERIES, 1024, 8, 100000)
462: });
463: 
464: function declareAll(r) {
465:   /* ── HTTP ── */
466:   r.counter('payesh_http_requests_total', 'HTTP requests served, by route template, method and status code.', ['route', 'method', 'code']);
467:   r.histogram('payesh_http_request_duration_seconds', 'HTTP request latency in seconds.', ['route', 'method'], [0.005, 0.01, 0.025, 0.05, 0.1, 0.25, 0.5, 1, 2.5, 5, 10]);
468:   r.counter('payesh_http_responses_bytes_total', 'Response body bytes written (size signal for payload regressions).', ['route']);
469:   /* ── Auth / abuse ── */
470:   r.counter('payesh_auth_otp_requests_total', 'OTP send-code requests by outcome.', ['outcome']);
471:   r.counter('payesh_auth_login_total', 'Login attempts by outcome.', ['outcome']);
472:   r.counter('payesh_auth_rejections_total', 'Session-scoped rejections (401/403/404) counted by the enumeration guard.', ['stage']);
473:   /* ── Runtime security monitoring (Q3) ── */
474:   r.counter('payesh_runtime_anomalies_total', 'Rolling-baseline runtime anomalies by bounded signal.', ['signal']);
475:   r.counter('payesh_attack_patterns_detected_total', 'Runtime attack signatures detected by bounded pattern and severity.', ['pattern', 'severity']);
476:   r.gauge('payesh_suspicious_sessions', 'Distinct suspicious runtime sessions in the last 24 hours.', []);
477:   r.gauge('payesh_attack_patterns_blocked', 'Detected attack patterns associated with blocked/denied attempts in the last 24 hours.', []);
478:   /* ── Sync / A01 ── */
479:   r.counter('payesh_sync_requests_total', 'POST /api/sync requests by status code.', ['code']);
480:   r.histogram('payesh_sync_batch_ops', 'Ops per sync push (offline queue drain size).', [], [1, 5, 10, 25, 50, 100, 250, 500]);
481:   r.counter('payesh_sync_conflicts_total', 'Sync conflicts detected (optimistic concurrency).', ['collection']);
482:   /* Delta Hardening Phase 2 (gap 4): OCC base_version gate latency — the
483:      detection step (locate record + version compare) on every versioned
484:      write. Labels are a closed set: conflict | stale | clean. */
485:   r.histogram('payesh_sync_conflict_detection_seconds', 'OCC conflict-detection time (base_version gate).', ['outcome'],
486:     [0.00005, 0.0001, 0.00025, 0.0005, 0.001, 0.0025, 0.005, 0.01, 0.025, 0.05, 0.1, 0.25]);
487:   /* ── Delta Phase 4 — backpressure / compression / observability / region ── */
488:   r.counter('payesh_sync_backpressure_rejections_total', 'Sync batches rejected with 429 sync_backpressure (per-session op window exceeded).', []);
489:   r.counter('payesh_sync_pulls_total', 'Pull requests served, by mode (delta | full).', ['mode']);
490:   r.counter('payesh_sync_pushes_total', 'Sync pushes received (POST /api/sync with a non-empty ops batch).', []);
491:   r.histogram('payesh_sync_delta_size_bytes', 'Pull response JSON size before compression (bytes).', [],
492:     [256, 1024, 4096, 16384, 65536, 262144, 1048576, 4194304]);
493:   r.histogram('payesh_sync_delta_wire_bytes', 'Pull response bytes actually put on the wire (post-compression when negotiated).', [],
494:     [256, 1024, 4096, 16384, 65536, 262144, 1048576, 4194304]);
495:   r.counter('payesh_sync_delta_compressions_total', 'Pull responses compressed, by encoding (gzip | br).', ['encoding']);
496:   r.counter('payesh_cursor_expired_total', 'Pull cursors rejected as expired (TTL).', []);
497:   /* ── پ۳ تله‌متری بریدگی/resume — سنجشِ معیارِ بازفعال‌سازی #۱ دوزیهٔ کش
498:      (REPORT_CACHE_ISOLATION_DOSSIER §۵). کاردینالیته کران‌دار: برچسبِ
499:      collection فقط از مجموعهٔ ثابتِ سنگین + 'other' (pull.js تحمیل می‌کند). */
500:   r.counter('payesh_pull_partial_collections_total',
501:     'Collections delivered truncated (row-cap or byte-budget) in a pull response.', ['collection']);
502:   r.counter('payesh_pull_full_snapshot_required_total',
503:     'Truncated-delta collections flagged full_snapshot_required (client must resume).', ['collection']);
504:   r.counter('payesh_pull_resume_snapshot_total',
505:     'Bounded full-snapshot pulls served for a client resume (resume=1).', ['collection']);
506:   r.counter('payesh_cursor_region_mismatch_total', 'Pull cursors rejected because they were issued by another region (cursor v2 rg binding).', []);
507:   /* ── Database ── */
508:   r.histogram('payesh_db_query_duration_seconds', 'SQL round-trip latency.', ['op', 'target']);
509:   r.counter('payesh_db_query_errors_total', 'SQL errors by op and target.', ['op', 'target']);
510:   r.counter('payesh_db_slow_queries_total', 'SQL round trips slower than PAYESH_DB_SLOW_MS.', ['op', 'target']);
511:   r.gauge('payesh_db_pool_connections', 'Pool connections by state.', ['pool', 'state']);
512:   r.gauge('payesh_db_pool_waiting', 'Clients waiting for a pool connection.', ['pool']);
513:   /* ── Audit & Authority Observability (S2 Remediation) ── */
514:   r.counter('payesh_audit_write_failures_total', 'Failures writing structured audit log entries.', ['sink', 'reason']);
515:   r.counter('payesh_authority_unavailable_total', 'Authority queries or assertions failing due to database detachment or connection drop.', ['subsystem', 'reason']);
516:   /* ── Cache / Redis ── */
517:   r.counter('payesh_cache_lookups_total', 'Bootstrap cache lookups by layer and outcome.', ['layer', 'outcome']);
518:   r.counter('payesh_cache_invalidations_total', 'Cache invalidations by scope.', ['scope']);
519:   r.counter('payesh_rate_limit_decisions_total', 'Distributed rate-limit decisions.', ['action', 'decision']);
520:   /* ── Queue / worker ── */
521:   r.gauge('payesh_outbox_depth', 'Pending outbox events (async queue depth).', ['status']);
522:   r.counter('payesh_worker_events_total', 'Worker event processing outcome.', ['outcome']);
523:   /* ── Runtime ── */
524:   r.gauge('payesh_node_heap_used_bytes', 'Node heap used.', []);
525:   r.gauge('payesh_node_heap_total_bytes', 'Node heap total.', []);
526:   r.gauge('payesh_node_rss_bytes', 'Process RSS.', []);
527:   r.gauge('payesh_node_eventloop_lag_seconds', 'Event-loop scheduling lag (sampled).', []);
528:   /* Declared a GAUGE on purpose: startRuntimeCollector() `set`s an absolute
529:      seconds value from process.cpuUsage(). Declaring it a counter and then
530:      setting it would both lie in the exposition format AND be silently
531:      dropped by set() (not_a_gauge) — the CPU series would never appear. */
532:   r.gauge('payesh_node_cpu_seconds_total', 'Process CPU time in seconds (absolute, sampled).', ['mode']);
533:   r.gauge('payesh_node_uptime_seconds', 'Process uptime.', []);
534:   r.gauge('payesh_build_info', 'Build/deploy identity (always 1).', ['version', 'phase']);
535:   /* قراردادِ exposition (فرمتِ Prometheus): هیستوگرام‌ها علاوه بر خودِ نام،
536:      سری‌هایِ payesh_http_request_duration_seconds_bucket و _sum و _count را
537:      می‌سازند (رندررِ پایین، خطِ m.name + '_bucket'). */
538:   /* ── main-stack compatibility series (چت ۵ live-deploy: alert-rules.yml و
539:         dashboards/payesh-main.json این نام‌ها را می‌خوانند). مقادیر در
540:         زمانِ scrape توسط publishRuntimeProbes() تازه می‌شوند. نام‌ها عمداً
541:         همان‌هایِ استکِ زندهٔ main هستند تا زنجیرهٔ alert/dashboard نشکند. ── */
542:   r.gauge('payesh_redis_up', 'Redis reachable (scrape-time probe).', []);
543:   r.gauge('payesh_redis_ping_latency_ms', 'Redis PING round-trip in ms (scrape-time probe).', []);
544:   r.gauge('payesh_db_up', 'Database engine reachable (scrape-time probe).', []);
545:   r.gauge('payesh_db_query_latency_ms', 'DB health-check latency in ms (scrape-time probe).', []);
546:   r.gauge('payesh_db_pool_total', 'Pool connections (total) by pool.', ['pool']);
547:   r.gauge('payesh_db_pool_idle', 'Pool connections (idle) by pool.', ['pool']);
548:   r.gauge('payesh_sync_queue_depth', 'Pending outbox rows (sync queue depth).', []);
549:   r.gauge('payesh_cache_hits_total', 'L1 cache hits (compat alias of l1Stats).', []);
550:   r.gauge('payesh_cache_misses_total', 'L1 cache misses (compat alias of l1Stats).', []);
551:   r.gauge('payesh_eventloop_lag_ms', 'Event-loop lag in ms (compat alias of the sampled seconds gauge).', []);
552:   r.gauge('payesh_process_heap_bytes', 'Process heap used (compat alias).', []);
553:   r.gauge('payesh_process_rss_bytes', 'Process RSS (compat alias).', []);
554:   r.gauge('payesh_process_uptime_seconds', 'Process uptime (compat alias).', []);
555:   r.gauge('payesh_process_gc_total', 'GC cycles since collector start (best-effort).', []);
556:   /* ── دیسک (F5) — فضای فایل‌سیستمِ مسیرِ داده. statfs نیتیوِ Node، بدون وابستگیِ تازه.
557:        این سه سری با قاعدهٔ DiskSpaceLow در infra/observability/alert-rules.yml (تنها
558:        فایلی که prometheus.yml rule_files بارگذاری می‌کند) و با ردیفِ متناظر در آرایهٔ
559:        RULES در tests/observability-config.js قفلِ متقابل دارند. این سه فایل باید با هم
560:        land شوند: آن تست ruleBlocks.length === RULES.length را می‌سنجد، پس افزودنِ
561:        قاعده بدونِ افزودنِ ردیف، گیت را قرمز می‌کند. ── */
562:   r.gauge('payesh_disk_total_bytes', 'Total bytes of the filesystem holding the data directory (statfs blocks*bsize).', []);
563:   r.gauge('payesh_disk_avail_bytes', 'Non-privileged available bytes on the data filesystem (statfs bavail*bsize).', []);
564:   r.gauge('payesh_disk_used_ratio', 'Used ratio 0..1 of the data filesystem (node_exporter convention: root-reserved blocks excluded).', []);
565: }
566: 
567: declareAll(registry);
568: 
569: /* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
570:    Runtime collector — started explicitly by server/index.js so that
571:    merely requiring this module (tests) has no timers and no side effects.
572:    ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
573: let runtimeTimers = null;
574: 
575: 
576: /* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
577:    publishRuntimeProbes — کشفِ لحظهٔ scrape برای سری‌های سازگاریِ استکِ main
578:    (منتقل از metrics.js قدیمیِ main — چت ۵؛ هر بلوک مستقل fail-safe است).
579:    ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
580: async function _safe(fn, fallback) { try { return await fn(); } catch (e) { return fallback; } }
581: 
582: async function publishRuntimeProbes() {
583:   /* Redis */
584:   try {
585:     const redisMod = await _safe(() => require('./redis'), null);
586:     let up = 0, pingMs = 0;
587:     if (redisMod && typeof redisMod.isRedis === 'function' && redisMod.isRedis()) {
588:       up = 1;
589:       if (typeof redisMod.ping === 'function') {
590:         const t0 = process.hrtime.bigint();
591:         const ok = await _safe(() => redisMod.ping(), false);
592:         pingMs = Number(process.hrtime.bigint() - t0) / 1e6;
593:         if (!ok || ok.ok !== true || ok.ping === false) up = 0;
594:       }
595:     }
596:     registry.set('payesh_redis_up', [], up);
597:     registry.set('payesh_redis_ping_latency_ms', [], pingMs);
598:   } catch (e) {}
599:   /* DB (engine up + health latency + pools + outbox depth) */
600:   try {
601:     const dbMod = await _safe(() => require('./db'), null);
602:     if (dbMod && typeof dbMod.isPostgres === 'function' && dbMod.isPostgres()) {
603:       registry.set('payesh_db_up', [], 1);
604:       const h = await _safe(() => dbMod.healthCheck(), null);
605:       registry.set('payesh_db_query_latency_ms', [], (h && Number(h.latency_ms)) || 0);
606:       const s = await _safe(() => dbMod.poolStats(), null);
607:       if (s) {
608:         const pools = [];
609:         if (s.primary) pools.push(['primary', s.primary]);
610:         if (s.read_replica && s.read_replica.active) pools.push(['read_replica', s.read_replica]);
611:         for (const [nm, ps] of pools) {
612:           registry.set('payesh_db_pool_total', [nm], Number(ps.total_count) || 0);
613:           registry.set('payesh_db_pool_idle', [nm], Number(ps.idle_count) || 0);
614:         }
615:       }
616:       const r = await _safe(() => dbMod.query("SELECT count(*)::int AS n FROM server_outbox WHERE status = 'pending'"), null);
617:       registry.set('payesh_sync_queue_depth', [], r && r.rows && r.rows[0] ? Number(r.rows[0].n) : 0);
618:     } else {
619:       registry.set('payesh_db_up', [], 1); /* memory mode: process-local, inline flush */
620:       registry.set('payesh_sync_queue_depth', [], 0);
621:     }
622:   } catch (e) { try { registry.set('payesh_db_up', [], 0); } catch (e2) {} }
623:   /* Cache L1 (compat aliases) */
624:   try {
625:     const cacheMod = await _safe(() => require('./cache'), null);
626:     if (cacheMod && typeof cacheMod.l1Stats === 'function') {
627:       const s = cacheMod.l1Stats() || {};
628:       registry.set('payesh_cache_hits_total', [], s.hits | 0);
629:       registry.set('payesh_cache_misses_total', [], s.misses | 0);
630:     }
631:   } catch (e) {}
632:   /* Process compat aliases */
633:   try {
634:     const mu = process.memoryUsage();
635:     registry.set('payesh_process_heap_bytes', [], mu.heapUsed || 0);
636:     registry.set('payesh_process_rss_bytes', [], mu.rss || 0);
637:     registry.set('payesh_process_uptime_seconds', [], process.uptime() || 0);
638:     const lagS = Number(registry.value('payesh_node_eventloop_lag_seconds', []) || 0);
639:     registry.set('payesh_eventloop_lag_ms', [], lagS * 1000);
640:     if (typeof gcCounter === 'number') registry.set('payesh_process_gc_total', [], gcCounter);
641:   } catch (e) {}
642:   /* Disk (F5) — statfs on the filesystem holding the data directory.
643:      Native fs.promises.statfs (Node >=19.6; fs.statfs since >=18.15) — no new dependency.
644:      Fail-soft by design: any error leaves the series unset instead of breaking the scrape,
645:      matching the try/catch discipline of every other probe in this function. */
646:   try {
647:     const fsMod = await _safe(() => require('fs'), null);
648:     const pathMod = await _safe(() => require('path'), null);
649:     if (fsMod && pathMod && fsMod.promises && typeof fsMod.promises.statfs === 'function') {
650:       const dataDir = process.env.PAYESH_DATA_DIR || pathMod.join(__dirname, 'data');
651:       /* server/data may not exist before the first seed — walk up to the nearest existing
652:          ancestor so the probe reports the filesystem that would hold the data rather than
653:          throwing ENOENT and silently publishing nothing. */
654:       let probe = pathMod.resolve(dataDir);
655:       while (!fsMod.existsSync(probe)) {
656:         const up = pathMod.dirname(probe);
657:         if (up === probe) break; /* filesystem root */
658:         probe = up;
659:       }
660:       const st = await _safe(() => fsMod.promises.statfs(probe), null);
661:       const bsize = st ? Number(st.bsize) : 0;
662:       const blocks = st ? Number(st.blocks) : 0;
663:       if (bsize > 0 && blocks > 0) {
664:         const total = blocks * bsize;
665:         const free = Number(st.bfree) * bsize;
666:         const avail = Number(st.bavail) * bsize;
667:         const used = Math.max(0, total - free);
668:         /* node_exporter convention: the denominator excludes root-reserved blocks
669:            (bfree - bavail), so the ratio matches what operators compare against. */
670:         const denom = used + avail;
671:         registry.set('payesh_disk_total_bytes', [], total);
672:         registry.set('payesh_disk_avail_bytes', [], avail);
673:         registry.set('payesh_disk_used_ratio', [], denom > 0 ? used / denom : 0);
674:       }
675:     }
676:   } catch (e) {}
677: }
678: 
679: /* شمارشِ GC فقط با اجرایِ کلکتور (بدون side-effect در require) */
680: let gcCounter = 0;
681: let gcObserverInstalled = false;
682: function installGcObserver() {
683:   if (gcObserverInstalled) return;
684:   gcObserverInstalled = true;
685:   try {
686:     const { PerformanceObserver } = require('perf_hooks');
687:     new PerformanceObserver((list) => { gcCounter += list.getEntries().length; }).observe({ entryTypes: ['gc'] });
688:   } catch (e) {}
689: }
690: function startRuntimeCollector(intervalMs) {
691:   installGcObserver();
692:   if (runtimeTimers) return runtimeTimers;
693:   const ms = clampInt(intervalMs || process.env.PAYESH_METRICS_INTERVAL_MS, 5000, 250, 600000);
694: 
695:   /* Event-loop lag: schedule a timer, measure how late it actually fired. */
696:   let last = process.hrtime.bigint();
697:   const lag = setInterval(() => {
698:     try {
699:       const now = process.hrtime.bigint();
700:       const deltaS = Number(now - last) / 1e9;
701:       last = now;
702:       const lagS = deltaS - ms / 1000;
703:       registry.set('payesh_node_eventloop_lag_seconds', null, lagS > 0 ? lagS : 0);
704:     } catch (e) { /* R1 */ }
705:   }, ms);
706: 
707:   const sample = setInterval(() => {
708:     try {
709:       const mu = process.memoryUsage();
710:       registry.set('payesh_node_heap_used_bytes', null, mu.heapUsed);
711:       registry.set('payesh_node_heap_total_bytes', null, mu.heapTotal);
712:       registry.set('payesh_node_rss_bytes', null, mu.rss);
713:       const cpu = process.cpuUsage();
714:       registry.set('payesh_node_cpu_seconds_total', { mode: 'user' }, cpu.user / 1e6);
715:       registry.set('payesh_node_cpu_seconds_total', { mode: 'system' }, cpu.system / 1e6);
716:       registry.set('payesh_node_uptime_seconds', null, process.uptime());
717:     } catch (e) { /* R1 */ }
718:   }, ms);
719:   /* cpu counters are monotonic; declare them as gauges to stay format-honest
720:      (we `set` an absolute seconds value, we never `inc`). */
721: 
722:   if (lag.unref) lag.unref();
723:   if (sample.unref) sample.unref();
724:   runtimeTimers = { lag, sample, intervalMs: ms };
725:   /* one immediate sample so a fresh scrape is never empty */
726:   try {
727:     const mu = process.memoryUsage();
728:     registry.set('payesh_node_heap_used_bytes', null, mu.heapUsed);
729:     registry.set('payesh_node_rss_bytes', null, mu.rss);
730:     registry.set('payesh_node_uptime_seconds', null, process.uptime());
731:   } catch (e) {}
732:   return runtimeTimers;
733: }
734: 
735: function stopRuntimeCollector() {
736:   if (!runtimeTimers) return;
737:   clearInterval(runtimeTimers.lag);
738:   clearInterval(runtimeTimers.sample);
739:   runtimeTimers = null;
740: }
741: 
742: /* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
743:    Scrape gate (R4) — fail-closed.
744:      PAYESH_METRICS=0                     → disabled (404)
745:      production && no token               → disabled (404, endpoint hidden)
746:      token configured                     → Authorization: Bearer <token>
747:                                             (timing-safe) else 403
748:      no token, non-production             → loopback remote address only
749:    ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
750: const LOOPBACK = new Set(['127.0.0.1', '::1', '::ffff:127.0.0.1', 'localhost']);
751: 
752: function scrapeGate(req) {
753:   const env = process.env;
754:   if (env.PAYESH_METRICS === '0') return { ok: false, status: 404, code: 'not_found' };
755:   const token = env.PAYESH_METRICS_TOKEN;
756:   const isProd = env.PAYESH_ENV === 'production';
757:   if (token) {
758:     const h = (req && req.headers && req.headers.authorization) || '';
759:     const m = /^Bearer\s+(.+)$/.exec(h);
760:     const provided = m ? m[1] : '';
761:     const a = Buffer.from(String(token), 'utf8');
762:     const b = Buffer.from(String(provided), 'utf8');
763:     const ok = a.length === b.length && a.length > 0 && crypto.timingSafeEqual(a, b);
764:     return ok ? { ok: true } : { ok: false, status: 403, code: 'forbidden' };
765:   }
766:   if (isProd) return { ok: false, status: 404, code: 'not_found' };
767:   const addr = (req && (req.socket && req.socket.remoteAddress)) || '';
768:   return LOOPBACK.has(addr) ? { ok: true } : { ok: false, status: 403, code: 'forbidden' };
769: }
770: 
771: /* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
772:    Convenience recorders used by server/index.js
773:    ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
774: function observeHttpRequest(o) {
775:   /* خوداسکرپ حذف شده (ضدفیدبک‌لوپ — قراردادِ استکِ زندهٔ main): شمردنِ
776:      خودِ /metrics یعنی هر scrape سریِ http را بزرگ‌تر می‌کند؛ هرگز شمرده نمی‌شود. */
777:   if (o && o.route === '/metrics') return;
778:   try {
779:     const route = routeTemplate(o && o.route);
780:     const method = String((o && o.method) || 'GET').toUpperCase().slice(0, 8);
781:     const code = String((o && o.status) || 0).slice(0, 3);
782:     registry.inc('payesh_http_requests_total', { route, method, code });
783:     const d = Number(o && o.durationSeconds);
784:     if (Number.isFinite(d) && d >= 0) registry.observe('payesh_http_request_duration_seconds', { route, method }, d);
785:     const bytes = Number(o && o.bytes);
786:     if (Number.isFinite(bytes) && bytes >= 0) registry.inc('payesh_http_responses_bytes_total', { route }, bytes);
787:   } catch (e) { /* R1 */ }
788: }
789: 
790: function observeAuth(event, outcome) {
791:   try {
792:     if (event === 'otp') registry.inc('payesh_auth_otp_requests_total', { outcome: String(outcome || 'unknown').slice(0, 32) });
793:     else if (event === 'login') registry.inc('payesh_auth_login_total', { outcome: String(outcome || 'unknown').slice(0, 32) });
794:     else if (event === 'rejection') registry.inc('payesh_auth_rejections_total', { stage: String(outcome || 'unknown').slice(0, 32) });
795:   } catch (e) { /* R1 */ }
796: }
797: 
798: function observeSyncBatch(opCount) {
799:   try {
800:     const n = Number(opCount);
801:     if (!Number.isFinite(n) || n < 0) return;
802:     registry.observe('payesh_sync_batch_ops', null, n);
803:   } catch (e) { /* R1 */ }
804: }
805: 
806: function observeDb(op, target, durationSeconds, errored, slow) {
807:   try {
808:     const labels = { op: String(op || 'query').slice(0, 32), target: String(target || 'primary').slice(0, 16) };
809:     const d = Number(durationSeconds);
810:     if (Number.isFinite(d) && d >= 0) registry.observe('payesh_db_query_duration_seconds', labels, d);
811:     if (errored) registry.inc('payesh_db_query_errors_total', labels);
812:     if (slow) registry.inc('payesh_db_slow_queries_total', labels);
813:   } catch (e) { /* R1 */ }
814: }
815: 
816: /* Outbox / async queue depth — closed label set (see outbox.depth()). */
817: function publishOutboxDepth(d) {
818:   try {
819:     if (!d || typeof d !== 'object') return;
820:     for (const k of ['pending', 'processed', 'failed', 'legacy', 'total']) {
821:       const v = Number(d[k]);
822:       if (Number.isFinite(v)) registry.set('payesh_outbox_depth', { status: k }, v);
823:     }
824:   } catch (e) { /* R1 */ }
825: }
826: 
827: function publishDbPools(stats) {
828:   try {
829:     if (!stats) return;
830:     const pools = [['primary', stats.primary], ['read_replica', stats.read_replica]];
831:     for (const [pname, p] of pools) {
832:       if (!p || p.active === false) continue;
833:       registry.set('payesh_db_pool_connections', { pool: pname, state: 'total' }, p.total_count || 0);
834:       registry.set('payesh_db_pool_connections', { pool: pname, state: 'idle' }, p.idle_count || 0);
835:       registry.set('payesh_db_pool_waiting', { pool: pname }, p.waiting_count || 0);
836:     }
837:   } catch (e) { /* R1 */ }
838: }
839:
