116: /* ── store ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
117: function loadStore(){
118:   if(!fs.existsSync(STORE_FILE)){
119:     /* Wave 1: with PostgreSQL configured, the file is only a bootstrap
120:        artifact — boot from an empty skeleton and hydrate from PG below. */
121:     if(process.env.DATABASE_URL){
122:       console.log('[store] no JSON file; DATABASE_URL set — booting skeleton for PG hydration');
123:       return { __processed_uids: {}, __revoked_jti: {}, __auth: { codes: {}, login_fail: {}, code_rate: {}, enum: {} } };
124:     }
125:     console.error('no store found: ' + STORE_FILE);
126:     console.error('run:  node server/seed.js   (builds it from the demo world)');
127:     process.exit(1);
128:   }
129:   /* Wave 24 (KPI-3): پارس مستقیم از Buffer — بدونِ ساختِ رشتهٔ میانیِ
130:      ~۵.۸MB در فضای JS (decode جدا ≈ ۲۲ms). V8 خودش UTF-8 را در مسیرِ
131:      سریع‌ترِ داخلی decode می‌کند: ~۵۸ms → ~۴۸ms روی استورِ مرجع. */
132:   const s = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(STORE_FILE));
133:   s.__processed_uids = s.__processed_uids || {};
134:   s.__revoked_jti    = s.__revoked_jti || {};
135:   s.__auth = s.__auth || { codes: {}, login_fail: {}, code_rate: {} };
136:   /* Wave 6: شمارندهٔ نگهبان به Redis رفت — نگه‌داشتنِ نسخهٔ قدیمی
137:      (فقط‌رشد و بدونِ GC در payesh.json) معنا ندارد. */
138:   if(s.__auth && s.__auth.enum) delete s.__auth.enum;
139:   return s;
140: }
141: const store = loadStore();
142: syncAttach(store);
143: /* بازخوردِ بازبینِ PR #94: وقتی هیدراتاسیون از PG عمداً سقف‌دار/بریده
144:    است (PAYESH_PG_HYDRATE_LIMIT/SKIP)، هیچ مسیری نباید این آینهٔ ناقص
145:    را روی store.json بنویسد (خاموشی، فال‌بکِ ورکر، خروجِ FATAL) — چون
146:    فایلِ کاملِ قبلی را می‌کُشد و بوتِ بدونِ PG فقط دادهٔ بریده می‌بیند.
147:    در PG-live مرجع PG است؛ فایلِ قدیمی دست‌نخورده می‌ماند. */
148: let mirrorIncomplete = false;
149: 
150: /* ── Wave 9 — رشتهٔ کارِ عملیاتِ سنگین ─────────────────────────────
151:    JSON.stringify(store) و نوشتنِ سنکرونِ فایل از رشتهٔ اصلی به ورکر
152:    می‌روند (persist هر ۲ ثانیه، بکاپ، گزارشِ عمومی). ورکر دیرزیاد و
153:    unref است؛ شکستش به مسیرِ سنکرونِ قدیمی برمی‌گردد (فال‌بک). */
154: const workers = createHeavyWorker({ getStore: () => store });
155: /* Wave 9 — کشِ استاتیک: خواندنِ async + اعتبارسنجیِ mtime (نه readFileSync در هر درخواست) */
156: const staticCache = createStaticCache();
157: 
158: /* ── database and caching layers initialization ── */
159: /* P0-1: dbReady is awaited by the boot gate at the bottom of this file, so a
160:    production process never calls listen() before the backing store is known
161:    good. Resolves to the db.init result, or null if init itself threw. */
162: const dbReady = db.init(store).then(async info => {
163:   /* Chat 2 remediation (P0-BUG-04): one-time mirror of the bootstrap JSON store
164:    into an empty PostgreSQL, FK-safe order, chunked, column-filtered against
165:    information_schema. Rows that cannot map are skipped and counted — the
166:    seed must never abort the boot. */
167: async function seedPgFromBootstrap(store, db) {
168:   const CHUNK = 100;
169:   const colsCache = {};
170:   const colsOf = async (t) => {
171:     if (!colsCache[t]) {
172:       const r = await db.query(`SELECT column_name FROM information_schema.columns WHERE table_schema='public' AND table_name=$1`, [t]);
173:       colsCache[t] = new Set(r.rows.map(x => x.column_name));
174:     }
175:     return colsCache[t];
176:   };
177:   const ident = (n) => '"' + String(n).replace(/"/g, '""') + '"';
178:   const valOf = (v) => (v !== null && typeof v === 'object') ? JSON.stringify(v) : v;
179:   const names = Object.keys(store).filter((k) => /^[a-z][a-z0-9_]*$/.test(k) && Array.isArray(store[k]));
180:   const head = ['schools', 'users', 'subjects', 'classes'].filter((c) => names.includes(c));
181:   const tail = names.filter((c) => !head.includes(c));
182:   const seq = head.concat(tail);
183:   let rows = 0, tables = 0, skipped = 0;
184:   for (const col of seq) {
185:     let cols;
186:     try { cols = await colsOf(col); } catch (e) { continue; }
187:     if (!cols || !cols.size) continue;
188:     const arr = (store[col] || []).filter((r) => r && r.id != null);
189:     if (!arr.length) continue;
190:     tables++;
191:     for (let i = 0; i < arr.length; i += CHUNK) {
192:       const cleanRows = [];
193:       const fieldSet = new Set();
194:       for (const r of arr.slice(i, i + CHUNK)) {
195:         const data = {};
196:         for (const k of Object.keys(r)) {
197:           if (cols.has(k)) {
198:             let val = r[k];
199:             if (col === 'attendance' && (k === 'late_at' || k === 'exit_at') && typeof val === 'string' && /^\d{2}:\d{2}$/.test(val)) {
200:               val = (r.date || '2026-09-01') + 'T' + val + ':00Z';
201:             }
202:             data[k] = val;
203:             fieldSet.add(k);
204:           }
205:         }
206:         if (data.id != null) cleanRows.push(data);
207:       }
208:       if (!cleanRows.length) continue;
209: 
210:       const rowFields = Array.from(fieldSet);
211:       try {
212:         await db.transaction(async (client) => {
213:           const params = [];
214:           const rowClauses = [];
215:           for (const row of cleanRows) {
216:             const placeholders = [];
217:             for (const f of rowFields) {
218:               const v = row[f] !== undefined ? row[f] : null;
219:               params.push(valOf(v));
220:               placeholders.push('$' + params.length);
221:             }
222:             rowClauses.push('(' + placeholders.join(', ') + ')');
223:           }
224:           const sql = 'INSERT INTO ' + ident(col) + ' (' + rowFields.map(ident).join(', ') + ') VALUES ' + rowClauses.join(', ') + ' ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING;';
225:           await client.query(sql, params);
226:         });
227:         rows += cleanRows.length;
228:       } catch (e) {
229:         /* fallback per row if bulk insert fails */
230:         for (const row of cleanRows) {
231:           try { await db.persistOpsBatch([{ c: col, t: 'ins', data: row }]); rows++; } catch (e2) { skipped++; }
232:         }
233:       }
234:     }
235:   }
236:   return { rows, tables, skipped };
237: }
238: 
239: /* P0-1: mirror of the cache/Redis readiness gate below. db.init now reports
240:      ok:false in production when PostgreSQL is absent/unreachable — the server
241:      must not serve traffic from the JSON store. */
242:   if (info && info.ok === false) {
243:     console.error('[FATAL] Database readiness failed:', info.error || info.warning || 'unknown');
244:     if (process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production' || process.env.PAYESH_ENV === 'production') {
245:       try { db.close(); } catch (e) {}
246:       process.exit(1);
247:     }
248:     return info;
249:   }
250:   if (info.driver === 'postgres') {
251:     console.log('[DB] Connected to PostgreSQL relational engine');
252:     /* Migration-012 contract, self-configured (Chat 2 remediation): on a chain-
253:        migrated database the grades/attendance tables are partitioned with PK
254:        (id, created_at) — writes only stay correct when PAYESH_PARTITIONED_TABLES
255:        lists them. Operators kept forgetting the env ⇒ every attendance/grades
256:        mirror write failed with `no unique ... for ON CONFLICT`. Detect once at
257:        boot and merge into the env (explicit operator value still wins). */
258:     try {
259:       const pr = await db.query(`SELECT c.relname FROM pg_partitioned_table pt JOIN pg_class c ON c.oid = pt.partrelid WHERE c.relname IN ('grades','attendance')`);
260:       const parts = pr.rows.map(x => x.relname);
261:       if (parts.length) {
262:         const cur = String(process.env.PAYESH_PARTITIONED_TABLES || '').split(',').map(x => x.trim()).filter(Boolean);
263:         const merged = [...new Set([...cur, ...parts])].join(',');
264:         if (cur.join(',') !== merged) {
265:           process.env.PAYESH_PARTITIONED_TABLES = merged;
266:           console.log('[DB] partitioned tables detected — PAYESH_PARTITIONED_TABLES=' + merged + ' (auto-configured, migration 012 contract)');
267:         }
268:       }
269:     } catch (e) { /* pre-012 database — nothing to configure */ }
270:     /* P0-BUG-04 fix (Chat 2 remediation, 2026-09-19): PG empty + a populated
271:        bootstrap JSON store ⇒ hydration would REPLACE the bootstrap data with
272:        empty arrays and the persist loop then overwrites the store FILE with an
273:        empty shell (auth dies, local data destroyed — reproduced live).
274:        Guard: when both users and schools are empty in PG and the bootstrap
275:        store has users, keep the bootstrap data this boot AND seed PostgreSQL
276:        from it once (mission option A+B combined) — PG becomes the real SSoT
277:        and the next boot hydrates normally. */
278:     let keepBootstrap = false;
279:     try {
280:       const er = await db.query('SELECT (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM users) AS u, (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM schools) AS s');
281:       const pgEmpty = Number(er.rows[0].u) === 0 && Number(er.rows[0].s) === 0;
282:       keepBootstrap = pgEmpty && Array.isArray(store.users) && store.users.length > 0
283:         && process.env.PAYESH_FORCE_HYDRATION !== '1';   /* B7: explicit force overrides the guard */
284:     } catch (e) { /* tables missing ⇒ not a clean-empty PG — hydrate as before */ }
285:     if (keepBootstrap) {
286:       console.log('[store] PG is empty and a bootstrap JSON store is present — hydration SKIPPED (P0-BUG-04 guard); seeding PG from the bootstrap store now (one-time)...');
287:       try {
288:         const r = await seedPgFromBootstrap(store, db);
289:         console.log('[store] bootstrap→PG seed done: ' + r.rows + ' row(s) / ' + r.tables + ' table(s)' + (r.skipped ? ' — skipped ' + r.skipped + ' non-mappable row(s)' : ''));
290:       } catch (e) {
291:         console.warn('[store] bootstrap→PG seed failed (bootstrap mode stays; retried next boot):', e.message);
292:       }
293:     } else
294:     /* Wave 1: PG is authoritative — replace store domain collections with
295:        PG truth at boot (per-table failures warn and keep going). */
296:     try {
297:       const h = await db.hydrateStoreFromPg(store);
298:       mirrorIncomplete = db.shouldPersistMirrorFile(db.isPostgres(), h) === false;
299:       if (mirrorIncomplete) {
300:         console.warn('[store] mirror incomplete (capped/env-skipped hydration) — JSON file persist DISABLED: a trimmed snapshot must never overwrite the full store file (PG is authoritative)');
301:       }
302:       if (db.hydrationUsersCapped && db.hydrationUsersCapped(h)) {
303:         console.warn('[store] WARNING: users hydration is capped — users beyond the cap CANNOT authenticate; PAYESH_PG_HYDRATE_LIMIT is for load-test/staging sandboxes only (see docs/WAVE18_LOAD_TEST_REPORT.md §5-4)');
304:       }
305:       console.log('[DB] Hydrated ' + h.hydrated + ' collections from PostgreSQL' +
306:         (h.skipped.length ? ' (skipped: ' + h.skipped.join(',') + ')' : '') +
307:         (h.capped && h.capped.length ? ' (capped: ' + h.capped.join(',') + ')' : '') +
308:         (h.env_skipped && h.env_skipped.length ? ' (env-skipped: ' + h.env_skipped.join(',') + ')' : ''));
309:     } catch (e) { console.warn('[DB] Hydration warning:', e.message); }
310:     try {
311:       require('./infrastructure/ops-kv').attach(db);
312:       await require('./infrastructure/national-traffic-fabric').refreshTrafficFromSoT();
313:       console.log('[OPS-KV] attached — Phase-5 traffic fabric hydrates from phase6_ops_kv');
314:     } catch (e) {
315:       console.error('[OPS-KV] attach/hydrate failed:', e && e.message);
316:       if (process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production' || process.env.PAYESH_ENV === 'production') throw e;
317:     }
318:     try {
319:       authority.attach(db);
320:       await authority.hydrateControlPlane();
321:       console.log('[AUTHORITY] attached + control-plane hydrated from PostgreSQL');
322:     } catch (e) {
323:       console.error('[AUTHORITY] attach/hydrate failed:', e && e.message);
324:       if (process.env.DATABASE_URL) throw e;
325:     }
326:   }
327:   return info;
328: }).catch(err => {
329:   console.warn('[DB] PostgreSQL init warning:', err.message);
330:   return null;
331: });
332: 
333: cache.init().then((r) => {
334:   if (r && r.ok === false) {
335:     /* P0-13: در تولید بدونِ ردیسِ زنده سرویس نمی‌دهیم — فال‌بک به حافظهٔ
336:        محلی بین نمونه‌ها واگرا می‌شود. خروجی غیرصفر = شکستِ ریدی. */
337:     console.error('[FATAL] Cache readiness failed:', r.error || r.warning || 'unknown');
338:     /* Phase 8.1 (R5): the fail-fast predicate now covers every
339:        production-equivalent shape, not only NODE_ENV. Previously a boot that
340:        declared just PAYESH_ENV=production — or set DATABASE_URL with no
341:        production flag (PostgreSQL mode; redis.js isProduction() treats it as
342:        production for fail-closed) — logged this FATAL but kept listening
343:        forever with health=503: a zombie startup. A configured-but-unreachable
344:        Redis at boot keeps the same exit (Wave-15 boot contract); runtime
345:        outages after a healthy boot remain loud degradation (readiness 503,
346:        reconnect loop), unchanged. */
347:     if (process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production' || process.env.PAYESH_ENV === 'production' || process.env.DATABASE_URL) {
348:       try { persistStore(); } catch (e) {}
349:       try { db.close(); } catch (e) {}
350:       process.exit(1);
351:     }
352:     return;
353:   }
354:   if (redis.isRedis()) {
355:     console.log('[Cache] Redis distributed caching and pub/sub active');
356:   }
357: }).catch((err) => {
358:   console.error('[FATAL] Cache init crashed:', (err && err.message) || err);
359:   if (process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production' || process.env.PAYESH_ENV === 'production' || process.env.DATABASE_URL) process.exit(1);
360: });
361: 
362: /* ── R97 (TODO 2.7) — نگهبانِ شمردنِ شناسه، سطحِ روتر ──────────────
363:    هر رد (401/403/404) برایِ هر نشست در پنجرهٔ ۱۰ دقیقه شمرده می‌شود:
364:      ≥ WARN   → auditِ هشدار
365:      ≥ SLOW1  → تأخیرِ ۵۰۰ms روی درخواست‌هایِ بعدیِ همان نشست
366:      ≥ SLOW2  → تأخیرِ ۲s
367:      ≥ REVOKE → ابطالِ نشست (jti) — ادامه = 401
368:    /api/auth/* مستثنی است (cooldown/سقف‌هایِ OTP سقفِ خودشان را دارند). */
369: const ENUM_WINDOW_MS = 10 * 60 * 1000;
370: const _num = (v, d) => { const n = Number(v); return Number.isFinite(n) && n > 0 ? n : d; };
371: const ENUM_WARN   = _num(process.env.PAYESH_ENUM_WARN, 20);
372: const ENUM_SLOW1  = _num(process.env.PAYESH_ENUM_SLOW1, 100);
373: const ENUM_SLOW2  = _num(process.env.PAYESH_ENUM_SLOW2, 500);
374: const ENUM_REVOKE = _num(process.env.PAYESH_ENUM_REVOKE, 2000);
375: const REQ_STATE = { sess: null };
376: /* Wave 6: شمارندهٔ نگهبان روی Redis — پنجرهٔ ۱۰ دقیقه با TTL؛ بین نمونه‌ها
377:    مشترک (پیش‌تر درون‌فروشگاهی بود و چرخشِ حمله بین نمونه‌ها آن را صفر
378:    می‌کرد؛ هم‌چنین در payesh.json بدونِ GC رشدِ بی‌پایان داشت). */
379: const ENUM_TTL_S = ENUM_WINDOW_MS / 1000;
380: function enumKey(sess){ return 'payesh:enum:' + sess.jti; }
381: async function enumTouch(sess){
382:   try{
383:     return await redis.incrWithTtl(enumKey(sess), ENUM_TTL_S);
384:   }catch(e){ return 0; } /* خطای ردیس = شمارِ این درخواست گم می‌شود (سکوت) — پنجرهٔ بعدی از نو می‌شمارد */
385: }
386: async function enumRead(sess){
387:   try{
388:     const v = await redis.get(enumKey(sess));
389:     const n = v == null ? 0 : parseInt(v, 10);
390:     return Number.isFinite(n) && n > 0 ? n : 0;
391:   }catch(e){ return 0; }
392: }
393: function enumDelayMs(n){
394:   if(n >= ENUM_SLOW2) return 2000;
395:   if(n >= ENUM_SLOW1) return 500;
396:   return 0;
397: }
398: /* R97 — مراحلِ نگهبان (هشدار/تأخیر/ابطال)؛ Wave 6: ورودی = nِ شمارندهٔ ردیس.
399:    ابطال در REVOKE هم توزیع‌شده می‌شود (denylistِ Redis) — پیش‌تر فقط محلی بود. */
400: function enumStage(n, sess){
401:   if(n === ENUM_WARN) audit('enum_warn', { user_id: sess.id, n });
402:   else if(n === ENUM_SLOW1) audit('enum_slow', { user_id: sess.id, n, delay_ms: 500 });
403:   else if(n === ENUM_SLOW2) audit('enum_slow2', { user_id: sess.id, n, delay_ms: 2000 });
404:   else if(n === ENUM_REVOKE){
405:     store.__revoked_jti[sess.jti] = { at: Date.now(), reason: 'enumeration' };
406:     revocation.revokeSession(sess.jti, SESSION_TTL_S).catch(() => {});
407:     audit('enum_revoke', { user_id: sess.id, n });
408:   }
409: }
410: 
411: let dirty = false;
412: function markDirty(){ dirty = true; workers.bump(); }
413: 
414: /* ── GC of internal state (دور ۸۵ P1-3 — AD ۸۵.۲) ─────────────
415:    سه نقشهٔ فقط-رشد:
416:    __processed_uids — ایدمپوتانس؛ صفِ کلاینت‌ها عمرش کمتر از ۳۰ روز
417:      است، پس uidهایِ کهنه‌تر هرگز دوباره ارسال نمی‌شوند. ری‌پلایِ
418:      یک uidِ کهنه پس از GC با قرارداد سازگار است (ایدمپوتانس در
419:      حدِ عمرِ صف برقرار است).
420:    __revoked_jti — فقط در حدِ TTLِ نشست (۸h، بند ۲.۱) معنا دارد.
421:    (R101: __auth.codes به otp.json رفت — جارویش با همان فایل است.)
422:    در حلقهٔ persist اجرا می‌شود: سه جارو O(n) ناچیز؛ payesh.json
423:    (و بکاپ‌هایش) دیگر بی‌پایان رشد نمی‌کنند. */
424: const UID_GC_MS = 30 * 24 * 3600 * 1000;
425: const JTI_GC_MS = SESSION_TTL_S * 1000;
426: function gcStore(){
427:   const now = Date.now();
428:   let n = 0;
429:   /* logout stores a scalar timestamp, while the enumeration guard stores
430:      { at, reason }. Normalize both shapes before applying the retention
431:      window; subtracting an object yields NaN and made those entries live
432:      forever in a long-running process. Invalid internal timestamps are
433:      discarded as unusable state so the maps remain bounded. */
434:   const timestampOf = (value) => {
435:     if (typeof value === 'number') return value;
436:     if (value && typeof value === 'object') return Number(value.at);
437:     return Number(value);
438:   };
439:   const expired = (value, ttl) => {
440:     const at = timestampOf(value);
441:     return !Number.isFinite(at) || now - at > ttl;
442:   };
443:   for(const k in store.__processed_uids){
444:     if(expired(store.__processed_uids[k], UID_GC_MS)){ delete store.__processed_uids[k]; n++; }
445:   }
446:   for(const k in store.__revoked_jti){
447:     if(expired(store.__revoked_jti[k], JTI_GC_MS)){ delete store.__revoked_jti[k]; n++; }
448:   }
449:   return n;
450: }
451: /* ── Wave 9 — persist بیرون از رشتهٔ اصلی ──────────────────────────
452:    مسیرِ عادی (تیکرِ ۲ ثانیه): اسنپ‌شات با structured clone به ورکر
453:    می‌رود؛ JSON.stringify و نوشتنِ فایل در رشتهٔ پس‌زمینه انجام می‌شود.
454:    تازگیِ داده با نسخه‌ها تضمین می‌شود (workers.bump در markDirty):
455:    هر جهشِ حینِ پرواز دوباره dirty می‌کند و چرخهٔ بعدی می‌نویسد.
456:    هم‌جوشی: اگر نوشتنِ قبلی هنوز در جریان است، فقط پرچم می‌خورد. */
457: let persistBusy = false;    /* نوشتنِ ورکر در جریان است */
458: let persistQueued = false;  /* حینِ پرواز دوباره کثیف شد */
459: function persistStore(){
460:   /* PR #94 review-guard: با آینهٔ سقف‌دار، فایل هرگز با اسنپ‌شاتِ بریده
461:      بازنویسی نمی‌شود (این مسیرِ FATAL/فال‌بک هم هست). شرط عمداً فقط
462:      mirrorIncomplete است، نه isPostgres(): در خاموشی، db.close() پیش از
463:      رویدادِ exit اجرا می‌شود و isPostgres() دیگر false است — تصمیم باید
464:      با snapshotِ بوت قفل بماند (یافتهٔ آزمونِ لایو). */
465:   if(mirrorIncomplete) return;
466:   if(!dirty) return;
467:   if(persistBusy){ persistQueued = true; return; }
468:   dirty = false; /* نقطهٔ اسنپ‌شات — جهشِ بعدی دوباره کثیف می‌کند */
469:   const gc = gcStore();
470:   if(gc) try { audit('store_gc', { removed: gc }); } catch(e){}
471:   persistBusy = true;
472:   workers.runPersist(STORE_FILE).then(() => {
473:     persistBusy = false;
474:     if(persistQueued || dirty){ persistQueued = false; persistStore(); }
475:   }).catch(() => {
476:     persistBusy = false;
477:     persistStoreSync(); /* ورکر در دسترس نیست → مسیرِ سنکرونِ قدیمی */
478:   });
479: }
480: /* مسیرِ سنکرون — فقط خاموشی (exit/SIGTERM/SIGINT) و فال‌بکِ خطای ورکر؛
481:    هرگز در مسیرِ درخواست یا تیکرِ دوره‌ای صدا نمی‌شود. */
482: function persistStoreSync(){
483:   /* همان گارد — مسیرِ خاموشی (exit/SIGTERM/SIGINT) و فال‌بکِ ورکر.
484:      مستقل از isPostgres(): در exit بعد از db.close() اتصال مرده است ولی
485:      آینه هنوز بریده است — نباید نوشت. */
486:   if(mirrorIncomplete) return;
487:   if(!dirty && !persistBusy && !persistQueued) return;
488:   dirty = false; persistQueued = false;
489:   const gc = gcStore();
490:   if(gc) try { audit('store_gc', { removed: gc }); } catch(e){}
491:   try{
492:     const tmp = STORE_FILE + '.tmp';
493:     /* Wave 24 (KPI-3): همان قالبِ ASCII-escapedِ ورکر — پارسِ بوتِ بعدی سریع‌تر.
494:        این مسیر فقط در خاموشی/فال‌بک اجرا می‌شود؛ ~۴۰ms اضافه بی‌اثر است. */
495:     fs.writeFileSync(tmp, require('./json-fast').stringifyAscii(store), { encoding: 'utf8', mode: 0o600 }); /* S-73-3: PII — owner-only */
496:     fs.renameSync(tmp, STORE_FILE);
497:     try{ fs.chmodSync(STORE_FILE, 0o600); }catch(e){}
498:   }catch(e){ /* store file may be gone (tests) — never crash on exit */ }
499: }
500: /* P0-1: the periodic JSON mirror must never run in production. If PostgreSQL
501:    drops mid-session, isPostgres() goes false — writing payesh.json at that
502:    point would silently fork state per instance, which is exactly what the boot
503:    gate refuses. Production therefore logs FATAL once and leaves readiness red
504:    (db.healthCheck() → ok:false) instead of persisting JSON. */
505: let pgLostWarned = false;
506: setInterval(() => {
507:   if (db.isPostgres()) return;
508:   if (db.memoryFallbackAllowed()) { persistStore(); return; }
509:   if (!pgLostWarned) {
510:     pgLostWarned = true;
511:     console.error('[FATAL] PostgreSQL is not active in production — JSON/in-memory persistence stays DISABLED (writes must fail loudly, not fork per instance)');
512:   }
513: }, 2000).unref(); /* Wave 1: no periodic JSON persist in PG mode */
514: process.on('exit', () => {
515:   try { worker.stop(); } catch (e) {}
516:   persistStoreSync();
517:   try{ if(typeof auditLogger.flushSync === 'function') auditLogger.flushSync(); }catch(e){}
518: });
519: /* Wave 15: SIGTERM/SIGINT → handleShutdown (drain + close ناهمگام) —
520:    ثبتِ آن در پایینی فایل است؛ رویدادِ exit فقط کارِ همگام انجام می‌دهد
521:    (در رویدادِ exit promise‌ها هرگز به‌جا نمی‌رسند). */
522: 
523: /* ── JWT secret (env, or generated once; never committed) ──────────── */
524: /* P0#2 (چندنمونه‌ای): در production با بک‌اندِ مشترک (Redis/PG) کلیدِ نشست
525:    باید **مشترک** باشد — اگر env نباشد، هر instance کلیدِ خودش را روی
526:    دیسکِ محلی تولید می‌کند و توکنِ صادرشده در A در B نامعتبر می‌شود
527:    (جلساتِ چندنمونه‌ای ساکت می‌شکنند). ⇒ fail-fast در استارت.
528:    تک‌نمونهٔ production بدون Redis/PG دست‌نخورده می‌ماند (حالتِ پیشین). */
529: const SHARED_BACKEND_CONFIGURED = !!(process.env.REDIS_URL || process.env.REDIS_CLUSTER_NODES
530:   || process.env.REDIS_SENTINELS || process.env.DATABASE_URL);
531: if((process.env.PAYESH_ENV === 'production' || process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production')
532:    && !process.env.PAYESH_JWT_SECRET && SHARED_BACKEND_CONFIGURED){
533:   console.error('Error: production with shared state (Redis/PostgreSQL) requires a shared PAYESH_JWT_SECRET — the auto-generated per-instance key makes tokens valid only on the issuing instance (multi-instance sessions break). Set the same PAYESH_JWT_SECRET (>=32 bytes) in every instance.');
534:   process.exit(1);
535: }
536: let JWT_SECRET = process.env.PAYESH_JWT_SECRET || null;
537: if(!JWT_SECRET){
538:   if(fs.existsSync(KEY_FILE)) JWT_SECRET = fs.readFileSync(KEY_FILE, 'utf8').trim();
539:   else{
540:     JWT_SECRET = crypto.randomBytes(32).toString('hex');
541:     fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(KEY_FILE), { recursive: true });
542:     fs.writeFileSync(KEY_FILE, JWT_SECRET, { mode: 0o600 });
543:     console.log('generated JWT secret -> ' + KEY_FILE);
544:   }
545: }
546: /* R96 P0-3: کلیدِ HS256 باید حداقل 256 بیت باشد — کلیدِ ضعیف، استارت را
547:    می‌کُشد تا مجبور به rotation شود (خارج از repository). */
548: if(Buffer.byteLength(JWT_SECRET, 'utf8') < 32){
549:   console.error('Error: JWT key shorter than 256 bits — rotate it (set PAYESH_JWT_SECRET or regenerate ' + KEY_FILE + ')');
550:   process.exit(1);
551: }
552: /* R96 P0-3: rotation — کلیدِ قبلی برایِ مدتِ عمرِ نشست‌ها معتبر می‌ماند */
553: const JWT_PREV_SECRET = (process.env.PAYESH_JWT_SECRET_PREV || '').trim() || null;
554: /* Delta Phase 4 (gap 3): خاستگاهِ کلیدِ امضا — سلامتِ کرسر همین را
555:    بازمی‌گوید (env پایدارِ config / keyfile پایدارِ دیسک). */
556: const JWT_KEY_ORIGIN = process.env.PAYESH_JWT_SECRET ? 'env' : 'keyfile';
557: 
558: /* ── audit log (append-only, sanitized: no phone / nid / password) ───
559:    R96 P1-8 + Audit Hardening: outside store, 0600 mode, rotation on 1000 events / daily / 10MB */
560: const AUDIT_MAX_BYTES = 10 * 1024 * 1024;
561: const auditLogger = createAudit({
562:   auditFile: AUDIT_FILE,
563:   auditDir: path.join(path.dirname(AUDIT_FILE), 'audit'),
564:   maxEvents: parseInt(process.env.PAYESH_AUDIT_MAX_EVENTS || '1000', 10),
565:   maxBytes: AUDIT_MAX_BYTES
566: });
567: const audit = auditLogger.audit;
568: 
569: /* ── Q3 runtime security monitoring ──────────────────────────────────
570:    The monitor's observations are bounded and fail-safe. Attack detection is
571:    deliberately separated from enforcement: WAF/authz/rate-limits continue to
572:    make access decisions, while abuseGuard emits redacted audit/metric/webhook
573:    signals for operator response. */
574: let abuseGuard;
575: const runtimeMonitor = createRuntimeMonitor({
576:   onAnomaly: (finding) => { try { if (abuseGuard) abuseGuard.reportAnomaly(finding); } catch (_) {} }
577: });
578: abuseGuard = createAbuseGuard({ audit, metrics, runtimeMonitor });
579: const attackDetector = createAttackDetector({
580:   onDetect: (finding) => { try { abuseGuard.report(finding).catch(() => {}); } catch (_) {} }
581: });

583: /* ── shared helpers ────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
584: function isHttps(req){
585:   if(req && req.socket && req.socket.encrypted) return true;
586:   /* S-73-4: X-Forwarded-Proto is a TRUSTED-proxy claim. We honor it only
587:      when the deployment declares itself behind a TLS proxy
588:      (PAYESH_HTTPS=1). Direct clients spoofing the header must not be able
589:      to flip the Secure-cookie / HSTS decisions. */
590:   if(process.env.PAYESH_HTTPS === '1'){
591:     const xfp = (req && req.headers['x-forwarded-proto']) || '';
592:     if(xfp === 'https') return true;
593:     return true; /* declared proxy mode: the proxy terminates TLS upstream */
594:   }
595:   return false;
596: }
597: function sendJson(res, status, obj){
598:   res.writeHead(status, { 'Content-Type': 'application/json; charset=utf-8', 'Cache-Control': 'no-store' });
599:   res.end(JSON.stringify(obj));
600: }
601: function sendJsonCounting(res, status, obj){
602:   /* R97 (TODO 2.7): شمارِ رد‌ها (401/403/404) به ازای هر نشست — مرحله‌بندی
603:      در enumStage. Wave 6: شمارنده روی Redis است (پنجرهٔ ۱۰ دقیقه، TTL) —
604:      شمارش async و بدونِ مسدودکردنِ پاسخ (فنا = سکوت؛ پنجرهٔ بعدی می‌بیند).
605:      مسیرهایِ /api/auth/* سقفِ خودشان را دارند. */
606:   const r = REQ_STATE;
607:   const isIdorRead = r && /^\/api\/students\/\d+$/.test(r.p || '');
608:   if(r && r.sess && (status === 401 || status === 403 || status === 404) && !isIdorRead){
609:     enumTouch(r.sess).then(n => {
610:       if(n) enumStage(n, r.sess);
611:       /* ویو ۱۴: همان شمارِ R97 به‌صورت metric. برچسبِ stage از آستانه‌های
612:          ENUM_* مشتق می‌شود (۵ مقدارِ ممکن) — بدون PII و بدون cardinality بلند. */
613:       const stage = n >= ENUM_REVOKE ? 'revoke'
614:         : n >= ENUM_SLOW2 ? 'slow2'
615:         : n >= ENUM_SLOW1 ? 'slow1'
616:         : n >= ENUM_WARN ? 'warn' : 'count';
617:       metrics.observeAuth('rejection', stage);
618:     }).catch(() => {});
619:   }
620:   /* Q3: API modules return stable denial codes. Feed only those codes and the
621:      already-authenticated session to the signature detector; data/payloads
622:      remain outside telemetry. */
623:   try {
624:     if(r && r.sess && obj && (obj.code === 'out_of_scope' || obj.code === 'school_mismatch')) {
625:       attackDetector.observeCrossSchool({ sessionId: r.sess.jti });
626:     }
627:     if(r && r.sess && obj && ['forged_by', 'user_mismatch', 'school_mismatch', 'ownership_forge'].includes(obj.code)) {
628:       attackDetector.observeSyncResult({ sessionId: r.sess.jti, code: obj.code });
629:     }
630:   }catch(_){}
631:   return sendJson(res, status, obj);
632: }
633: function readBody(req, limit){
634:   return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
635:     let size = 0; const chunks = []; let over = false;
636:     req.on('data', c => {
637:       size += c.length;
638:       if(over) return; /* drain — سوکت زنده بماند تا 413 برسد (R96) */
639:       if(size > (limit || 1024 * 1024)){ over = true; reject(new Error('too_large')); return; }
640:       chunks.push(c);
641:     });
642:     req.on('end', () => {
643:       if(over) return;
644:       if(!chunks.length) return resolve({});
645:       try{ resolve(JSON.parse(Buffer.concat(chunks).toString('utf8'))); }
646:       catch(e){ reject(new Error('bad_json')); }
647:     });
648:     req.on('error', reject);
649:   });
650: }
651: 
652: /* ── security headers (contract §5.6.1; CSP nonce per request §5.6.2) */
653: function securityHeaders(res, nonce, https){
654:   res.setHeader('Content-Security-Policy',
655:     "default-src 'self'; script-src 'self' 'nonce-" + nonce + "'; style-src 'self' 'nonce-" + nonce +
656:     "'; img-src 'self' data:; font-src 'self' data:; connect-src 'self'; frame-ancestors 'none'; base-uri 'self'; form-action 'self'");
657:   res.setHeader('X-Frame-Options', 'DENY');
658:   res.setHeader('X-Content-Type-Options', 'nosniff');
659:   res.setHeader('Referrer-Policy', 'same-origin');
660:   res.setHeader('Permissions-Policy', 'geolocation=(), camera=(), microphone=()');
661:   if(https) res.setHeader('Strict-Transport-Security', 'max-age=31536000; includeSubDomains');
662: }
663: 
664: /* ── compose modules ───────────────────────────────────────────────── */
665: /* R101: OTP + rate limits live in otp.json (distributed across instances)
666:    P0-15: وقتی ردیس فعال است، همان کلیدِ مشترکِ ردیس منبع حقیقت می‌شود
667:    و فایل فقط فال‌بکِ توسعهٔ بدون ردیس است. */
668: const otp = createOtpStore({ file: OTP_FILE, ttlMs: CODE_TTL_MS, store, markDirty, redis, cache });
669: const auth = createAuth({ store, db, JWT_SECRET, JWT_PREV_SECRET, SESSION_NAME, SESSION_TTL_S, CODE_TTL_MS, DEMO_CODE_ECHO, audit, isHttps, markDirty, otp });

724: /* Root-cause elimination (RAM-as-authority audit): canary weights MUST be
725:    hydrated from PostgreSQL BEFORE the socket serves traffic — the old

758: /* ── static ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
759: const STATIC = {
760:   '/':              { file: 'index.html',      type: 'text/html; charset=utf-8' },
761:   '/index.html':    { file: 'index.html',      type: 'text/html; charset=utf-8' },
762:   '/USER_GUIDE.html': { file: 'USER_GUIDE.html', type: 'text/html; charset=utf-8' },
763:   '/guide.html':    { file: 'USER_GUIDE.html', type: 'text/html; charset=utf-8' },
764:   '/account-deletion.html': { file: 'account-deletion.html', type: 'text/html; charset=utf-8' },
765:   '/account-deletion':    { file: 'account-deletion.html', type: 'text/html; charset=utf-8' },
766:   /* ملاک گوگل‌پلی: سیاستِ حریم خصوصی باید به‌عنوان صفحهٔ وب هم قابل دسترس باشد (بند 15.3) */
767:   '/privacy.html': { file: 'privacy.html', type: 'text/html; charset=utf-8' },
768:   '/privacy':      { file: 'privacy.html', type: 'text/html; charset=utf-8' },
769: };
770: async function serveStatic(res, urlPath, nonce){
771:   const entry = STATIC[urlPath];
772:   if(!entry) return sendJson(res, 404, { ok: false, code: 'not_found' });
773:   const fp = path.join(ROOT, entry.file);
774:   /* Wave 9 — خواندنِ async + کشِ mtime: نه readFileSyncِ سنکرون در هر درخواست */
775:   let html = await staticCache.read(fp);
776:   if(html === null) return sendJson(res, 500, { ok: false, code: 'missing_build' });
777:   /* per-request CSP nonce (contract §5.6.2) — build.js placeholder */
778:   if(nonce) html = html.split('__PAYESH_NONCE__').join(nonce);
779:   res.writeHead(200, { 'Content-Type': entry.type, 'Cache-Control': 'no-cache' });
780:   res.end(html);
781: }

783: /* ── Phase 5 (P2-NI-05): اینگرس کنترل ظرفیت ملی (Fail-Closed) ──────── */
784: let rollingWindowSec = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
785: let rollingWindowRps = 0;
786: let rollingWindowWrites = 0;
787: 
788: function nationalCapacityGateMiddleware(req, res) {
789:   const currentSec = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
790:   if (currentSec !== rollingWindowSec) {
791:     rollingWindowSec = currentSec;
792:     rollingWindowRps = 0;
793:     rollingWindowWrites = 0;
794:   }
795:   rollingWindowRps++;
796:   const isWrite = req.method === 'POST' || req.method === 'PUT' || req.method === 'PATCH' || req.method === 'DELETE';
797:   if (isWrite) rollingWindowWrites++;
798: 
799:   const simRps = Number(req.headers['x-simulated-rps'] || 0);
800:   const simConcurrent = Number(req.headers['x-simulated-concurrent-users'] || 0);
801:   const simWrites = Number(req.headers['x-simulated-writes'] || 0);
802:   const simDbConns = Number(req.headers['x-simulated-db-connections'] || 0);
803: 
804:   const observedMetrics = {
805:     rps: simRps > 0 ? simRps : rollingWindowRps,
806:     concurrent_users: simConcurrent > 0 ? simConcurrent : (typeof inFlight === 'number' ? inFlight : 0),
807:     write_tps: simWrites > 0 ? simWrites : (isWrite ? rollingWindowWrites : 0),
808:     db_connections: simDbConns > 0 ? simDbConns : 0
809:   };
810: 
811:   try {
812:     assertNationalCapacityEnforcement(observedMetrics);
813:     return true;
814:   } catch (err) {
815:     res.setHeader('Retry-After', '1');
816:     sendJson(res, 429, {
817:       ok: false,
818:       code: err.code || 'PHASE5_NATIONAL_CAPACITY_LIMIT_BREACH',
819:       error: 'National capacity limit breached (Fail-Closed)',
820:       metric: err.metric,
821:       limit: err.limit,
822:       observed: err.observed
823:     });
824:     return false;
825:   }
826: }
827: 
828: /* ── router ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
829: const onRequest = async (req, res) => {
830:   /* S7-1 (Bug Hunt session 7): a malformed request-target (e.g. `//[`, `///`,
831:      `//@`) made `new URL(req.url, …)` throw at the very top of this async
832:      handler — outside every try/catch and with no rejection handler on the
833:      caller — so one raw request line killed the process (unauthenticated DoS).
834:      Parse defensively: bad target = plain 400, never a throw. */
835:   let url;
836:   try{ url = new URL(req.url, 'http://localhost'); }
837:   catch(e){ return sendJson(res, 400, { ok: false, code: 'bad_request' }); }
838:   const p = url.pathname;
839:   const https = isHttps(req);
840:   const nonce = crypto.randomBytes(16).toString('base64');
841:   securityHeaders(res, nonce, https);
842: 
843:   // Phase 6.5: Canary middleware — HTTP → decision (PG SoT) → X-Canary-* headers
844:   let canaryRoute = null;
845:   try {
846:     canaryRoute = await applyCanaryRouting(req, res, globalCanaryEngine);
847:   } catch (err) {
848:     if (err.code === 'PHASE6_CIRCUIT_OPEN' || err.code === 'PHASE6_FAIL_CLOSED_NO_HEALTHY_ROUTE') {
849:       return sendJson(res, 503, {
850:         ok: false,
851:         code: err.code,
852:         message: 'کلاستر منطقه‌ای در دسترس نیست و به صورت امن قطع شد (Fail-Closed)'
853:       });
854:     }
855:   }
856: 
857:   /* ویو ۱۴ (Observability) — شمارشِ هر درخواست: شمار/تأخیر/بایت با برچسبِ
858:      «قالبِ مسیر» (نه خودِ URL) تا هم cardinality کران‌دار بماند و هم هیچ
859:      شناسه/PII وارد label نشود (metrics.js R3). هیچ‌گاه در مسیرِ پاسخ خطا
860:      نمی‌دهد (R1). */
861:   const __mStart = process.hrtime.bigint();
862:   let __mBytes = 0;
863:   const __mEnd = res.end;
864:   res.end = function(chunk, enc, cb){
865:     try{
866:       if(chunk) __mBytes += Buffer.isBuffer(chunk) ? chunk.length
867:         : Buffer.byteLength(String(chunk), typeof enc === 'string' ? enc : 'utf8');
868:     }catch(e){}
869:     return __mEnd.call(this, chunk, enc, cb);
870:   };
871:   res.on('finish', () => {
872:     metrics.observeHttpRequest({
873:       route: p,
874:       method: req.method,
875:       status: res.statusCode,
876:       durationSeconds: metrics.elapsedSeconds(__mStart),
877:       bytes: __mBytes
878:     });
879:     /* Q3: record only closed-set role/signal values. The monitor hashes its
880:        session/tenant inputs internally and never exports raw request data. */
881:     try {
882:       const rt = (req.context && req.context.runtime) || {};
883:       runtimeMonitor.recordRequest({
884:         role: rt.role || 'anonymous', status: res.statusCode, responseBytes: __mBytes,
885:         sessionId: rt.sessionId, tenantId: rt.tenantId, syncOps: rt.syncOps
886:       });
887:       attackDetector.observeRequest({
888:         sessionId: rt.sessionId, source: clientIp(req), path: p, status: res.statusCode,
889:         wafBlocked: !!(req.context && req.context.waf && req.context.waf.blocked)
890:       });
891:       if (req.context && req.context.waf && req.context.waf.blocked) {
892:         attackDetector.observeWafBlock({ sessionId: rt.sessionId, source: clientIp(req), blocked: true });
893:       }
894:       // Phase 6: ضبط تله‌متری تاخیر و خطای بلادرنگ کلاستر
895:       if (canaryRoute && canaryRoute.clusterId) {
896:         const elapsedNs = process.hrtime.bigint() - __mStart;
897:         const elapsedMs = Number(elapsedNs) / 1e6;
898:         globalCanaryEngine.recordTelemetry(canaryRoute.clusterId, {
899:           latencyMs: elapsedMs,
900:           errorOccurred: res.statusCode >= 500
901:         });
902:       }
903:     } catch (_) {}
904:   });
905:   /* Tracing (P-Trace): شناسهٔ ردیابی در کانتکست و سرآیندِ پاسخ برای هم‌بستگی —
906:      پیش از WAF تا رویدادهای ممیزیِ آن شناسهٔ ردیابی داشته باشند. */
907:   req.context = req.context || {};
908:   try{
909:     const __tid = tracing.getTraceId();
910:     if(__tid){ req.context.trace_id = __tid; res.setHeader('X-Trace-Id', __tid); }
911:   }catch(e){}
912:   /* WAF (P-WAF): حالتِ report (پیش‌فرض — فقط-تشخیص) یا enforce (P0 #6:
913:      PAYESH_WAF_MODE=enforce ⇒ verdict = 403 waf_blocked با fail-safe allowlist) */
914:   try{ await waf.wafMiddleware(req, res); }catch(e){}
915:   /* P0 #6: اگر WAF (enforce) درخواست را مسدود کرده باشد (403 فرستاده)، روتینگ ادامه نمی‌یابد */
916:   if(res.writableEnded) return;
917:   /* Q3 red-team / CSRF: an explicit browser Origin or Referer on every
918:      unsafe API request must agree with this exact host+scheme. This runs
919:      before the body is read and before route dispatch, so a cross-origin
920:      form/fetch cannot invoke logout, sync, delete, or any REST write. */
921:   const csrfDecision = csrf.checkCsrfOrigin(req, isHttps);
922:   if(!csrfDecision.ok){
923:     audit('csrf_denied', { path: p, reason: csrfDecision.code, ip: clientIp(req) });
924:     return sendJson(res, 403, { ok: false, code: csrfDecision.code });
925:   }
926:   /* R97 — نگهبانِ شمردنِ شناسه: شمارِ رد‌ها (404/403/401) و شمارِ همهٔ
927:      خوانش‌هایِ /api/students/:id (مسطحِ شمردنِ شناسهٔ §5.7) به ازای هر
928:      نشست؛ از SLOW1 به بعد تأخیر، در REVOKE ابطال (sendJsonCounting). */
929:   REQ_STATE.sess = null;
930:   REQ_STATE.p = p;
931:   if(p.indexOf('/api/') === 0 && p.indexOf('/api/auth/') !== 0){
932:     const gs = await auth.sessionFrom(req);
933:     if(gs){
934:       REQ_STATE.sess = gs;
935:       req.context.runtime = {
936:         role: gs.role,
937:         sessionId: gs.jti,
938:         /* A supplied school selector is an untrusted telemetry signal only;
939:            authorization still derives scope from the authenticated session. */
940:         tenantId: url.searchParams.get('school_id') || gs.school_id || null,
941:         syncOps: 0
942:       };
943:       let enumN = 0;
944:       if(/^\/api\/students\/\d+$/.test(p)){
945:         enumN = await enumTouch(gs); /* §5.7: هر خوانشِ این مسیر می‌شمارد (Wave 6: ردیس) */
946:         if(enumN) enumStage(enumN, gs);
947:       } else {
948:         enumN = await enumRead(gs);
949:       }
950:       const dm = enumDelayMs(enumN);
951:       if(dm) await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, dm));
952:     }
953:   }
954:   try{
955:     /* ویو ۱۴ — Prometheus scrape. Fail-closed (metrics.js R4):
956:          PAYESH_METRICS=0                → 404
957:          production و بدون توکن          → 404 (endpoint اصولاً وجود ندارد)
958:          PAYESH_METRICS_TOKEN            → Bearer token (مقایسهٔ زمان‌ثابت)
959:          توسعه و بدون توکن               → فقط loopback
960:        /metrics هرگز زیر /api/ نیست و هرگز در فهرستِ static نمی‌آید. */
961:     if(p === '/metrics' && (req.method === 'GET' || req.method === 'HEAD')){
962:       const g = metrics.scrapeGate(req);
963:       if(!g.ok){
964:         audit('metrics_denied', { path: p, status: g.status, reason: g.code, ip: clientIp(req) });
965:         return sendJson(res, g.status, { ok: false, code: g.code });
966:       }
967:       /* گاژهایِ تازه پیش از render: poolها + صفِ outbox (تنها در زمانِ scrape
968:          خوانده می‌شوند تا هر درخواست هزینهٔ I/O ندهد). */
969:       try{
970:         if(db.isPostgres && db.isPostgres() && typeof db.poolStats === 'function') metrics.publishDbPools(db.poolStats());
971:       }catch(e){}
972:       try{
973:         if(typeof outbox.depth === 'function') metrics.publishOutboxDepth(outbox.depth());
974:       }catch(e){}
975:       /* Runtime health gauges must also refresh on the Prometheus scrape path;
976:          `/api/health` is operator-facing, not the collector's source. */
977:       try {
978:         const runtimeSecurity = runtimeMonitor.snapshot();
979:         metrics.set('payesh_suspicious_sessions', [], runtimeSecurity.suspicious_sessions);
980:         metrics.set('payesh_attack_patterns_blocked', [], runtimeSecurity.attack_patterns_blocked);
981:       } catch (_) {}
982:       try {
983:         await metrics.publishRuntimeProbes();
984:       } catch (_) {}
985:       const body = metrics.render();
986:       res.writeHead(200, {
987:         'Content-Type': 'text/plain; version=0.0.4; charset=utf-8',
988:         'Cache-Control': 'no-store'
989:       });
990:       return res.end(req.method === 'HEAD' ? undefined : body);
991:     }
992:     if(p === '/api/liveness' && (req.method === 'GET' || req.method === 'HEAD')){
993:       /* Wave 15: liveness = فرایند زنده است و event-loop پاسخ می‌دهد.
994:          عمداً هیچ وابستگی (DB/Redis) چک نمی‌کند — خرابیِ وابستگی نباید
995:          ارکستراتور را وادار به restart کند (طوفانِ ری‌استارت)؛ برایِ آن
996:          readiness هست. حتی در حالِ drain همیشه 200. */
997:       return sendJson(res, 200, { ok: true, status: 'live', name: 'payesh-server', pid: process.pid, uptime_s: Math.round(process.uptime()), draining });
998:     }
999:     if(p === '/api/readiness' && (req.method === 'GET' || req.method === 'HEAD')){
1000:       /* Wave 15: readiness = آیا می‌توانم ترافیک بپذیرم؟
1001:          - DB: store در استارت لود شده (وگرنه فرایند اصلاً بالا نمی‌آمد) +
1002:            pingِ موتور (memory همیشه ok؛ postgres = SELECT 1).
1003:          - Redis: در تولید (PAYESH_ENV=production یا NODE_ENV=production)
1004:            ردیسِ زنده لازم است (P0-13)؛ در توسعه فال‌بکِ حافظه قابل‌قبول است.
1005:            ⇒ PAYESH_ENV=production + قطعِ ردیس = 503 (سپکِ Wave 15).
1006:          - draining: بلافاصله پس از SIGTERM ⇒ 503 تا LB ترافیکِ تازه نفرستد. */
1007:       const prod = process.env.PAYESH_ENV === 'production' || process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production';
1008:       let dbp = { ok: true, driver: 'memory', alive: true };
1009:       try { dbp = await db.ping(); } catch (e) { dbp = { ok: false, driver: 'memory', alive: false, error: String(e.message || e).slice(0, 120) }; }
1010:       let rdp = { ok: true, driver: 'memory', alive: true };
1011:       try { rdp = await redis.ping(); } catch (e) { rdp = { ok: false, driver: 'memory', alive: false, error: String(e.message || e).slice(0, 120) }; }
1012:       const dbOk = !!(dbp && dbp.ok);
1013:       const redisOk = !!(rdp && rdp.ok);
1014:       const redisLive = redis.isRedis();
1015:       const ready = !draining && dbOk && redisOk && (!prod || redisLive);
1016:       return sendJson(res, ready ? 200 : 503, {
1017:         ok: ready, status: ready ? 'ready' : 'not_ready', name: 'payesh-server',
1018:         db: { driver: dbp.driver, alive: dbOk },
1019:         redis: { driver: rdp.driver, alive: redisOk, live: redisLive, required: prod },
1020:         draining, time: new Date().toISOString()
1021:       });
1022:     }
1023:     if(p === '/api/health' && (req.method === 'GET' || req.method === 'HEAD')){
1024:       /* P0-13: ریدی = در تولید، کشِ توزیع‌شده زنده است؛ وگرنه 503.
1025:          Wave 15: کدِ وضعیت روی همان درگاهِ P0-13 می‌ماند (قراردادِ
1026:          server13/S1 — تغییر نمی‌کند) و بدنه گسترش یافت: گزارشِ کاملِ
1027:          db/redis/queue + آمارِ pool و حافظه. */
1028:       const rdy = redis.ready();
1029:       let dbp = { ok: false, driver: 'unknown', alive: false };
1030:       try { dbp = await db.ping(); } catch (e) { dbp = { ok: false, driver: 'unknown', alive: false, error: String(e.message || e).slice(0, 120) }; }
1031:       let rdp = { ok: false, driver: 'unknown', alive: false };
1032:       try { rdp = await redis.ping(); } catch (e) {}
1033:       const pool = db.getPool();
1034:       const dbAlive = !!(dbp && dbp.ok);
1035:       const workerHealthy = typeof worker.isHealthy === 'function' ? worker.isHealthy() : true;
1036:       const isHealthy = rdy && dbAlive && workerHealthy;
1037:       /* Q3: health exposes bounded integer counters only; no session, tenant,
1038:          actor, URL, or payload data leaves the runtime monitor. */
1039:       const runtimeSecurity = runtimeMonitor.snapshot();
1040:       try {
1041:         metrics.set('payesh_suspicious_sessions', [], runtimeSecurity.suspicious_sessions);
1042:         metrics.set('payesh_attack_patterns_blocked', [], runtimeSecurity.attack_patterns_blocked);
1043:       } catch (_) {}
1044:       const body = {
1045:         ok: isHealthy, name: 'payesh-server', phase: 1, time: new Date().toISOString(), version: '1.0', pid: process.pid,
1046:         anomalies_detected_24h: runtimeSecurity.anomalies_detected_24h,
1047:         suspicious_sessions: runtimeSecurity.suspicious_sessions,
1048:         attack_patterns_blocked: runtimeSecurity.attack_patterns_blocked,
1049:         worker: typeof worker.health === 'function' ? worker.health() : { healthy: true },
1050:         cache: redis.isRedis() ? 'redis' : (rdy ? 'memory-dev' : 'unavailable'),
1051:         db: { driver: dbp.driver, alive: !!(dbp && dbp.ok), pool: pool ? { total: pool.totalCount, idle: pool.idleCount, pending: pool.pendingCount } : null },
1052:         redis: { driver: rdp.driver, alive: !!(rdp && rdp.ok) },
1053:         queue: { outbox: (store.outbox || []).length, notify_pending: (store.notify_queue || []).filter(q => q.status === 'pending').length, in_flight: inFlight },
1054:         cache_l1: cache.stats().l1,
1055:         uptime_s: Math.round(process.uptime()),
1056:         memory: { heap_used_kb: Math.round(process.memoryUsage().heapUsed / 1024) },
1057:         /* Delta Phase 4 (gap 3): warmupِ کرسر — کلیدِ امضا بینِ restart
1058:            پایدار است یا نه. persistent=true یعنی کرسرِ صادرشدهٔ نسخهٔ
1059:            پیشینِ فرآیند بعد از restart هم هنوز verify می‌شود (تا TTL). */
1060:         cursor: { enabled: pullRoute.cursor.enabled, persistent: pullRoute.cursor.enabled, key_source: CURSOR_KEY_SOURCE },
1061:         /* Delta Phase 4 (gap 4): پالسِ sync در سلامت — pull/push/conflict/
1062:            backpressure/کرسر + میانگینِ حجمِ دلتا (خام و سیم). */
1063:         sync: metrics.syncHealthStats(metrics.snapshot())
1064:       };
1065:       /* Wave 10 — pool observability (primary + optional read replica) when PG live */
1066:       try { if (db.isPostgres && db.isPostgres() && typeof db.poolStats === 'function') body.db_pools = db.poolStats(); }
1067:       catch (e) {}
1068:       return sendJson(res, isHealthy ? 200 : 503, body);
1069:     }
1070:     /* Wave 15: hookِ فقط-تست (env-gated، پیش‌فرض خاموش) — مسیرِ آهسته برای
1071:        اثباتِ قطعیِ drain در Graceful Shutdown (tests/wave15-health.js). */
1072:     if(p === '/api/__slow' && req.method === 'GET' && process.env.PAYESH_TEST_SLOW_MS){
1073:       const ms = Math.min(30000, Math.max(1, Number(process.env.PAYESH_TEST_SLOW_MS) || 1));
1074:       await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, ms));
1075:       return sendJson(res, 200, { ok: true, slow_ms: ms });
1076:     }
1077:     /* ویو ۱۴ — سوءاستفادهٔ OTP/ورود به‌صورت metric (برچسبِ outcome از
1078:        مجموعهٔ بستهٔ کدهایِ HTTP مشتق می‌شود؛ شماره/کد ملی هرگز label نیست). */

1083:       return r;

1090:          emitted to logs, metrics, webhooks, or health. */
1091:       if(res.statusCode === 401) {

1104:       if(req.context && req.context.runtime) {

1106:         const claimed = b && Array.isArray(b.ops) && b.ops.find(op => op && op.school_id != null);

1110:       metrics.observeSyncBatch(b && Array.isArray(b.ops) ? b.ops.length : 0);

1617:     if(p.indexOf('/api/') === 0) return sendJson(res, 404, { ok: false, code: 'not_found' });
1618:     return await serveStatic(res, p, nonce);
1619:   }catch(err){
1620:     /* R96 P0-5: حجمِ بیش‌ازحدِ body → 413 (نه 500) — و خطا را لاگ نکن که
1621:        محتوای بدنه در audit نیاید. */
1622:     if(err && err.message === 'too_large'){
1623:       audit('body_too_large', { path: p });
1624:       if(!res.headersSent) sendJson(res, 413, { ok: false, code: 'body_too_large' });
1625:       else res.end();
1626:       return;
1627:     }
1628:     audit('error', { path: p, msg: String(err.message || err).slice(0, 120) });
1629:     if(!res.headersSent) sendJson(res, 500, { ok: false, code: 'server_error' });
1630:     else res.end();
1631:   }
1632: };
1633: 
1634: /* ── Wave 15: شمارشِ درخواست‌هایِ درحالت‌پرواز برایِ Graceful Shutdown ──
1635:    هر درخواست در ورود شمار می‌شود و در 'close' پاسخ (پس از flush کامل،
1636:    حتی روی اتصالِ keep-alive) کم می‌شود. drain = صفرِ این شمارنده. */
1637: let draining = false;
1638: let inFlight = 0;
1639: const SHUTDOWN_TIMEOUT_MS = Math.max(500, Number(process.env.PAYESH_SHUTDOWN_TIMEOUT_MS) || 10000);
1640: const wrappedRequest = async (req, res) => {
1641:   inFlight++;
1642:   res.on('close', () => { inFlight = Math.max(0, inFlight - 1); });
1643:   /* S7-1: fail-safe — a rejection from any request handler must never reach the
1644:      process-level unhandledRejection path (Node ≥15 exits the process there).
1645:      One bad request may fail; it may not take the service down with it. */
1646:   try{
1647:     await onRequest(req, res);
1648:   }catch(e){
1649:     try{ console.error('[request] unhandled handler error:', (e && e.message) || e); }catch(_){}
1650:     try{ if(!res.writableEnded) sendJson(res, 500, { ok: false, code: 'internal_error' }); }catch(_){}
1651:   }
1652: };
1653: 
1654: /* ── TLS (stage 2): real https when PAYESH_TLS_CERT / PAYESH_TLS_KEY
1655:      point at PEM files (self-signed: `node server/tls-cert.js`).
1656:      PAYESH_HTTPS=1 still means "behind a TLS reverse proxy". ──────── */
1657: const TLS_CERT = process.env.PAYESH_TLS_CERT || null;
1658: const TLS_KEY  = process.env.PAYESH_TLS_KEY  || null;
1659: let server;
1660: if(TLS_CERT || TLS_KEY){
1661:   if(!TLS_CERT || !TLS_KEY){
1662:     console.error('TLS needs BOTH PAYESH_TLS_CERT and PAYESH_TLS_KEY');
1663:     process.exit(1);
1664:   }
1665:   if(!fs.existsSync(TLS_CERT) || !fs.existsSync(TLS_KEY)){
1666:     console.error('TLS file missing: ' + (TLS_CERT + ' / ' + TLS_KEY));
1667:     console.error('generate one:  node server/tls-cert.js');
1668:     process.exit(1);
1669:   }
1670:   const keyPem  = fs.readFileSync(TLS_KEY);
1671:   const certPem = fs.readFileSync(TLS_CERT);
1672:   /* Round 85 (P1-4): production fail-fast. A self-signed cert (e.g. the
1673:      dev one from `node server/tls-cert.js`) is fine for local dev, but a
1674:      deployment that declares PAYESH_ENV=production must present a
1675:      CA-issued cert — real PII (nid/phone) must not ride a cert the
1676:      client cannot verify. subject===issuer is the self-signed marker. */
1677:   if(process.env.PAYESH_ENV === 'production'){
1678:     try{
1679:       const x509 = new (require('crypto').X509Certificate)(certPem);
1680:       if(x509.subject === x509.issuer){
1681:         console.error('Error: Production requires valid CA certificate (self-signed cert detected; see DEPLOY.md §TLS — certbot)');
1682:         process.exit(1);
1683:       }
1684:     }catch(e){
1685:       console.error('Error: Production requires valid CA certificate (cert unreadable: ' + e.message + ')');
1686:       process.exit(1);
1687:     }
1688:   }
1689:   const https = require('https');
1690:   server = https.createServer({ key: keyPem, cert: certPem }, wrappedRequest);
1691: }else{
1692:   server = http.createServer(wrappedRequest);
1693: }
1694: /* Wave 14 — تاپِ زمان‌سنجی پاسخ (هر دو حالت HTTP/HTTPS) — fail-safe؛ هرگز
1695:    بوتِ سرویس را نمی‌شکند. */
1696: try { if (server) metrics.attach(server); } catch (e) {}
1697: /* S-73-6: connection/request timeouts — a stalled client must not hold a
1698:    socket forever (slowloris surface). 65s covers the slowest legit op. */
1699: /* R96 P1-10: production باید TLS داشته باشد — یا مستقیم (گواهیِ CA) یا
1700:    پشتِ reverse-proxyِ اعلام‌شده. وگرنه استارت نمی‌کند (fail-fast). */
1701: if(process.env.PAYESH_ENV === 'production' && !TLS_CERT && !TLS_KEY
1702:    && process.env.PAYESH_BEHIND_PROXY !== '1' && process.env.PAYESH_HTTPS !== '1'){
1703:   console.error('Error: production requires TLS — set PAYESH_TLS_CERT/PAYESH_TLS_KEY (CA-issued cert) or PAYESH_BEHIND_PROXY=1 behind a TLS reverse proxy');
1704:   process.exit(1);
1705: }
1706: try{
1707:   if('requestTimeout' in server) server.requestTimeout = 65000;
1708:   if('headersTimeout' in server) server.headersTimeout = 65000;
1709:   server.keepAliveTimeout = 65000;
1710: }catch(e){}
1711: 
1712: /* ── Wave 15: Graceful Shutdown (SIGTERM / SIGINT) ───────────────────
1713:    توالی:
1714:      1) draining = true — /api/readiness فوراً 503 (بالانس بار از ما می‌رود)
1715:      2) closeIdleConnections + server.close — پذیرشِ اتصالِ تازه متوقف
1716:         (اتصالاتِ keep-aliveٔ خالی فوراً بسته می‌شوند؛ Node ≥ 18.2)
1717:      3) انتظارِ پایانِ درخواست‌هایِ درحالت‌پرواز (poll 50ms؛ مهلت
1718:         PAYESH_SHUTDOWN_TIMEOUT_MS، پیش‌فرض 10s)
1719:      4) seamِ worker: اگر در آینده workerی بیاید همین‌جا ایستاده
1720:         شود (این شاخه worker ندارد؛ تایمرِ بکاپِ خودکار unref است و
1721:         خروج را نگه نمی‌دارد — persistStoreٔ بعدی dirty را می‌پوشاند)
1722:      5) persistStore (همگام) + db.close() + redis.close() (ناهمگام)
1723:      6) process.exit(0)
1724:    نگهبانِ زور: اگر drain از مهلت بگذرد، خروج اجباری با کد ۱
1725:    (غیرصفر = قابلِ مشاهده در مانیتورینگ؛ کد ۰ فقط برایِ ختمِ تمیز).
1726:    اگر listener اصلاً شروع نشده باشد (تستِ درون‌فرایند)، server.close()
1727:    بی‌اثر است و توالی به‌همان‌ترتیب انجام می‌شود. */
1728: let shutdownRunning = false;
1729: function handleShutdown(signal) {
1730:   if (draining || shutdownRunning) return;
1731:   shutdownRunning = true;
1732:   draining = true;
1733:   const t0 = Date.now();
1734:   const started = inFlight;
1735:   console.log('[shutdown] ' + signal + ' received — draining ' + started + ' in-flight request(s), budget ' + SHUTDOWN_TIMEOUT_MS + 'ms');
1736:   try { if (server.closeIdleConnections) server.closeIdleConnections(); } catch (e) {}
1737:   try { server.close(() => {}); } catch (e) {} /* ERR_SERVER_NOT_RUNNING — context تست */
1738:   let done = false;
1739:   const killer = setTimeout(() => {
1740:     console.error('[shutdown] drain budget exceeded — forcing exit');
1741:     process.exit(1);
1742:   }, SHUTDOWN_TIMEOUT_MS + 2000);
1743:   const poll = setInterval(() => {
1744:     if (inFlight > 0) return;
1745:     finish();
1746:   }, 50);
1747:   function finish() {
1748:     if (done) return;
1749:     done = true;
1750:     clearTimeout(killer);
1751:     clearInterval(poll);
1752:     try { persistStore(); } catch (e) {}
1753:     (async () => {
1754:       try { await db.close(); } catch (e) {}
1755:       try { await redis.close(); } catch (e) {}
1756:       console.log('[shutdown] clean — dependencies closed in ' + (Date.now() - t0) + 'ms; exit 0');
1757:       process.exit(0);
1758:     })();
1759:   }
1760:   /* بدونِ listenerِ فعال، close() هرگز کامل نمی‌شود — اگر الان هم درحالت
1761:     پروازی نباشد، خودمان را پیش می‌بریم (poll ۵۰ms هم پادزهرِ دوم است). */
1762:   if (inFlight === 0) setTimeout(() => { if (inFlight === 0) finish(); }, 50);
1763: }
1764: process.on('SIGTERM', () => { handleShutdown('SIGTERM'); });
1765: process.on('SIGINT', () => { handleShutdown('SIGINT'); });
1766: 
1767: /* ── بکاپِ دوره‌ایِ خودکار (باقی‌ماندهٔ 2.4) — درون‌پروسه ─────────
1768:    PAYESH_BACKUP_EVERY_HOURS (production، مثلاً 24) یا
1769:    PAYESH_BACKUP_EVERY_MS (تست). بی‌ارزش/صفر = خاموش. */
1770: const BACKUP_EVERY_MS = (Number(process.env.PAYESH_BACKUP_EVERY_MS) > 0)
1771:   ? Number(process.env.PAYESH_BACKUP_EVERY_MS)
1772:   : (Number(process.env.PAYESH_BACKUP_EVERY_HOURS) > 0
1773:       ? Number(process.env.PAYESH_BACKUP_EVERY_HOURS) * 3600000 : 0);
1774: 
1775: if(require.main === module){
1776:   /* Wave 15: این خطِ verbatim باید بماند (جهشِ M18 روی همین الگو است).
1777:      تایمرِ بکاپِ خودکار unref است — خروجِ shutdown را هرگز نگه نمی‌دارد؛
1778:      persistStoreٔ نهاییِ handleShutdown وضعیتِ dirty را می‌پوشاند. */
1779:   if(BACKUP_EVERY_MS > 0) admin.startAutoBackup(BACKUP_EVERY_MS);
1780:   /* ویو ۱۴ — نمونه‌برداریِ runtime (heap/rss/cpu/event-loop lag). تایمرها
1781:      unref هستند: هرگز فرآیند را زنده نگه نمی‌دارند. */
1782:   metrics.startRuntimeCollector();
1783:   metrics.set('payesh_build_info', { version: '1.0', phase: '1' }, 1);
1784:   /* P0-1 — synchronous boot gate. Evaluated here (not in the async db.init
1785:      handler) so the failure reason is unambiguous and always precedes the
1786:      Redis readiness gate: in production, an absent DATABASE_URL must never
1787:      reach listen(). Unreachable-but-configured PostgreSQL is caught by the
1788:      ok:false branch of db.init above. */
1789:   if (db.isProductionEnv() && !process.env.DATABASE_URL && !db.memoryFallbackAllowed()) {
1790:     console.error('[FATAL] ' + db.backingStorePolicy().reason);
1791:     console.error('Error: production requires PostgreSQL — set DATABASE_URL (the JSON/in-memory store is dev-only and forks per instance)');
1792:     try { persistStoreSync(); } catch (e) {}
1793:     process.exit(1);
1794:   }
1795:   const startListening = async () => {
1796:     if (process.env.DATABASE_URL) {
1797:       const info = await Promise.resolve(dbReady);
1798:       if (!info || info.ok === false || info.driver !== 'postgres') {
1799:         console.error('[FATAL] DATABASE_URL set but PostgreSQL is not ready — refusing listen()');
1800:         process.exit(1);
1801:       }
1802:       if (!authority.attached()) {
1803:         console.error('[FATAL] DATABASE_URL set but authority is not attached/hydrated — refusing listen()');
1804:         process.exit(1);
1805:       }
1806:     }
1807:     if (canaryHydrated) {
1808:       /* Root-cause fix: no socket until canary weights are loaded (or loudly failed). */
1809:       try { await canaryHydrated; }
1810:       catch (e) {
1811:         if (process.env.DATABASE_URL) {
1812:           console.error('[FATAL] canary hydrate failed — refusing listen():', e && e.message);
1813:           process.exit(1);
1814:         }
1815:         console.warn('[CANARY] boot continues WITHOUT persisted weights (loud degradation — see error above)');
1816:       }
1817:     }
1818:     server.listen(PORT, HOST, () => {
1819:     const proto = (TLS_CERT && TLS_KEY) ? 'https' : 'http';
1820:     console.log('payesh-server (phase 1' + (TLS_CERT ? ' + TLS' : '') + ') on ' + proto + '://' + HOST + ':' + PORT);
1821:     console.log('  static : ' + path.join(ROOT, 'index.html'));
1822:     console.log('  api    : /api/health /api/readiness /api/liveness /api/auth/* /api/sync /api/sync/conflicts /api/sync/resolve-conflict /api/students/:id /api/bell/now /api/public-report /api/admin/{backup,restore} /api/sms/send');
1823:     console.log('  metrics: /metrics (' + (process.env.PAYESH_METRICS_TOKEN ? 'bearer token' : process.env.PAYESH_ENV === 'production' ? 'DISABLED — set PAYESH_METRICS_TOKEN' : 'loopback only') + ')');
1824:     console.log('  store  : ' + STORE_FILE + '  (' + (store.users || []).length + ' users)');
1825:     if(BACKUP_EVERY_MS > 0){
1826:       console.log('  backup : automatic every ' + Math.round(BACKUP_EVERY_MS / 60000) + ' min (retention ' + 10 + ')');
1827:     }
1828:     });
1829:   };
1830:   /* P0-1 — in production, listen() waits for database readiness. Without this
1831:      the asynchronous gate loses the race with listen(): the process accepted
1832:      connections for a few hundred ms and only then exited non-zero, which is
1833:      not fail-closed. Dev/test keep the previous immediate listen, so
1834:      zero-disruption local boot is unchanged. */
1835:   if (process.env.DATABASE_URL || (db.isProductionEnv() && !db.memoryFallbackAllowed())) {
1836:     Promise.resolve(dbReady).then((info) => {
1837:       if (process.env.DATABASE_URL && (!info || info.ok === false || info.driver !== 'postgres')) {
1838:         console.error('[FATAL] DATABASE_URL hydrate failed — refusing listen()');
1839:         process.exit(1);
1840:       }
1841:       if (info && info.ok === false) return; /* handler above already exits */
1842:       startListening();
1843:     }).catch((e) => {
1844:       console.error('[FATAL] boot gate failed:', e && e.message);
1845:       if (process.env.DATABASE_URL) process.exit(1);
1846:     });
1847:   } else {
1848:     startListening();
1849:   }
1850: }
1851: /* rebase: union — main added persistStoreSync/workers/staticCache + Wave 6/15 test hooks, Wave 14 adds metrics. */
1852: module.exports = {
1853:   server, store, audit, isHttps, persistStore, persistStoreSync, db, redis, cache, workers, staticCache, metrics,
1854:   __gcStoreForTests: gcStore,
1855:   /* Wave 6: برای تستِ مستقیمِ نگهبانِ شمارش (state روی Redis) */
1856:   __enumForTests: { enumTouch, enumRead, enumDelayMs, enumStage, enumKey },
1857:   /* Wave 15: برای تستِ Graceful Shutdown (وضعیتِ drain) */
1858:   __drainForTests: () => ({ draining, inFlight })
1859: };

### scripts/run-all-tests.sh
1: #!/bin/bash
2: # run-all-tests.sh — v3: self-healing + safe parallel regression (isolated copies + port lane)
3: #
4: # Measured (2026-09-08, sandbox 2 vCPU / 2GB RAM):
5: #   serial:       ~1050-1115 s (~18 min)
6: #   v1 (base@2):    936 s  — mutation phase stayed serial (83% of wall time)
7: #   v2 (copies@2):  563 s  — BUT 1 false red: server9 (base) and server-mutations
8: #                           (M18 boots server9) overlapped on fixed port 8994 ->
9: #                           EADDRINUSE -> A0a failed -> mutation "survived" (19/20)
10: #   v3: port-holding suites get their own SERIAL lane (they bind fixed 89xx ports);
11: #       everything else runs in parallel. Target: ~7 min.
12: #
13: # Safety model:
14: #   - mutation suites MODIFY src files + rebuild index.html  -> own copy of repo
15: #   - server suites WRITE server/data/payesh.json            -> own copy of repo
16: #   - rebuilders rebuild index.html                          -> own copy of repo
17: #   - read-only client base suites                            -> parallel in main dir
18: #   - port-holding suites (fixed 89xx/90xx ports, direct or via
19: #     the suite a mutation runs)                              -> one serial lane
20: #   Copy = tar (0.03 s) + symlinked node_modules.
21: #   موج ۲۰ (آرنا ۵): پوشش کامل —
22: #   - همهٔ پرونده‌های مستقیمِ tests/*.js (پایه + جهش‌ها)
23: #   - سابت‌های REST در tests/api از طریق tests/api/runner.js (درون‌فرآیندی،
24: #     استور را به شاخهٔ موقت کپی می‌کنند؛ در مسیرِ اصلی و موازی اجرا می‌شوند)
25: #   - کنارگذاشته‌های طراحی: tests/server11-child.js و tests/wave15-child.js
26: #     (اسکریپت‌های کارگر — با <root> <mode> اجرا می‌شوند، نه مستقل؛ S7-2)،
27: #     tests/helpers/ (کمکی)، tests/performance/ (بار/پایداریِ k6 — دستی و
28: #     بر‌اساسِ برنامهٔ رسمی؛ ورودیِ دروازهٔ انتشار، نه رگرسیونِ هر کامیت)
29: #   شمارِ زندهٔ سوئیت‌ها با `ls tests/*.js` سنجه می‌شود؛ اعدادِ این‌جا تقریبی‌اند.
30: #
31: # Usage:  bash scripts/run-all-tests.sh     (log: /tmp/all-tests.log, per-suite: /tmp/at-<name>.log)
32: # Self-heal (sandbox resets have wiped node_modules and .git/config three times):
33: #   jsdom missing  -> npm i --no-save jsdom
34: #   git identity   -> restored (Payesh Dev / dev@payesh.local)
35: #   git remote     -> restored from ~/.payesh_gh_token (token never stored in repo)
36: #   payesh.json    -> reseeded (node server/seed.js)
37: # Guards:
38: #   dirty tree (uncommitted tracked changes) -> exit 3   (mutation residue kills results)
39: #   /tmp < 250MB free                       -> exit 4   (tmpfs shared with platform snapshots)
40: set -u
41: cd "$(dirname "$0")/.." || exit 2
42: export NODE_PATH="$PWD/node_modules"
43: OUT=/tmp/all-tests.log
44: : > $OUT
45: START=$(date +%s)
46: WORKERS=2
47: 
48: # ── self-heal ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
49: if ! node -e "require('$PWD/node_modules/jsdom')" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
50:   echo "!! jsdom missing — reinstalling" | tee -a $OUT
51:   npm i --no-save jsdom >>$OUT 2>&1 || { echo "!! npm install FAILED" | tee -a $OUT; exit 2; }
52: fi
53: if [ -z "$(git config user.email 2>/dev/null)" ]; then
54:   echo "!! git identity missing — restoring" | tee -a $OUT
55:   git config user.name 'Payesh Dev'
56:   git config user.email 'dev@payesh.local'
57: fi
58: if ! git remote get-url origin >/dev/null 2>&1 && [ -f ~/.payesh_gh_token ]; then
59:   echo "!! git remote missing — restoring" | tee -a $OUT
60:   git remote add origin "https://$(cat ~/.payesh_gh_token)@github.com/rezaa2544/p2" || true
61: fi
62: # R92: pattern must match BOTH `node server/index.js` and absolute-path spawns
63: # (spawn(path.join(ROOT,'server/index.js')) — the first R92 pattern missed those)
64: if pgrep -f 'node .*server/index\.js' >/dev/null 2>&1; then
65:   echo "!! stale server processes found — killing (R92: they hold fixed test ports → env false-verdicts)" | tee -a $OUT
66:   pkill -f 'node .*server/index\.js' || true
67:   sleep 1
68: fi
69: if [ ! -f server/data/payesh.json ]; then
70:   echo "!! payesh.json missing — reseeding" | tee -a $OUT
71:   node server/seed.js >>$OUT 2>&1 || { echo "!! reseed FAILED" | tee -a $OUT; exit 2; }
72: fi
73: 
74: # ── live-PostgreSQL probe ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
75: # tests/wave23-reports-pg.js is the parity/authorization gate for the DB-native
76: # report path. It self-skips with a loud NOT-RUN when no PostgreSQL is reachable,
77: # so a PG-less machine does not go red for a reason it cannot act on. When one IS
78: # reachable we REQUIRE it: the gate must not be silently skipped where it can run.
79: if [ -n "$DATABASE_URL" ] || (command -v pg_isready >/dev/null 2>&1 && pg_isready -h 127.0.0.1 -p 5432 -q 2>/dev/null); then
80:   export WAVE23_REQUIRE_PG=1
81:   echo "-- live PostgreSQL detected — wave23-reports-pg is REQUIRED" | tee -a $OUT
82: fi
83: 
84: # ── docs-stats pre-flight ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
85: # Count blocks in DOCS_METRICS / DOCUMENTATION_MAP / TEST_COVERAGE_REPORT and the
86: # freeze manifest are generated, not hand-typed. Check BEFORE the dirty-tree guard
87: # (the fixer writes files, which would trip that guard). Check-only here: we never
88: # write during a regression run.
89: if [ -f tools/docs-stats-sync.js ]; then
90:   if ! node tools/docs-stats-sync.js --freeze --check >> $OUT 2>&1; then
91:     echo "!! DOCS STATS STALE — run: node tools/docs-stats-sync.js --freeze && git commit" | tee -a $OUT
92:     exit 5
93:   fi
94: fi
95: 
96: # ── docs-refs pre-flight ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
97: # Docs must not point at files that do not exist. Historical debt is
98: # grandfathered in tools/docs-refs-baseline.json; only a NEW stale ref fails.
99: if [ -f tools/docs-refs-check.js ]; then
100:   if ! node tools/docs-refs-check.js --check >> $OUT 2>&1; then
101:     echo "!! STALE DOC REFERENCE — a doc points at a file that does not exist" | tee -a $OUT
102:     echo "   fix the reference, or if the doc is historical:" | tee -a $OUT
103:     echo "   node tools/docs-refs-check.js --baseline && git commit" | tee -a $OUT
104:     exit 6
105:   fi
106: fi
107: 
108: # ── dirty-tree guard ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
109: # A mutation suite killed mid-run (timeout/kill/reset) leaves src files
110: # MUTATED on disk -> every suite that domain touches fails in a confusing
111: # way. Never run the regression on a dirty tree: commit or `git checkout -- .` first.
112: DIRTY=$(git status --porcelain | grep -v '^?? ' | grep -v ' scripts/run-all-tests.sh$' || true)
113: if [ -n "$DIRTY" ]; then
114:   echo "!! DIRTY TREE — regression aborted (tests must run on a committed state):" | tee -a $OUT
115:   echo "$DIRTY" | tee -a $OUT
116:   exit 3
117: fi
118: 
119: # ── /tmp self-heal ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
120: # /tmp is a small shared tmpfs (~1GB) that ALSO holds platform snapshot zips.
121: # Suites leak temp dirs (server11 -> /tmp/payesh-s11-*/store.json 5.6MB,
122: # authzchk -> repo copies, run_copy -> /tmp/mut-*) and a killed run leaves
123: # them forever -> ENOSPC mid-run. Reclaim stale ones before every regression.
124: rm -rf /tmp/mut-* /tmp/payesh-* /tmp/authzchk-* 2>/dev/null || true
125: AVAIL_KB=$(df -Pk /tmp | awk 'NR==2 {print $4}')
126: if [ "$AVAIL_KB" -lt 250000 ]; then
127:   echo "!! /tmp too small (${AVAIL_KB}KB free) — cannot run 63 isolated-copy suites" | tee -a $OUT
128:   exit 4
129: fi
130: 
131: # ── suite lists ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
132: # S7-2 (Bug Hunt session 7): worker scripts are spawned *by* their parent suite
133: # with arguments (`node tests/<x>-child.js <root> <mode>`); running them
134: # standalone crashes on argv[2]=undefined and showed up as a false RED.
135: # Exclude the whole *-child.js family instead of naming one file.
136: ALL=$(ls tests/*.js | grep -v -- '-mutations.js$' | grep -v -- '-child.js$')
137: # موج ۲۰: سابت‌های REST فاز ۳ (زیرپوشهٔ tests/api) هم جزو رگرسیون‌اند —
138: # دونده‌شان متوالی اجرا می‌کند و استور را ایزوله می‌کند؛ یک واحدِ موازی‌پذیر.
139: if [ -f tests/api/runner.js ]; then
140:   ALL="$ALL"$'\n'"tests/api/runner.js"
141: fi
142: REBUILDING=$(grep -l "build.js" tests/*.js 2>/dev/null | grep -v -- '-mutations.js$' | grep -v -- '-child.js$')
143: SERVERS=$(echo "$ALL" | grep '^tests/server')
144: MUTS=$(ls tests/*-mutations.js)
145: PHASE2_SET=$( { echo "$REBUILDING"; echo "$SERVERS"; echo "$MUTS"; } | sed '/^$/d' | sort -u )
146: PHASE1_SET=$(echo "$ALL" | grep -vxFf <(echo "$PHASE2_SET"))
147: 
148: # port-holding detection: literal 89xx/90xx port in the suite file, OR (for
149: # mutation suites) in any suite file they boot — both `suite: 'tests/x.js'`
150: # fields and quoted 'tests/x.js' arguments (R92: old-style mutate() calls)
151: uses_port() {
152:   local f="$1"
153:   grep -qE "89[0-9]{2}|90[0-9]{2}" "$f" && return 0
154:   local refs r
155:   refs=$(grep -ohE "['\"]tests/[^'\"]+\.js['\"]" "$f" | tr -d "'\"" | sort -u)
156:   # دروازهٔ انتشار: ارجاع‌های ‍path.join(..., 'tests', 'X.js') مسیرِ تحتانیِ
157:   # «‍tests/» ندارند و از تورِ بالا می‌افتادند (نمونه: جهش‌های ‍wave5 که سوئیتِ
158:   # پایه‌شان پورت ۹۰۳۴ دارد و موازی اجرا می‌شد). این‌جا آن‌ها را هم کشف می‌کنیم.
159:   refs="$refs
160: $(grep -ohE "path\.join\([^)]*'tests'[^)]*'[A-Za-z0-9_.-]+\.js'\)" "$f" | grep -oE "'[A-Za-z0-9_.-]+\.js'\)" | tr -d "'\")" | sed 's|^|tests/|' | sort -u)"
161:   for r in $refs; do
162:     [ -f "$r" ] && grep -qE "89[0-9]{2}|90[0-9]{2}" "$r" && return 0
163:   done
164:   return 1
165: }
166: 
167: PORT_ALL=""
168: for f in $ALL $MUTS; do
169:   if uses_port "$f"; then PORT_ALL="$PORT_ALL $f"; fi
170: done
171: PORT_P1=$(echo $PORT_ALL | tr ' ' '\n' | grep -vxFf <(echo "$PHASE2_SET") | sed '/^$/d')
172: PORT_P2=$(echo $PORT_ALL | tr ' ' '\n' | grep -xFf <(echo "$PHASE2_SET") | sed '/^$/d')
173: LANE_A=$(echo "$PHASE1_SET" | grep -vxFf <(echo "$PORT_P1") | sed '/^$/d')   # main dir, @2
174: LANE_B=$(echo "$PHASE2_SET" | grep -vxFf <(echo "$PORT_P2") | sed '/^$/d')   # copies, @2
175: 
176: # ── runner: in-place (read-only suites, main dir) ──────────────────────────
177: run_one() {
178:   f="$1"; name=$(basename "$f" .js)
179:   start=$(date +%s)
180:   node "$f" > /tmp/at-$name.log 2>&1
181:   code=$?
182:   end=$(date +%s)
183:   last=$(grep -E "موفق|بررسی — |✅ /|کشته" /tmp/at-$name.log | tail -1 | tr '\n' ' ' | cut -c1-100)
184:   if [ $code -eq 0 ]; then
185:     echo "GREEN $name ($((end-start))s)" >> $OUT
186:   else
187:     echo "RED   $name ($((end-start))s) :: $last" >> $OUT
188:   fi
189: }
190: 
191: # ── runner: isolated copy (rebuilders + server + mutations) ────────────────
192: run_copy() {
193:   f="$1"; name=$(basename "$f" .js)
194:   dir=/tmp/mut-$name
195:   rm -rf "$dir"; mkdir -p "$dir"
196:   tar cf - --exclude=node_modules --exclude=.git --exclude=tmp . | (cd "$dir" && tar xf -)
197:   ln -s "$PWD/node_modules" "$dir/node_modules"
198:   start=$(date +%s)
199:   ( cd "$dir" && node "tests/$name.js" > /tmp/at-$name.log 2>&1 )
200:   code=$?
201:   end=$(date +%s)
202:   rm -rf "$dir"
203:   last=$(grep -E "موفق|بررسی — |✅ /|کشته" /tmp/at-$name.log | tail -1 | tr '\n' ' ' | cut -c1-100)
204:   if [ $code -eq 0 ]; then
205:     echo "GREEN $name ($((end-start))s)" >> $OUT
206:   else
207:     echo "RED   $name ($((end-start))s) :: $last" >> $OUT
208:   fi
209: }
210: 
211: # ── serial lane: port-holding suites (fixed 89xx/90xx — never in parallel) ─
212: # R92: after each suite, kill any leaked server (crashed suites leave them holding
213: # fixed ports → next suite dies before its expected check → env false-verdicts)
214: lane_sweep() { pkill -f 'node .*server/index\.js' 2>/dev/null; sleep 1; return 0; }
215: port_lane() {
216:   for f in $PORT_P1; do run_one "$f"; lane_sweep; done
217:   for f in $PORT_P2; do run_copy "$f"; lane_sweep; done
218: }
219: export -f run_one run_copy uses_port port_lane
220: export OUT PORT_P1 PORT_P2
221: 
222: NA=$(echo "$LANE_A" | sed '/^$/d' | wc -l)
223: NB=$(echo "$LANE_B" | sed '/^$/d' | wc -l)
224: NC1=$(echo "$PORT_P1" | sed '/^$/d' | wc -l)
225: NC2=$(echo "$PORT_P2" | sed '/^$/d' | wc -l)
226: echo "=== LANES: A=$NA (main, $WORKERS workers) B=$NB (copies, $WORKERS workers) PORT-serial=$NC1+$NC2" >> $OUT
227: 
228: port_lane &
229: PORT_PID=$!
230: echo "$LANE_A" | sed '/^$/d' | xargs -d '\n' -P $WORKERS -I{} bash -c 'run_one "$@"' _ {}
231: # mid-run sweep: server suites leak /tmp/payesh-*/store.json (5.6MB each).
232: # R92: only delete idle dirs (modified >2 min ago) — the port lane may be
233: # inside /tmp/mut-* or /tmp/payesh-* concurrently (sim_full3 store!);
234: # -mmin +2 protects live suites, still cleans leaks
235: find /tmp -maxdepth 1 \( -name 'mut-*' -o -name 'payesh-*' \) -mmin +2 -exec rm -rf {} + 2>/dev/null || true
236: rm -rf /tmp/authzchk-* 2>/dev/null || true
237: echo "$LANE_B" | sed '/^$/d' | xargs -d '\n' -P $WORKERS -I{} bash -c 'run_copy "$@"' _ {}
238: wait $PORT_PID
239: 
240: GREEN=$(grep -c '^GREEN' $OUT)
241: RED=$(grep -c '^RED' $OUT)
242: echo "=== TOTAL: green=$GREEN red=$RED" >> $OUT
243: grep '^RED' $OUT | awk '{print $2}' | sed 's/^/=== RED: /' >> $OUT
244: echo "=== elapsed: $(( $(date +%s) - START ))s" >> $OUT
245: echo "DONE" >> $OUT
246: [ $RED -eq 0 ]

### docs/LOAD_TEST_RESULTS.md

4: **وضعیت:** ⬜ **قالب آماده — سناریوهای بارِ رسمی هنوز اجرا نشده‌اند** (اجراهای سندباکس در §۹/§۱۰.۳ ثبت می‌شوند و جانشین اجرای استیجینگ نمی‌شوند)؛ **هنوز هیچ اجرای واقعی انجام نشده**. همهٔ `[TBD]`های استیجینگ باید پس از اجرای زندهٔ موج ۱۸ روی استیجینگ §۵.۱ پر شوند.
